<div class="api-field"><div class="api-field__left"><code class="api-field__name">metadataKey</code><span class="api-field__badge api-field__badge--required">required</span><div class="api-field__type"><div>string</div></div></div><div class="api-field__right"><div class="api-field__description">MetadataKey is the key of the metadata to store this cost of the request.</div></div></div>
<div class="api-field"><div class="api-field__left"><code class="api-field__name">type</code><span class="api-field__badge api-field__badge--required">required</span><div class="api-field__type"><div><a href="#github-com-envoyproxy-ai-gateway-api-v1alpha1-llmrequestcosttype">LLMRequestCostType</a></div></div></div><div class="api-field__right"><div class="api-field__description">Type specifies the type of the request cost. The default is <code class="thin">OutputToken</code>,<br>and it uses <code class="thin">output token</code> as the cost. The other types are <code class="thin">InputToken</code>, <code class="thin">TotalToken</code>,<br><code class="thin">CachedInputToken</code>, <code class="thin">CacheCreationInputToken</code>, <code class="thin">ReasoningToken</code>, and <code class="thin">CEL</code>.</div></div></div>
<div class="api-field"><div class="api-field__left"><code class="api-field__name">cel</code><span class="api-field__badge api-field__badge--optional">optional</span><div class="api-field__type"><div>string</div></div></div><div class="api-field__right"><div class="api-field__description">CEL is the CEL expression to calculate the cost of the request.<br>The CEL expression must return a signed or unsigned integer. If the<br>return value is negative, it will be error.<br>The expression can use the following variables:<br>	* model: the model name extracted from the request content. Type: string.<br>	* backend: the backend name in the form of <code class="thin">name.namespace</code>. Type: string.<br>	* input_tokens: the number of input tokens. Type: unsigned integer.<br>	* cached_input_tokens: the number of cached read input tokens. Type: unsigned integer.<br>	* cache_creation_input_tokens: the number of cache creation input tokens. Type: unsigned integer.<br>	* output_tokens: the number of output tokens. Type: unsigned integer.<br>	* total_tokens: the total number of tokens. Type: unsigned integer.<br>	* reasoning_tokens: the number of reasoning tokens. Type: unsigned integer.<br>For example, the following expressions are valid:<br>	* <code class="thin">model == 'llama' ?  input_tokens + output_token * 0.5 : total_tokens</code><br>	* <code class="thin">backend == 'foo.default' ?  input_tokens + output_tokens : total_tokens</code><br>	* <code class="thin">backend == 'bar.default' ?  (input_tokens - cached_input_tokens) + cached_input_tokens * 0.1 + cache_creation_input_tokens * 1.25 + output_tokens : total_tokens</code><br>	* <code class="thin">input_tokens + output_tokens + total_tokens</code><br>	* <code class="thin">input_tokens * output_tokens</code></div></div></div>
<h4 class="anchor anchorTargetStickyNavbar_Vzrq" id="llmrequestcosttype"><a id="github-com-envoyproxy-ai-gateway-api-v1alpha1-llmrequestcosttype">LLMRequestCostType</a><a href="#llmrequestcosttype" class="hash-link" aria-label="Direct link to llmrequestcosttype" title="Direct link to llmrequestcosttype" translate="no">​</a></h4>
<p><strong>Underlying type:</strong> string</p>
<p><strong>Appears in:</strong></p>
\<ul\>
<li class=""><a href="#github-com-envoyproxy-ai-gateway-api-v1alpha1-llmrequestcost" class="">LLMRequestCost</a></li>
</ul>
<p>LLMRequestCostType specifies the type of the LLMRequestCost.</p>
<h5 class="anchor anchorTargetStickyNavbar_Vzrq" id="possible-values-2">Possible Values<a href="#possible-values-2" class="hash-link" aria-label="Direct link to Possible Values" title="Direct link to Possible Values" translate="no">​</a></h5>
<div class="api-field" data-type="enum"><div class="api-field__left"><code class="api-field__name">InputToken</code></div><div class="api-field__right"><div class="api-field__description">LLMRequestCostTypeInputToken is the cost type of the input token.<br></div></div></div>
<div class="api-field" data-type="enum"><div class="api-field__left"><code class="api-field__name">CachedInputToken</code></div><div class="api-field__right"><div class="api-field__description">LLMRequestCostTypeCachedInputToken is the cost type of the cached input token.<br></div></div></div>
<div class="api-field" data-type="enum"><div class="api-field__left"><code class="api-field__name">CacheCreationInputToken</code></div><div class="api-field__right"><div class="api-field__description">LLMRequestCostTypeCacheCreationInputToken is the cost type of the cached input token.<br></div></div></div>
<div class="api-field" data-type="enum"><div class="api-field__left"><code class="api-field__name">OutputToken</code></div><div class="api-field__right"><div class="api-field__description">LLMRequestCostTypeOutputToken is the cost type of the output token.<br></div></div></div>
<div class="api-field" data-type="enum"><div class="api-field__left"><code class="api-field__name">TotalToken</code></div><div class="api-field__right"><div class="api-field__description">LLMRequestCostTypeTotalToken is the cost type of the total token.<br></div></div></div>
<div class="api-field" data-type="enum"><div class="api-field__left"><code class="api-field__name">ReasoningToken</code></div><div class="api-field__right"><div class="api-field__description">LLMRequestCostTypeReasoningToken is the cost type of the reasoning token.<br></div></div></div>
<div class="api-field" data-type="enum"><div class="api-field__left"><code class="api-field__name">CEL</code></div><div class="api-field__right"><div class="api-field__description">LLMRequestCostTypeCEL is for calculating the cost using the CEL expression.<br></div></div></div>
<h4 class="anchor anchorTargetStickyNavbar_Vzrq" id="mcpauthorizationsource"><a id="github-com-envoyproxy-ai-gateway-api-v1alpha1-mcpauthorizationsource">MCPAuthorizationSource</a><a href="#mcpauthorizationsource" class="hash-link" aria-label="Direct link to mcpauthorizationsource" title="Direct link to mcpauthorizationsource" translate="no">​</a></h4>
<p><strong>Appears in:</strong></p>
\<ul\>
<li class=""><a href="#github-com-envoyproxy-ai-gateway-api-v1alpha1-mcprouteauthorizationrule" class="">MCPRouteAuthorizationRule</a></li>
</ul>
<p>MCPAuthorizationSource defines the source of an authorization rule.</p>
<h5 class="anchor anchorTargetStickyNavbar_Vzrq" id="fields-45">Fields<a href="#fields-45" class="hash-link" aria-label="Direct link to Fields" title="Direct link to Fields" translate="no">​</a></h5>
<div class="api-field"><div class="api-field__left"><code class="api-field__name">jwt</code><span class="api-field__badge api-field__badge--required">required</span><div class="api-field__type"><div><a href="#github-com-envoyproxy-ai-gateway-api-v1alpha1-jwtsource">JWTSource</a></div></div></div><div class="api-field__right"><div class="api-field__description">JWT defines the JWT scopes required for this rule to match.</div></div></div>
<h4 class="anchor anchorTargetStickyNavbar_Vzrq" id="mcpauthorizationtarget"><a id="github-com-envoyproxy-ai-gateway-api-v1alpha1-mcpauthorizationtarget">MCPAuthorizationTarget</a><a href="#mcpauthorizationtarget" class="hash-link" aria-label="Direct link to mcpauthorizationtarget" title="Direct link to mcpauthorizationtarget" translate="no">​</a></h4>
<p><strong>Appears in:</strong></p>
\<ul\>
<li class=""><a href="#github-com-envoyproxy-ai-gateway-api-v1alpha1-mcprouteauthorizationrule" class="">MCPRouteAuthorizationRule</a></li>
</ul>
<p>MCPAuthorizationTarget defines the target of an authorization rule.</p>
<h5 class="anchor anchorTargetStickyNavbar_Vzrq" id="fields-46">Fields<a href="#fields-46" class="hash-link" aria-label="Direct link to Fields" title="Direct link to Fields" translate="no">​</a></h5>
<div class="api-field"><div class="api-field__left"><code class="api-field__name">tools</code><span class="api-field__badge api-field__badge--required">required</span><div class="api-field__type"><div><a href="#github-com-envoyproxy-ai-gateway-api-v1alpha1-toolcall">ToolCall</a> array</div></div></div><div class="api-field__right"><div class="api-field__description">Tools defines the list of tools this rule applies to.</div></div></div>
<h4 class="anchor anchorTargetStickyNavbar_Vzrq" id="mcpbackendapikey"><a id="github-com-envoyproxy-ai-gateway-api-v1alpha1-mcpbackendapikey">MCPBackendAPIKey</a><a href="#mcpbackendapikey" class="hash-link" aria-label="Direct link to mcpbackendapikey" title="Direct link to mcpbackendapikey" translate="no">​</a></h4>
<p><strong>Appears in:</strong></p>
\<ul\>
<li class=""><a href="#github-com-envoyproxy-ai-gateway-api-v1alpha1-mcpbackendsecuritypolicy" class="">MCPBackendSecurityPolicy</a></li>
</ul>
<p>MCPBackendAPIKey defines the configuration for the API Key Authentication to a backend.
When both <code>header</code> and <code>queryParam</code> are unspecified, the API key will be injected into the "Authorization" header by default.</p>
<h5 class="anchor anchorTargetStickyNavbar_Vzrq" id="fields-47">Fields<a href="#fields-47" class="hash-link" aria-label="Direct link to Fields" title="Direct link to Fields" translate="no">​</a></h5>
<div class="api-field"><div class="api-field__left"><code class="api-field__name">secretRef</code><span class="api-field__badge api-field__badge--optional">optional</span><div class="api-field__type"><div><a href="https://gateway-api.sigs.k8s.io/references/spec/#gateway.networking.k8s.io/v1.SecretObjectReference">SecretObjectReference</a></div></div></div><div class="api-field__right"><div class="api-field__description">secretRef is the Kubernetes secret which contains the API keys.<br>The key of the secret should be <code class="thin">apiKey</code>.</div></div></div>
<div class="api-field"><div class="api-field__left"><code class="api-field__name">inline</code><span class="api-field__badge api-field__badge--optional">optional</span><div class="api-field__type"><div>string</div></div></div><div class="api-field__right"><div class="api-field__description">Inline contains the API key as an inline string.</div></div></div>
<div class="api-field"><div class="api-field__left"><code class="api-field__name">header</code><span class="api-field__badge api-field__badge--optional">optional</span><div class="api-field__type"><div>string</div></div></div><div class="api-field__right"><div class="api-field__description">Header is the HTTP header to inject the API key into. If not specified,<br>defaults to <code class="thin">Authorization</code>.<br>When the header is <code class="thin">Authorization</code>, the injected header value will be<br>prefixed with <code class="thin">Bearer </code>.<br>Either one of Header or QueryParam can be specified to inject the API key.</div></div></div>
<div class="api-field"><div class="api-field__left"><code class="api-field__name">queryParam</code><span class="api-field__badge api-field__badge--optional">optional</span><div class="api-field__type"><div>string</div></div></div><div class="api-field__right"><div class="api-field__description">QueryParam is the HTTP query parameter to inject the API key into.<br>For example, if QueryParam is set to <code class="thin">api_key</code>, and the API key is <code class="thin">mysecretkey</code>, the request URL will be modified to include<br><code class="thin">?api_key=mysecretkey</code>.<br>Either one of Header or QueryParam can be specified to inject the API key.</div></div></div>
<h4 class="anchor anchorTargetStickyNavbar_Vzrq" id="mcpbackendsecuritypolicy"><a id="github-com-envoyproxy-ai-gateway-api-v1alpha1-mcpbackendsecuritypolicy">MCPBackendSecurityPolicy</a><a href="#mcpbackendsecuritypolicy" class="hash-link" aria-label="Direct link to mcpbackendsecuritypolicy" title="Direct link to mcpbackendsecuritypolicy" translate="no">​</a></h4>
<p><strong>Appears in:</strong></p>
\<ul\>
<li class=""><a href="#github-com-envoyproxy-ai-gateway-api-v1alpha1-mcproutebackendref" class="">MCPRouteBackendRef</a></li>
</ul>
<p>MCPBackendSecurityPolicy defines the security policy for a backend MCP server.</p>
<h5 class="anchor anchorTargetStickyNavbar_Vzrq" id="fields-48">Fields<a href="#fields-48" class="hash-link" aria-label="Direct link to Fields" title="Direct link to Fields" translate="no">​</a></h5>
<div class="api-field"><div class="api-field__left"><code class="api-field__name">apiKey</code><span class="api-field__badge api-field__badge--optional">optional</span><div class="api-field__type"><div><a href="#github-com-envoyproxy-ai-gateway-api-v1alpha1-mcpbackendapikey">MCPBackendAPIKey</a></div></div></div><div class="api-field__right"><div class="api-field__description">APIKey is a mechanism to access a backend. The API key will be injected into the request headers.</div></div></div>
<h4 class="anchor anchorTargetStickyNavbar_Vzrq" id="mcpbackendselector"><a id="github-com-envoyproxy-ai-gateway-api-v1alpha1-mcpbackendselector">MCPBackendSelector</a><a href="#mcpbackendselector" class="hash-link" aria-label="Direct link to mcpbackendselector" title="Direct link to mcpbackendselector" translate="no">​</a></h4>
<p><strong>Appears in:</strong></p>
\<ul\>
<li class=""><a href="#github-com-envoyproxy-ai-gateway-api-v1alpha1-mcproutespec" class="">MCPRouteSpec</a></li>
</ul>
<p>MCPBackendSelector defines which of a MCPRoute&#x27;s backends a request may fan out to.</p>
<h5 class="anchor anchorTargetStickyNavbar_Vzrq" id="fields-49">Fields<a href="#fields-49" class="hash-link" aria-label="Direct link to Fields" title="Direct link to Fields" translate="no">​</a></h5>
<div class="api-field"><div class="api-field__left"><code class="api-field__name">defaultAction</code><span class="api-field__badge api-field__badge--optional">optional</span><div class="api-field__type"><div><a href="#github-com-envoyproxy-gateway-api-v1alpha1-authorizationaction">AuthorizationAction</a></div></div></div><div class="api-field__right"><div class="api-field__default">Deny</div><div class="api-field__description">DefaultAction is the action to take when no rules match. If unspecified, defaults to Deny.</div></div></div>
<div class="api-field"><div class="api-field__left"><code class="api-field__name">rules</code><span class="api-field__badge api-field__badge--optional">optional</span><div class="api-field__type"><div><a href="#github-com-envoyproxy-ai-gateway-api-v1alpha1-mcpbackendselectorrule">MCPBackendSelectorRule</a> array</div></div></div><div class="api-field__right"><div class="api-field__description">Rules defines a list of backend selection rules.<br>These rules are evaluated in order, the first matching rule will be applied,<br>and the rest will be skipped.<br>If no rules are defined, DefaultAction is applied to every candidate backend<br>(defaults to Deny).</div></div></div>
<h4 class="anchor anchorTargetStickyNavbar_Vzrq" id="mcpbackendselectorrule"><a id="github-com-envoyproxy-ai-gateway-api-v1alpha1-mcpbackendselectorrule">MCPBackendSelectorRule</a><a href="#mcpbackendselectorrule" class="hash-link" aria-label="Direct link to mcpbackendselectorrule" title="Direct link to mcpbackendselectorrule" translate="no">​</a></h4>
<p><strong>Appears in:</strong></p>
\<ul\>
<li class=""><a href="#github-com-envoyproxy-ai-gateway-api-v1alpha1-mcpbackendselector" class="">MCPBackendSelector</a></li>
</ul>
<p>MCPBackendSelectorRule defines a single backend selection rule.</p>
<h5 class="anchor anchorTargetStickyNavbar_Vzrq" id="fields-50">Fields<a href="#fields-50" class="hash-link" aria-label="Direct link to Fields" title="Direct link to Fields" translate="no">​</a></h5>
<div class="api-field"><div class="api-field__left"><code class="api-field__name">cel</code><span class="api-field__badge api-field__badge--optional">optional</span><div class="api-field__type"><div>string</div></div></div><div class="api-field__right"><div class="api-field__description">CEL specifies a Common Expression Language (CEL) expression, evaluated once for<br>each backend already listed in backendRefs. It does not parse a list of backends<br>out of the request. Each evaluation binds request.mcp.backend to the name of the<br>one candidate backend under test, so the expression should answer <code class="thin">is this backend<br>allowed</code>, not <code class="thin">which backends should be used</code>. The expression must return a<br>boolean; evaluation errors or non-boolean results are treated as <code class="thin">no match</code>. A<br>match means the rule applies, and Action below decides whether that's an Allow or<br>a Deny for that backend.<br>Example CEL expressions:<br>	* <code class="thin">request.mcp.backend in request.auth.jwt.claims.mcp_backends</code><br>	* <code class="thin">(</code>,<code class="thin"> + request.headers[</code>x-ai-eg-mcp-backend-subset<code class="thin">] + </code>,<code class="thin">).contains(</code>,<code class="thin"> + request.mcp.backend + </code>,<code class="thin">)</code><br>Available attributes are the same as documented on MCPRouteAuthorizationRule.CEL,<br>except request.mcp.method, request.mcp.tool, and request.mcp.params are not<br>populated (no MCP method has been selected yet at backend-selection time).<br>Note: The CEL expression support is experimental, and the attributes<br>available to the expression may change in future releases.</div></div></div>
<div class="api-field"><div class="api-field__left"><code class="api-field__name">action</code><span class="api-field__badge api-field__badge--optional">optional</span><div class="api-field__type"><div><a href="#github-com-envoyproxy-gateway-api-v1alpha1-authorizationaction">AuthorizationAction</a></div></div></div><div class="api-field__right"><div class="api-field__default">Allow</div><div class="api-field__description">Action is the decision for matching backends. If unspecified, defaults to Allow.</div></div></div>
<h4 class="anchor anchorTargetStickyNavbar_Vzrq" id="mcpheaderforward"><a id="github-com-envoyproxy-ai-gateway-api-v1alpha1-mcpheaderforward">MCPHeaderForward</a><a href="#mcpheaderforward" class="hash-link" aria-label="Direct link to mcpheaderforward" title="Direct link to mcpheaderforward" translate="no">​</a></h4>
<p><strong>Appears in:</strong></p>
\<ul\>
<li class=""><a href="#github-com-envoyproxy-ai-gateway-api-v1alpha1-mcproutebackendref" class="">MCPRouteBackendRef</a></li>
</ul>
<p>MCPHeaderForward specifies a header to extract from the incoming request and forward to a backend.</p>
<h5 class="anchor anchorTargetStickyNavbar_Vzrq" id="fields-51">Fields<a href="#fields-51" class="hash-link" aria-label="Direct link to Fields" title="Direct link to Fields" translate="no">​</a></h5>
<div class="api-field"><div class="api-field__left"><code class="api-field__name">name</code><span class="api-field__badge api-field__badge--required">required</span><div class="api-field__type"><div>string</div></div></div><div class="api-field__right"><div class="api-field__description">Name is the header name to extract from the incoming client request.</div></div></div>
<div class="api-field"><div class="api-field__left"><code class="api-field__name">backendHeader</code><span class="api-field__badge api-field__badge--optional">optional</span><div class="api-field__type"><div>string</div></div></div><div class="api-field__right"><div class="api-field__description">BackendHeader is the header name to use when forwarding to the backend.<br>If not specified, the original header name is used.</div></div></div>
<h4 class="anchor anchorTargetStickyNavbar_Vzrq" id="mcprouteauthorization"><a id="github-com-envoyproxy-ai-gateway-api-v1alpha1-mcprouteauthorization">MCPRouteAuthorization</a><a href="#mcprouteauthorization" class="hash-link" aria-label="Direct link to mcprouteauthorization" title="Direct link to mcprouteauthorization" translate="no">​</a></h4>
<p><strong>Appears in:</strong></p>
\<ul\>
<li class=""><a href="#github-com-envoyproxy-ai-gateway-api-v1alpha1-mcproutesecuritypolicy" class="">MCPRouteSecurityPolicy</a></li>
</ul>
<p>MCPRouteAuthorization defines the authorization configuration for a MCPRoute.</p>
<h5 class="anchor anchorTargetStickyNavbar_Vzrq" id="fields-52">Fields<a href="#fields-52" class="hash-link" aria-label="Direct link to Fields" title="Direct link to Fields" translate="no">​</a></h5>
<div class="api-field"><div class="api-field__left"><code class="api-field__name">defaultAction</code><span class="api-field__badge api-field__badge--optional">optional</span><div class="api-field__type"><div><a href="#github-com-envoyproxy-gateway-api-v1alpha1-authorizationaction">AuthorizationAction</a></div></div></div><div class="api-field__right"><div class="api-field__default">Deny</div><div class="api-field__description">DefaultAction is the action to take when no rules match. If unspecified, defaults to Deny.</div></div></div>
<div class="api-field"><div class="api-field__left"><code class="api-field__name">rules</code><span class="api-field__badge api-field__badge--optional">optional</span><div class="api-field__type"><div><a href="#github-com-envoyproxy-ai-gateway-api-v1alpha1-mcprouteauthorizationrule">MCPRouteAuthorizationRule</a> array</div></div></div><div class="api-field__right"><div class="api-field__description">Rules defines a list of authorization rules.<br>These rules are evaluated in order, the first matching rule will be applied,<br>and the rest will be skipped.<br>If no rules are defined, the default action will be applied to all requests.</div></div></div>
<h4 class="anchor anchorTargetStickyNavbar_Vzrq" id="mcprouteauthorizationrule"><a id="github-com-envoyproxy-ai-gateway-api-v1alpha1-mcprouteauthorizationrule">MCPRouteAuthorizationRule</a><a href="#mcprouteauthorizationrule" class="hash-link" aria-label="Direct link to mcprouteauthorizationrule" title="Direct link to mcprouteauthorizationrule" translate="no">​</a></h4>
<p><strong>Appears in:</strong></p>
\<ul\>
<li class=""><a href="#github-com-envoyproxy-ai-gateway-api-v1alpha1-mcprouteauthorization" class="">MCPRouteAuthorization</a></li>
</ul>
<p>MCPRouteAuthorizationRule defines an authorization rule for MCPRoute based on the MCP authorization spec.
Reference: <a href="https://modelcontextprotocol.io/specification/draft/basic/authorization#scope-challenge-handling" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" class="">https://modelcontextprotocol.io/specification/draft/basic/authorization#scope-challenge-handling</a></p>
<h5 class="anchor anchorTargetStickyNavbar_Vzrq" id="fields-53">Fields<a href="#fields-53" class="hash-link" aria-label="Direct link to Fields" title="Direct link to Fields" translate="no">​</a></h5>
<div class="api-field"><div class="api-field__left"><code class="api-field__name">source</code><span class="api-field__badge api-field__badge--required">required</span><div class="api-field__type"><div><a href="#github-com-envoyproxy-ai-gateway-api-v1alpha1-mcpauthorizationsource">MCPAuthorizationSource</a></div></div></div><div class="api-field__right"><div class="api-field__description">Source defines the authorization source for this rule.<br>If not specified, the rule will match all sources.</div></div></div>
<div class="api-field"><div class="api-field__left"><code class="api-field__name">target</code><span class="api-field__badge api-field__badge--required">required</span><div class="api-field__type"><div><a href="#github-com-envoyproxy-ai-gateway-api-v1alpha1-mcpauthorizationtarget">MCPAuthorizationTarget</a></div></div></div><div class="api-field__right"><div class="api-field__description">Target defines the authorization target for this rule.<br>If not specified, the rule will match all targets.</div></div></div>
<div class="api-field"><div class="api-field__left"><code class="api-field__name">cel</code><span class="api-field__badge api-field__badge--optional">optional</span><div class="api-field__type"><div>string</div></div></div><div class="api-field__right"><div class="api-field__description">CEL specifies a Common Expression Language (CEL) expression evaluated for this rule.<br>The expression must return a boolean; evaluation errors or non-boolean results<br>are treated as <code class="thin">no match</code>.<br>Example CEL expressions:<br>	* <code class="thin">request.method == </code>POST`<code class="thin"><br>	* </code>request.headers[<code class="thin">x-custom-header</code>] == <code class="thin">AllowedValue</code><code class="thin"><br>	* </code>request.mcp.tool in [<code class="thin">toolA</code>, <code class="thin">toolB</code>]<code class="thin"><br>Available attributes in the CEL expression:<br>	* request.method: HTTP method such as GET or POST. Type: string.<br>	* request.headers: map of headers with lowercased keys, first value only. Type: map[string]string.<br>	* request.headers_all: map of headers with lowercased keys, all values. Type: map[string][]string.<br>	* request.path: request path such as /mcp. Type: string.<br>	* request.auth.jwt.claims: JWT claims when a bearer JWT is present. Type: map[string]any.<br>	* request.auth.jwt.scopes: JWT scopes when a bearer JWT is present. Type: []string.<br>	* request.mcp.method: MCP method such as tools/list or tools/call. Type: string.<br>	* request.mcp.backend: upstream backend name (for example, </code>kiwi<code class="thin"> or </code>github<code class="thin">). Type: string.<br>	* request.mcp.tool: tool name without backend prefix (for example, </code>list_issues<code class="thin">). Type: string.<br>	* request.mcp.params: parameters of the MCP method, including keys like </code>_meta<code class="thin"> and </code>arguments`. Type: object.<br>Note: The CEL expression support is experimental, and the attributes<br>available to the expression may change in future releases.</div></div></div>
<div class="api-field"><div class="api-field__left"><code class="api-field__name">action</code><span class="api-field__badge api-field__badge--optional">optional</span><div class="api-field__type"><div><a href="#github-com-envoyproxy-gateway-api-v1alpha1-authorizationaction">AuthorizationAction</a></div></div></div><div class="api-field__right"><div class="api-field__default">Allow</div><div class="api-field__description">Action is the authorization decision for matching requests. If unspecified, defaults to Allow.</div></div></div>
<h4 class="anchor anchorTargetStickyNavbar_Vzrq" id="mcproutebackendref"><a id="github-com-envoyproxy-ai-gateway-api-v1alpha1-mcproutebackendref">MCPRouteBackendRef</a><a href="#mcproutebackendref" class="hash-link" aria-label="Direct link to mcproutebackendref" title="Direct link to mcproutebackendref" translate="no">​</a></h4>
<p><strong>Appears in:</strong></p>
\<ul\>
<li class=""><a href="#github-com-envoyproxy-ai-gateway-api-v1alpha1-mcproutespec" class="">MCPRouteSpec</a></li>
</ul>
<p>MCPRouteBackendRef wraps a EG&#x27;s BackendObjectReference to reference an MCP server.</p>
<h5 class="anchor anchorTargetStickyNavbar_Vzrq" id="fields-54">Fields<a href="#fields-54" class="hash-link" aria-label="Direct link to Fields" title="Direct link to Fields" translate="no">​</a></h5>
<div class="api-field"><div class="api-field__left"><code class="api-field__name">group</code><span class="api-field__badge api-field__badge--optional">optional</span><div class="api-field__type"><div><a href="#sigs-k8s-io-gateway-api-apis-v1-group">Group</a></div></div></div><div class="api-field__right"><div class="api-field__description">Group is the group of the referent. For example, <code class="thin">gateway.networking.k8s.io</code>.<br>When unspecified or empty string, core API group is inferred.</div></div></div>
<div class="api-field"><div class="api-field__left"><code class="api-field__name">kind</code><span class="api-field__badge api-field__badge--optional">optional</span><div class="api-field__type"><div><a href="#sigs-k8s-io-gateway-api-apis-v1-kind">Kind</a></div></div></div><div class="api-field__right"><div class="api-field__default">Service</div><div class="api-field__description">Kind is the Kubernetes resource kind of the referent. For example<br><code class="thin">Service</code>.<br>Defaults to <code class="thin">Service</code> when not specified.<br>ExternalName services can refer to CNAME DNS records that may live<br>outside of the cluster and as such are difficult to reason about in<br>terms of conformance. They also may not be safe to forward to (see<br>CVE-2021-25740 for more information). Implementations SHOULD NOT<br>support ExternalName Services.<br>Support: Core (Services with a type other than ExternalName)<br>Support: Implementation-specific (Services with type ExternalName)</div></div></div>
<div class="api-field"><div class="api-field__left"><code class="api-field__name">name</code><span class="api-field__badge api-field__badge--required">required</span><div class="api-field__type"><div><a href="#sigs-k8s-io-gateway-api-apis-v1-objectname">ObjectName</a></div></div></div><div class="api-field__right"><div class="api-field__description">Name is the name of the referent.</div></div></div>
<div class="api-field"><div class="api-field__left"><code class="api-field__name">namespace</code><span class="api-field__badge api-field__badge--optional">optional</span><div class="api-field__type"><div><a href="https://gateway-api.sigs.k8s.io/reference/spec/?h=httproutetimeouts#namespace">Namespace</a></div></div></div><div class="api-field__right"><div class="api-field__description">Namespace is the namespace of the backend. When unspecified, the local<br>namespace is inferred.<br>Note that when a namespace different than the local namespace is specified,<br>a ReferenceGrant object is required in the referent namespace to allow that<br>namespace's owner to accept the reference. See the ReferenceGrant<br>documentation for details.<br>Support: Core</div></div></div>
<div class="api-field"><div class="api-field__left"><code class="api-field__name">port</code><span class="api-field__badge api-field__badge--optional">optional</span><div class="api-field__type"><div><a href="#sigs-k8s-io-gateway-api-apis-v1-portnumber">PortNumber</a></div></div></div><div class="api-field__right"><div class="api-field__description">Port specifies the destination port number to use for this resource.<br>Port is required when the referent is a Kubernetes Service. In this<br>case, the port number is the service port number, not the target port.<br>For other resources, destination port might be derived from the referent<br>resource or this field.</div></div></div>
<div class="api-field"><div class="api-field__left"><code class="api-field__name">path</code><span class="api-field__badge api-field__badge--optional">optional</span><div class="api-field__type"><div>string</div></div></div><div class="api-field__right"><div class="api-field__default">/mcp</div><div class="api-field__description">Path is the HTTP endpoint path of the backend MCP server.<br>If not specified, the default is <code class="thin">/mcp</code>.</div></div></div>
<div class="api-field"><div class="api-field__left"><code class="api-field__name">toolSelector</code><span class="api-field__badge api-field__badge--optional">optional</span><div class="api-field__type"><div><a href="#github-com-envoyproxy-ai-gateway-api-v1alpha1-mcptoolfilter">MCPToolFilter</a></div></div></div><div class="api-field__right"><div class="api-field__description">ToolSelector filters the tools exposed by this MCP server.<br>Supports exact matches and RE2-compatible regular expressions for both include and exclude patterns.<br>If not specified, all tools from the MCP server are exposed.</div></div></div>
<div class="api-field"><div class="api-field__left"><code class="api-field__name">securityPolicy</code><span class="api-field__badge api-field__badge--optional">optional</span><div class="api-field__type"><div><a href="#github-com-envoyproxy-ai-gateway-api-v1alpha1-mcpbackendsecuritypolicy">MCPBackendSecurityPolicy</a></div></div></div><div class="api-field__right"><div class="api-field__description">SecurityPolicy is the security policy to apply to this MCP server.</div></div></div>
<div class="api-field"><div class="api-field__left"><code class="api-field__name">forwardHeaders</code><span class="api-field__badge api-field__badge--optional">optional</span><div class="api-field__type"><div><a href="#github-com-envoyproxy-ai-gateway-api-v1alpha1-mcpheaderforward">MCPHeaderForward</a> array</div></div></div><div class="api-field__right"><div class="api-field__description">ForwardHeaders specifies HTTP headers to extract from the incoming client request<br>and forward to this backend MCP server.<br>This enables per-user authentication passthrough (e.g., personal access tokens)<br>without requiring OAuth configuration.<br>Each entry specifies a header name to extract and an optional rename for the backend.</div></div></div>
<h4 class="anchor anchorTargetStickyNavbar_Vzrq" id="mcproutebackendtrafficpolicy"><a id="github-com-envoyproxy-ai-gateway-api-v1alpha1-mcproutebackendtrafficpolicy">MCPRouteBackendTrafficPolicy</a><a href="#mcproutebackendtrafficpolicy" class="hash-link" aria-label="Direct link to mcproutebackendtrafficpolicy" title="Direct link to mcproutebackendtrafficpolicy" translate="no">​</a></h4>
<p><strong>Appears in:</strong></p>
\<ul\>
<li class=""><a href="#github-com-envoyproxy-ai-gateway-api-v1alpha1-mcproutespec" class="">MCPRouteSpec</a></li>
</ul>
<p>MCPRouteBackendTrafficPolicy configures how the Envoy Gateway BackendTrafficPolicy
resources auto-generated for a MCPRoute interact with other BackendTrafficPolicy
resources (e.g. an operator-defined rate limiting policy) targeting the same HTTPRoute.</p>
<h5 class="anchor anchorTargetStickyNavbar_Vzrq" id="fields-55">Fields<a href="#fields-55" class="hash-link" aria-label="Direct link to Fields" title="Direct link to Fields" translate="no">​</a></h5>
<div class="api-field"><div class="api-field__left"><code class="api-field__name">mergeType</code><span class="api-field__badge api-field__badge--optional">optional</span><div class="api-field__type"><div><a href="#github-com-envoyproxy-gateway-api-v1alpha1-mergetype">MergeType</a></div></div></div><div class="api-field__right"><div class="api-field__description">MergeType determines how the auto-generated BackendTrafficPolicy (for example, the<br>OAuth protected resource metadata policy) is merged with other BackendTrafficPolicy<br>configurations targeting the same route or a parent Gateway/Listener.<br>When set, the auto-generated BackendTrafficPolicy will merge with the closest parent<br>BackendTrafficPolicy in the route's attachment hierarchy instead of overriding it<br>entirely, per Envoy Gateway's merge semantics.<br>If unset, no merging occurs, and the auto-generated BackendTrafficPolicy fully<br>overrides any other BackendTrafficPolicy targeting the same route.<br>See: https://gateway.envoyproxy.io/docs/tasks/traffic/backend-traffic-policy/</div></div></div>
<h4 class="anchor anchorTargetStickyNavbar_Vzrq" id="mcprouteoauth"><a id="github-com-envoyproxy-ai-gateway-api-v1alpha1-mcprouteoauth">MCPRouteOAuth</a><a href="#mcprouteoauth" class="hash-link" aria-label="Direct link to mcprouteoauth" title="Direct link to mcprouteoauth" translate="no">​</a></h4>
<p><strong>Appears in:</strong></p>
\<ul\>
<li class=""><a href="#github-com-envoyproxy-ai-gateway-api-v1alpha1-mcproutesecuritypolicy" class="">MCPRouteSecurityPolicy</a></li>
</ul>
<p>MCPRouteOAuth defines a MCP spec compatible OAuth authentication configuration for a MCPRoute.
Reference: <a href="https://modelcontextprotocol.io/specification/2025-06-18/basic/authorization" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" class="">https://modelcontextprotocol.io/specification/2025-06-18/basic/authorization</a></p>
<h5 class="anchor anchorTargetStickyNavbar_Vzrq" id="fields-56">Fields<a href="#fields-56" class="hash-link" aria-label="Direct link to Fields" title="Direct link to Fields" translate="no">​</a></h5>
<div class="api-field"><div class="api-field__left"><code class="api-field__name">issuer</code><span class="api-field__badge api-field__badge--required">required</span><div class="api-field__type"><div>string</div></div></div><div class="api-field__right"><div class="api-field__description">Issuer is the authorization server's issuer identity.</div></div></div>
<div class="api-field"><div class="api-field__left"><code class="api-field__name">audiences</code><span class="api-field__badge api-field__badge--optional">optional</span><div class="api-field__type"><div>string array</div></div></div><div class="api-field__right"><div class="api-field__description">Audiences is a list of JWT audiences allowed access.<br>It is recommended to set this field for token audience validation, as it is a security best practice to prevent token misuse.<br>Reference: https://modelcontextprotocol.io/specification/2025-06-18/basic/authorization#token-audience-binding-and-validation</div></div></div>
<div class="api-field"><div class="api-field__left"><code class="api-field__name">jwks</code><span class="api-field__badge api-field__badge--optional">optional</span><div class="api-field__type"><div><a href="#github-com-envoyproxy-ai-gateway-api-v1alpha1-jwks">JWKS</a></div></div></div><div class="api-field__right"><div class="api-field__description">JWKS defines how a JSON Web Key Sets (JWKS) can be obtained to verify the access tokens presented by the clients.<br>If not specified, the JWKS URI will be discovered from the OAuth 2.0 Authorization Server Metadata<br>as per RFC 8414 by querying the <code class="thin">/.well-known/oauth-authorization-server</code> endpoint on the Issuer.</div></div></div>
<div class="api-field"><div class="api-field__left"><code class="api-field__name">authorizationServerMetadataUrl</code><span class="api-field__badge api-field__badge--optional">optional</span><div class="api-field__type"><div>string</div></div></div><div class="api-field__right"><div class="api-field__description">AuthorizationServerMetadataURL is the URL the controller fetches the OAuth 2.0 Authorization<br>Server Metadata document from, as defined in RFC 8414. When set, it replaces the well-known<br>URIs derived from Issuer.<br>Set this when the metadata lives somewhere the issuer does not lead to, for example an issuer<br>of <code class="thin">https://example.com/api/idp/authn</code> whose document is served only at<br><code class="thin">https://example.com/api/idp/v4/authn/.well-known/openid-configuration</code>.<br>Issuer is unaffected by this field. It continues to identify the authorization server in the<br>protected resource metadata the gateway publishes, and to derive the well-known URIs when this<br>field is unset. When JWKS is not set, the JWKS URI is discovered from the document fetched here.</div></div></div>
<div class="api-field"><div class="api-field__left"><code class="api-field__name">protectedResourceMetadata</code><span class="api-field__badge api-field__badge--required">required</span><div class="api-field__type"><div><a href="#github-com-envoyproxy-ai-gateway-api-v1alpha1-protectedresourcemetadata">ProtectedResourceMetadata</a></div></div></div><div class="api-field__right"><div class="api-field__description">ProtectedResourceMetadata defines the OAuth 2.0 Resource Server Metadata as per RFC 8414.<br>This is used to expose the metadata endpoint for mcp clients to discover the authorization servers,<br>supported scopes, and JWKS URI.</div></div></div>
<div class="api-field"><div class="api-field__left"><code class="api-field__name">claimToHeaders</code><span class="api-field__badge api-field__badge--optional">optional</span><div class="api-field__type"><div>ClaimToHeader array</div></div></div><div class="api-field__right"><div class="api-field__description">ClaimToHeaders specifies JWT claims to extract and forward as HTTP headers to backend MCP servers.<br>This enables backends to access user identity for authorization, auditing, or personalization.<br>Security considerations:<br>- Any client-provided headers matching the configured header names will be stripped to prevent forgery<br>- Only the specified claims are extracted; the full JWT is not forwarded to backends<br>- Consider using a header prefix (e.g., <code class="thin">X-Jwt-Claim-</code>) to avoid conflicts with other headers</div></div></div>
<h4 class="anchor anchorTargetStickyNavbar_Vzrq" id="mcproutesecuritypolicy"><a id="github-com-envoyproxy-ai-gateway-api-v1alpha1-mcproutesecuritypolicy">MCPRouteSecurityPolicy</a><a href="#mcproutesecuritypolicy" class="hash-link" aria-label="Direct link to mcproutesecuritypolicy" title="Direct link to mcproutesecuritypolicy" translate="no">​</a></h4>
<p><strong>Appears in:</strong></p>
\<ul\>
<li class=""><a href="#github-com-envoyproxy-ai-gateway-api-v1alpha1-mcproutespec" class="">MCPRouteSpec</a></li>
</ul>
<p>MCPRouteSecurityPolicy defines the security policy for a MCPRoute.</p>
<h5 class="anchor anchorTargetStickyNavbar_Vzrq" id="fields-57">Fields<a href="#fields-57" class="hash-link" aria-label="Direct link to Fields" title="Direct link to Fields" translate="no">​</a></h5>
<div class="api-field"><div class="api-field__left"><code class="api-field__name">oauth</code><span class="api-field__badge api-field__badge--optional">optional</span><div class="api-field__type"><div><a href="#github-com-envoyproxy-ai-gateway-api-v1alpha1-mcprouteoauth">MCPRouteOAuth</a></div></div></div><div class="api-field__right"><div class="api-field__description">OAuth defines the configuration for the MCP spec compatible OAuth authentication.</div></div></div>
<div class="api-field"><div class="api-field__left"><code class="api-field__name">apiKeyAuth</code><span class="api-field__badge api-field__badge--optional">optional</span><div class="api-field__type"><div><a href="https://gateway.envoyproxy.io/docs/api/extension_types/#apikeyauth">APIKeyAuth</a></div></div></div><div class="api-field__right"><div class="api-field__description">APIKeyAuth defines the configuration for the API Key Authentication.</div></div></div>
<div class="api-field"><div class="api-field__left"><code class="api-field__name">extAuth</code><span class="api-field__badge api-field__badge--optional">optional</span><div class="api-field__type"><div><a href="#github-com-envoyproxy-gateway-api-v1alpha1-extauth">ExtAuth</a></div></div></div><div class="api-field__right"><div class="api-field__description">ExtAuth defines the configuration for External Authorization.</div></div></div>
<div class="api-field"><div class="api-field__left"><code class="api-field__name">authorization</code><span class="api-field__badge api-field__badge--optional">optional</span><div class="api-field__type"><div><a href="#github-com-envoyproxy-ai-gateway-api-v1alpha1-mcprouteauthorization">MCPRouteAuthorization</a></div></div></div><div class="api-field__right"><div class="api-field__description">Authorization defines the configuration for the MCP spec compatible authorization.</div></div></div>
<div class="api-field"><div class="api-field__left"><code class="api-field__name">mergeType</code><span class="api-field__badge api-field__badge--optional">optional</span><div class="api-field__type"><div><a href="#github-com-envoyproxy-gateway-api-v1alpha1-mergetype">MergeType</a></div></div></div><div class="api-field__right"><div class="api-field__description">MergeType determines how the auto-generated SecurityPolicy for this MCPRoute (which<br>enforces OAuth/JWT, API key, or external authorization) is merged with other<br>SecurityPolicy configurations targeting the same route or a parent Gateway/Listener.<br>When set, the auto-generated SecurityPolicy will merge with the closest parent<br>SecurityPolicy in the route's attachment hierarchy (for example, one targeting a<br>Gateway, Gateway listener, ListenerSet, or ListenerSet listener) instead of<br>overriding it entirely, per Envoy Gateway's merge semantics.<br>If unset, no merging occurs, and the auto-generated SecurityPolicy fully overrides<br>any other SecurityPolicy targeting the same route.<br>See: https://gateway.envoyproxy.io/docs/tasks/security/apikey-authn/</div></div></div>
<h4 class="anchor anchorTargetStickyNavbar_Vzrq" id="mcproutespec"><a id="github-com-envoyproxy-ai-gateway-api-v1alpha1-mcproutespec">MCPRouteSpec</a><a href="#mcproutespec" class="hash-link" aria-label="Direct link to mcproutespec" title="Direct link to mcproutespec" translate="no">​</a></h4>
<p><strong>Appears in:</strong></p>
\<ul\>
<li class=""><a href="#github-com-envoyproxy-ai-gateway-api-v1alpha1-mcproute" class="">MCPRoute</a></li>
</ul>
<p>MCPRouteSpec details the MCPRoute configuration.</p>
<h5 class="anchor anchorTargetStickyNavbar_Vzrq" id="fields-58">Fields<a href="#fields-58" class="hash-link" aria-label="Direct link to Fields" title="Direct link to Fields" translate="no">​</a></h5>
<div class="api-field"><div class="api-field__left"><code class="api-field__name">parentRefs</code><span class="api-field__badge api-field__badge--required">required</span><div class="api-field__type"><div><a href="https://gateway-api.sigs.k8s.io/reference/spec/?h=httproutetimeouts#parentreference">ParentReference</a> array</div></div></div><div class="api-field__right"><div class="api-field__description">ParentRefs are the names of the Gateway resources this MCPRoute is being attached to.<br>Cross namespace references are not supported. In other words, the Gateway resources must be in the<br>same namespace as the MCPRoute. Currently, each reference's Kind must be Gateway.</div></div></div>
<div class="api-field"><div class="api-field__left"><code class="api-field__name">path</code><span class="api-field__badge api-field__badge--optional">optional</span><div class="api-field__type"><div>string</div></div></div><div class="api-field__right"><div class="api-field__default">/mcp</div><div class="api-field__description">Path is the HTTP endpoint path that serves MCP requests over the Streamable HTTP transport.<br>If not specified, the default is <code class="thin">/mcp</code>.</div></div></div>
<div class="api-field"><div class="api-field__left"><code class="api-field__name">headers</code><span class="api-field__badge api-field__badge--optional">optional</span><div class="api-field__type"><div><a href="https://gateway-api.sigs.k8s.io/reference/spec/?h=httproutetimeouts#httpheadermatch">HTTPHeaderMatch</a> array</div></div></div><div class="api-field__right"><div class="api-field__description">Headers are HTTP headers that must match for this route to be selected.<br>Multiple match values are ANDed together, meaning, a request must match all the specified headers to select the route.</div></div></div>
<div class="api-field"><div class="api-field__left"><code class="api-field__name">hostnames</code><span class="api-field__badge api-field__badge--optional">optional</span><div class="api-field__type"><div>Hostname array</div></div></div><div class="api-field__right"><div class="api-field__description">Hostnames is a list of hostnames matched against the HTTP Host header to select this MCPRoute.<br>This is equivalent to the Hostnames field in the Gateway API HTTPRouteSpec. When specified, the<br>generated HTTPRoute includes these hostnames, so the MCP route lands in the per-host virtual host<br>instead of the wildcard vhost (the same scoping AIGatewayRoute.Hostnames provides).<br>See https://gateway-api.sigs.k8s.io/reference/api-types/httproute/#hostnames<br>for the details of the Hostnames field in the Gateway API.</div></div></div>
<div class="api-field"><div class="api-field__left"><code class="api-field__name">backendRefs</code><span class="api-field__badge api-field__badge--required">required</span><div class="api-field__type"><div><a href="#github-com-envoyproxy-ai-gateway-api-v1alpha1-mcproutebackendref">MCPRouteBackendRef</a> array</div></div></div><div class="api-field__right"><div class="api-field__description">BackendRefs is a list of backend references to the MCP servers.<br>These MCP servers will be aggregated and exposed as a single MCP endpoint to the clients.<br>From the client's perspective, they only need to configure a single MCP server URL, e.g. <code class="thin">https://api.example.com/mcp</code>,<br>and the Envoy AI Gateway will route the requests to the appropriate MCP server based on the requests.<br>All names must be unique within this list to avoid potential tools, resources, etc. name collisions.<br>Also, cross-namespace references are not supported. In other words, the backend MCP servers must be in the<br>same namespace as the MCPRoute.</div></div></div>
<div class="api-field"><div class="api-field__left"><code class="api-field__name">securityPolicy</code><span class="api-field__badge api-field__badge--optional">optional</span><div class="api-field__type"><div><a href="#github-com-envoyproxy-ai-gateway-api-v1alpha1-mcproutesecuritypolicy">MCPRouteSecurityPolicy</a></div></div></div><div class="api-field__right"><div class="api-field__description">SecurityPolicy defines the security policy for this MCPRoute.</div></div></div>
<div class="api-field"><div class="api-field__left"><code class="api-field__name">backendTrafficPolicy</code><span class="api-field__badge api-field__badge--optional">optional</span><div class="api-field__type"><div><a href="#github-com-envoyproxy-ai-gateway-api-v1alpha1-mcproutebackendtrafficpolicy">MCPRouteBackendTrafficPolicy</a></div></div></div><div class="api-field__right"><div class="api-field__description">BackendTrafficPolicy configures the auto-generated Envoy Gateway BackendTrafficPolicy<br>resources for this MCPRoute, for example the OAuth protected resource metadata policy<br>created when SecurityPolicy.OAuth is configured.</div></div></div>
<div class="api-field"><div class="api-field__left"><code class="api-field__name">backendSelector</code><span class="api-field__badge api-field__badge--optional">optional</span><div class="api-field__type"><div><a href="#github-com-envoyproxy-ai-gateway-api-v1alpha1-mcpbackendselector">MCPBackendSelector</a></div></div></div><div class="api-field__right"><div class="api-field__description">BackendSelector restricts which of this route's backends a given request may fan<br>out to, evaluated once per candidate backend when a client session is initialized.<br>If unspecified, all backends on the route are considered.<br>Security note: a backendSelector rule whose CEL expression reads request.auth.jwt<br>(claims or scopes) only reflects verified JWT data when SecurityPolicy.OAuth is<br>configured for this route; the XValidation below enforces that pairing so that<br>unauthenticated or forged bearer tokens can't influence backend selection.</div></div></div>
<h4 class="anchor anchorTargetStickyNavbar_Vzrq" id="mcproutestatus"><a id="github-com-envoyproxy-ai-gateway-api-v1alpha1-mcproutestatus">MCPRouteStatus</a><a href="#mcproutestatus" class="hash-link" aria-label="Direct link to mcproutestatus" title="Direct link to mcproutestatus" translate="no">​</a></h4>
<p><strong>Appears in:</strong></p>
\<ul\>
<li class=""><a href="#github-com-envoyproxy-ai-gateway-api-v1alpha1-mcproute" class="">MCPRoute</a></li>
</ul>
<p>MCPRouteStatus contains the conditions by the reconciliation result.</p>
<h5 class="anchor anchorTargetStickyNavbar_Vzrq" id="fields-59">Fields<a href="#fields-59" class="hash-link" aria-label="Direct link to Fields" title="Direct link to Fields" translate="no">​</a></h5>
<div class="api-field"><div class="api-field__left"><code class="api-field__name">conditions</code><span class="api-field__badge api-field__badge--required">required</span><div class="api-field__type"><div><a href="https://kubernetes.io/docs/reference/generated/kubernetes-api/v1.29/#condition-v1-meta">Condition</a> array</div></div></div><div class="api-field__right"><div class="api-field__description">Conditions is the list of conditions by the reconciliation result.<br>Currently, at most one condition is set.<br>Known .status.conditions.type are: <code class="thin">Accepted</code>, <code class="thin">NotAccepted</code>.</div></div></div>
<h4 class="anchor anchorTargetStickyNavbar_Vzrq" id="mcptoolfilter"><a id="github-com-envoyproxy-ai-gateway-api-v1alpha1-mcptoolfilter">MCPToolFilter</a><a href="#mcptoolfilter" class="hash-link" aria-label="Direct link to mcptoolfilter" title="Direct link to mcptoolfilter" translate="no">​</a></h4>
<p><strong>Appears in:</strong></p>
\<ul\>
<li class=""><a href="#github-com-envoyproxy-ai-gateway-api-v1alpha1-mcproutebackendref" class="">MCPRouteBackendRef</a></li>
</ul>
<p>MCPToolFilter filters tools using include and exclude patterns with exact matches or regular expressions.
Exclude rules take precedence over include rules (deny-wins). When both include and exclude are specified,
a tool must match an include rule AND not match any exclude rule to be allowed.</p>
<h5 class="anchor anchorTargetStickyNavbar_Vzrq" id="fields-60">Fields<a href="#fields-60" class="hash-link" aria-label="Direct link to Fields" title="Direct link to Fields" translate="no">​</a></h5>
<div class="api-field"><div class="api-field__left"><code class="api-field__name">include</code><span class="api-field__badge api-field__badge--optional">optional</span><div class="api-field__type"><div>string array</div></div></div><div class="api-field__right"><div class="api-field__description">Include is a list of tool names to include. Only the specified tools will be available.</div></div></div>
<div class="api-field"><div class="api-field__left"><code class="api-field__name">includeRegex</code><span class="api-field__badge api-field__badge--optional">optional</span><div class="api-field__type"><div>string array</div></div></div><div class="api-field__right"><div class="api-field__description">IncludeRegex is a list of RE2-compatible regular expressions that, when matched, include the tool.<br>Only tools matching these patterns will be available.</div></div></div>
<div class="api-field"><div class="api-field__left"><code class="api-field__name">exclude</code><span class="api-field__badge api-field__badge--optional">optional</span><div class="api-field__type"><div>string array</div></div></div><div class="api-field__right"><div class="api-field__description">Exclude is a list of tool names to exclude. The specified tools will not be available.<br>Exclude rules take precedence over include rules.</div></div></div>
<div class="api-field"><div class="api-field__left"><code class="api-field__name">excludeRegex</code><span class="api-field__badge api-field__badge--optional">optional</span><div class="api-field__type"><div>string array</div></div></div><div class="api-field__right"><div class="api-field__description">ExcludeRegex is a list of RE2-compatible regular expressions that, when matched, exclude the tool.<br>Tools matching these patterns will not be available. Exclude rules take precedence over include rules.</div></div></div>
<h4 class="anchor anchorTargetStickyNavbar_Vzrq" id="permodelquota"><a id="github-com-envoyproxy-ai-gateway-api-v1alpha1-permodelquota">PerModelQuota</a><a href="#permodelquota" class="hash-link" aria-label="Direct link to permodelquota" title="Direct link to permodelquota" translate="no">​</a></h4>
<p><strong>Appears in:</strong></p>
\<ul\>
<li class=""><a href="#github-com-envoyproxy-ai-gateway-api-v1alpha1-quotapolicyspec" class="">QuotaPolicySpec</a></li>
</ul>
<h5 class="anchor anchorTargetStickyNavbar_Vzrq" id="fields-61">Fields<a href="#fields-61" class="hash-link" aria-label="Direct link to Fields" title="Direct link to Fields" translate="no">​</a></h5>
<div class="api-field"><div class="api-field__left"><code class="api-field__name">modelName</code><span class="api-field__badge api-field__badge--required">required</span><div class="api-field__type"><div>string</div></div></div><div class="api-field__right"><div class="api-field__description">Model name for which the quota is specified.<br>This must match the ModelNameOverride set in the AIGatewayRoute's BackendRef<br>for the quota policy to apply to that backend.</div></div></div>
<div class="api-field"><div class="api-field__left"><code class="api-field__name">quota</code><span class="api-field__badge api-field__badge--required">required</span><div class="api-field__type"><div><a href="#github-com-envoyproxy-ai-gateway-api-v1alpha1-quotadefinition">QuotaDefinition</a></div></div></div><div class="api-field__right"><div class="api-field__description">Expression for computing request cost and rules for matching requests to quota buckets.</div></div></div>
<h4 class="anchor anchorTargetStickyNavbar_Vzrq" id="protectedresourcemetadata"><a id="github-com-envoyproxy-ai-gateway-api-v1alpha1-protectedresourcemetadata">ProtectedResourceMetadata</a><a href="#protectedresourcemetadata" class="hash-link" aria-label="Direct link to protectedresourcemetadata" title="Direct link to protectedresourcemetadata" translate="no">​</a></h4>
<p><strong>Appears in:</strong></p>
\<ul\>
<li class=""><a href="#github-com-envoyproxy-ai-gateway-api-v1alpha1-mcprouteoauth" class="">MCPRouteOAuth</a></li>
</ul>
<p>ProtectedResourceMetadata represents the Protected Resource Metadata	of the MCP server as per RFC 9728.</p>
<p>References:</p>
\<ul\>
<li class=""><a href="https://modelcontextprotocol.io/specification/2025-06-18/basic/authorization#authorization-server-location" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" class="">https://modelcontextprotocol.io/specification/2025-06-18/basic/authorization#authorization-server-location</a></li>
<li class=""><a href="https://datatracker.ietf.org/doc/html/rfc9728#name-protected-resource-metadata" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" class="">https://datatracker.ietf.org/doc/html/rfc9728#name-protected-resource-metadata</a></li>
</ul>
<h5 class="anchor anchorTargetStickyNavbar_Vzrq" id="fields-62">Fields<a href="#fields-62" class="hash-link" aria-label="Direct link to Fields" title="Direct link to Fields" translate="no">​</a></h5>
<div class="api-field"><div class="api-field__left"><code class="api-field__name">resource</code><span class="api-field__badge api-field__badge--required">required</span><div class="api-field__type"><div>string</div></div></div><div class="api-field__right"><div class="api-field__description">Resource is the identifier of the protected resource.<br>This should match the MCPRoute's URL. For example, if the MCPRoute's URL is<br><code class="thin">https://api.example.com/mcp</code>, the Resource should be <code class="thin">https://api.example.com/mcp</code>.</div></div></div>
<div class="api-field"><div class="api-field__left"><code class="api-field__name">resourceName</code><span class="api-field__badge api-field__badge--optional">optional</span><div class="api-field__type"><div>string</div></div></div><div class="api-field__right"><div class="api-field__description">ResourceName is a human-readable name for the protected resource.</div></div></div>
<div class="api-field"><div class="api-field__left"><code class="api-field__name">scopesSupported</code><span class="api-field__badge api-field__badge--optional">optional</span><div class="api-field__type"><div>string array</div></div></div><div class="api-field__right"><div class="api-field__description">ScopesSupported defines the minimal set of scopes required for the basic functionality of the MCPRoute.<br>It should avoid broad or overly permissive scopes to prevent clients from requesting tokens with excessive privileges.<br>If an operation requires additional scopes that are not present in the access token, the client will receive a<br>403 Forbidden response that includes the required scopes in the <code class="thin">scope</code> field of the <code class="thin">WWW-Authenticate</code> header.<br>This enables incremental privilege elevation through targeted <code class="thin">WWW-Authenticate: scope=</code>...`` challenges when<br>privileged operations are first attempted.</div></div></div>
<div class="api-field"><div class="api-field__left"><code class="api-field__name">resourceSigningAlgValuesSupported</code><span class="api-field__badge api-field__badge--optional">optional</span><div class="api-field__type"><div>string array</div></div></div><div class="api-field__right"><div class="api-field__description">ResourceSigningAlgValuesSupported is a list of JWS signing algorithms supported by the resource server.<br>These algorithms are used in the <code class="thin">alg</code> field of the JOSE header in signed tokens.</div></div></div>
<div class="api-field"><div class="api-field__left"><code class="api-field__name">resourceDocumentation</code><span class="api-field__badge api-field__badge--optional">optional</span><div class="api-field__type"><div>string</div></div></div><div class="api-field__right"><div class="api-field__description">ResourceDocumentation is a URL that provides human-readable documentation for the resource.</div></div></div>
<div class="api-field"><div class="api-field__left"><code class="api-field__name">resourcePolicyUri</code><span class="api-field__badge api-field__badge--optional">optional</span><div class="api-field__type"><div>string</div></div></div><div class="api-field__right"><div class="api-field__description">ResourcePolicyURI is a URL that points to the resource server's policy document.</div></div></div>
<h4 class="anchor anchorTargetStickyNavbar_Vzrq" id="quotabucketmode"><a id="github-com-envoyproxy-ai-gateway-api-v1alpha1-quotabucketmode">QuotaBucketMode</a><a href="#quotabucketmode" class="hash-link" aria-label="Direct link to quotabucketmode" title="Direct link to quotabucketmode" translate="no">​</a></h4>
<p><strong>Underlying type:</strong> string</p>
<p><strong>Appears in:</strong></p>
\<ul\>
<li class=""><a href="#github-com-envoyproxy-ai-gateway-api-v1alpha1-quotadefinition" class="">QuotaDefinition</a></li>
</ul>
<p>QuotaBucketMode specifies whether the default and per request buckets values are exclusive or inclusive.</p>
<h5 class="anchor anchorTargetStickyNavbar_Vzrq" id="possible-values-3">Possible Values<a href="#possible-values-3" class="hash-link" aria-label="Direct link to Possible Values" title="Direct link to Possible Values" translate="no">​</a></h5>
<div class="api-field" data-type="enum"><div class="api-field__left"><code class="api-field__name">Shared</code></div><div class="api-field__right"></div></div>
<h4 class="anchor anchorTargetStickyNavbar_Vzrq" id="quotadefinition"><a id="github-com-envoyproxy-ai-gateway-api-v1alpha1-quotadefinition">QuotaDefinition</a><a href="#quotadefinition" class="hash-link" aria-label="Direct link to quotadefinition" title="Direct link to quotadefinition" translate="no">​</a></h4>
<p><strong>Appears in:</strong></p>
\<ul\>
<li class=""><a href="#github-com-envoyproxy-ai-gateway-api-v1alpha1-permodelquota" class="">PerModelQuota</a></li>
</ul>
<p>QuotaDefinition specified expression for computing request cost and rules for matching requests to quota buckets.</p>
<h5 class="anchor anchorTargetStickyNavbar_Vzrq" id="fields-63">Fields<a href="#fields-63" class="hash-link" aria-label="Direct link to Fields" title="Direct link to Fields" translate="no">​</a></h5>
<div class="api-field"><div class="api-field__left"><code class="api-field__name">costExpression</code><span class="api-field__badge api-field__badge--optional">optional</span><div class="api-field__type"><div>string</div></div></div><div class="api-field__right"><div class="api-field__description">CostExpression specifies a CEL expression for computing the quota burndown of the LLM-related request.<br>If no expression is specified the <code class="thin">total_tokens</code> value is used.<br>For example:<br> <code class="thin">input_tokens + cached_input_tokens + output_tokens</code></div></div></div>
<div class="api-field"><div class="api-field__left"><code class="api-field__name">mode</code><span class="api-field__badge api-field__badge--optional">optional</span><div class="api-field__type"><div><a href="#github-com-envoyproxy-ai-gateway-api-v1alpha1-quotabucketmode">QuotaBucketMode</a></div></div></div><div class="api-field__right"><div class="api-field__default">Shared</div><div class="api-field__description">The <code class="thin">Mode</code> determines how quota is charged to the <code class="thin">DefaultBucket</code> and matching <code class="thin">BucketRules</code>.<br>In the <code class="thin">shared</code> mode the quota is charged to all matching <code class="thin">BucketRules</code> AND the <code class="thin">DefaultBucket</code><br>and request is allowed only if the quota is available in at least one matching buckets.<br>Defaults to <code class="thin">Shared</code>.</div></div></div>
<div class="api-field"><div class="api-field__left"><code class="api-field__name">defaultBucket</code><span class="api-field__badge api-field__badge--optional">optional</span><div class="api-field__type"><div><a href="#github-com-envoyproxy-ai-gateway-api-v1alpha1-quotavalue">QuotaValue</a></div></div></div><div class="api-field__right"><div class="api-field__description">Quota applicable to all traffic. This value can be overridden for specific classes of requests<br>using the <code class="thin">BucketRules</code> configuration.</div></div></div>
<div class="api-field"><div class="api-field__left"><code class="api-field__name">bucketRules</code><span class="api-field__badge api-field__badge--optional">optional</span><div class="api-field__type"><div><a href="#github-com-envoyproxy-ai-gateway-api-v1alpha1-quotarule">QuotaRule</a> array</div></div></div><div class="api-field__right"><div class="api-field__description">BucketRules are a list of client selectors and quotas. If a request<br>matches multiple rules, each of their associated quotas get applied, so a<br>single request might burn down the quota for multiple rules.<br>Client selectors that match under the same model / service backend will be<br>combined with the first limit taking precedence.</div></div></div>
<h4 class="anchor anchorTargetStickyNavbar_Vzrq" id="quotapolicyspec"><a id="github-com-envoyproxy-ai-gateway-api-v1alpha1-quotapolicyspec">QuotaPolicySpec</a><a href="#quotapolicyspec" class="hash-link" aria-label="Direct link to quotapolicyspec" title="Direct link to quotapolicyspec" translate="no">​</a></h4>
<p><strong>Appears in:</strong></p>
\<ul\>
<li class=""><a href="#github-com-envoyproxy-ai-gateway-api-v1alpha1-quotapolicy" class="">QuotaPolicy</a></li>
</ul>
<p>QuotaPolicySpec specifies rules for computing token based costs of requests.</p>
<h5 class="anchor anchorTargetStickyNavbar_Vzrq" id="fields-64">Fields<a href="#fields-64" class="hash-link" aria-label="Direct link to Fields" title="Direct link to Fields" translate="no">​</a></h5>
<div class="api-field"><div class="api-field__left"><code class="api-field__name">targetRefs</code><span class="api-field__badge api-field__badge--optional">optional</span><div class="api-field__type"><div><a href="https://gateway-api.sigs.k8s.io/reference/spec/#gateway.networking.k8s.io/v1alpha2.LocalPolicyTargetReference">LocalPolicyTargetReference</a> array</div></div></div><div class="api-field__right"><div class="api-field__description">TargetRefs are the names of the AIServiceBackend resources this QuotaPolicy is being attached to.</div></div></div>
<div class="api-field"><div class="api-field__left"><code class="api-field__name">serviceQuota</code><span class="api-field__badge api-field__badge--optional">optional</span><div class="api-field__type"><div><a href="#github-com-envoyproxy-ai-gateway-api-v1alpha1-servicequotadefinition">ServiceQuotaDefinition</a></div></div></div><div class="api-field__right"><div class="api-field__description">Quota for all models served by AIServiceBackend(s). This value can be overridden for specific models using the <code class="thin">PerModelQuotas</code><br>configuration.<br>Currently, the rate limit configuration is properly set up, but the descriptor set is not being set in the Envoy Configuration</div></div></div>
<div class="api-field"><div class="api-field__left"><code class="api-field__name">perModelQuotas</code><span class="api-field__badge api-field__badge--optional">optional</span><div class="api-field__type"><div><a href="#github-com-envoyproxy-ai-gateway-api-v1alpha1-permodelquota">PerModelQuota</a> array</div></div></div><div class="api-field__right"><div class="api-field__description">PerModelQuotas specifies quota for different models served by the AIServiceBackend(s) where this<br>policy is attached.<br>When multiple QuotaPolicies define the same Model for the same AIServiceBackend,<br>the policy whose namespace/name is alphabetically first takes precedence.<br>Keys are sorted to ensure deterministic snapshot generation.</div></div></div>
<h4 class="anchor anchorTargetStickyNavbar_Vzrq" id="quotapolicystatus"><a id="github-com-envoyproxy-ai-gateway-api-v1alpha1-quotapolicystatus">QuotaPolicyStatus</a><a href="#quotapolicystatus" class="hash-link" aria-label="Direct link to quotapolicystatus" title="Direct link to quotapolicystatus" translate="no">​</a></h4>
<p><strong>Appears in:</strong></p>
\<ul\>
<li class=""><a href="#github-com-envoyproxy-ai-gateway-api-v1alpha1-quotapolicy" class="">QuotaPolicy</a></li>
</ul>
<p>QuotaPolicyStatus contains the conditions by the reconciliation result.</p>
<h5 class="anchor anchorTargetStickyNavbar_Vzrq" id="fields-65">Fields<a href="#fields-65" class="hash-link" aria-label="Direct link to Fields" title="Direct link to Fields" translate="no">​</a></h5>
<div class="api-field"><div class="api-field__left"><code class="api-field__name">conditions</code><span class="api-field__badge api-field__badge--required">required</span><div class="api-field__type"><div><a href="https://kubernetes.io/docs/reference/generated/kubernetes-api/v1.29/#condition-v1-meta">Condition</a> array</div></div></div><div class="api-field__right"><div class="api-field__description">Conditions is the list of conditions by the reconciliation result.<br>Currently, at most one condition is set.<br>Known .status.conditions.type are: <code class="thin">Accepted</code>, <code class="thin">NotAccepted</code>.</div></div></div>
<h4 class="anchor anchorTargetStickyNavbar_Vzrq" id="quotarule"><a id="github-com-envoyproxy-ai-gateway-api-v1alpha1-quotarule">QuotaRule</a><a href="#quotarule" class="hash-link" aria-label="Direct link to quotarule" title="Direct link to quotarule" translate="no">​</a></h4>
<p><strong>Appears in:</strong></p>
\<ul\>
<li class=""><a href="#github-com-envoyproxy-ai-gateway-api-v1alpha1-quotadefinition" class="">QuotaDefinition</a></li>
</ul>
<h5 class="anchor anchorTargetStickyNavbar_Vzrq" id="fields-66">Fields<a href="#fields-66" class="hash-link" aria-label="Direct link to Fields" title="Direct link to Fields" translate="no">​</a></h5>
<div class="api-field"><div class="api-field__left"><code class="api-field__name">clientSelectors</code><span class="api-field__badge api-field__badge--optional">optional</span><div class="api-field__type"><div>RateLimitSelectCondition array</div></div></div><div class="api-field__right"><div class="api-field__description">ClientSelectors holds the list of conditions to select<br>specific clients using attributes from the traffic flow.<br>All individual select conditions must hold True for this rule<br>and its limit to be applied.<br>Currently, only header types are honored.<br>If no client selectors are specified, the rule applies to all traffic of<br>the targeted AIServiceBackend.</div></div></div>
<div class="api-field"><div class="api-field__left"><code class="api-field__name">quota</code><span class="api-field__badge api-field__badge--required">required</span><div class="api-field__type"><div><a href="#github-com-envoyproxy-ai-gateway-api-v1alpha1-quotavalue">QuotaValue</a></div></div></div><div class="api-field__right"><div class="api-field__description">Quota value for given client selectors.<br>This quota is applied for traffic flows when the selectors<br>compute to True, causing the request to be counted towards the limit.<br>A response with 429 HTTP status code is sent back to the client when<br>the selected requests have exceeded the quota.</div></div></div>
<div class="api-field"><div class="api-field__left"><code class="api-field__name">shadowMode</code><span class="api-field__badge api-field__badge--optional">optional</span><div class="api-field__type"><div>boolean</div></div></div><div class="api-field__right"><div class="api-field__description">ShadowMode indicates whether this quota rule runs in shadow mode.<br>When enabled, all quota checks are performed (cache lookups,<br>counter updates, telemetry generation), but the outcome is never enforced.<br>The request always succeeds, even if the configured quota is exceeded.</div></div></div>
<h4 class="anchor anchorTargetStickyNavbar_Vzrq" id="quotavalue"><a id="github-com-envoyproxy-ai-gateway-api-v1alpha1-quotavalue">QuotaValue</a><a href="#quotavalue" class="hash-link" aria-label="Direct link to quotavalue" title="Direct link to quotavalue" translate="no">​</a></h4>
<p><strong>Appears in:</strong></p>
\<ul\>
<li class=""><a href="#github-com-envoyproxy-ai-gateway-api-v1alpha1-quotadefinition" class="">QuotaDefinition</a></li>
<li class=""><a href="#github-com-envoyproxy-ai-gateway-api-v1alpha1-quotarule" class="">QuotaRule</a></li>
<li class=""><a href="#github-com-envoyproxy-ai-gateway-api-v1alpha1-servicequotadefinition" class="">ServiceQuotaDefinition</a></li>
</ul>
<p>QuotaValue defines the quota limits using sliding window.</p>
<h5 class="anchor anchorTargetStickyNavbar_Vzrq" id="fields-67">Fields<a href="#fields-67" class="hash-link" aria-label="Direct link to Fields" title="Direct link to Fields" translate="no">​</a></h5>
<div class="api-field"><div class="api-field__left"><code class="api-field__name">limit</code><span class="api-field__badge api-field__badge--required">required</span><div class="api-field__type"><div>integer</div></div></div><div class="api-field__right"><div class="api-field__description">The limit alloted for a specified time window.</div></div></div>
<div class="api-field"><div class="api-field__left"><code class="api-field__name">duration</code><span class="api-field__badge api-field__badge--required">required</span><div class="api-field__type"><div>string</div></div></div><div class="api-field__right"><div class="api-field__description">Time window. Must be exactly one of: <code class="thin">1s</code> (1 second), <code class="thin">1m</code> (1 minute), <code class="thin">1h</code> (1 hour), or <code class="thin">1d</code> (1 day).</div></div></div>
<h4 class="anchor anchorTargetStickyNavbar_Vzrq" id="servicequotadefinition"><a id="github-com-envoyproxy-ai-gateway-api-v1alpha1-servicequotadefinition">ServiceQuotaDefinition</a><a href="#servicequotadefinition" class="hash-link" aria-label="Direct link to servicequotadefinition" title="Direct link to servicequotadefinition" translate="no">​</a></h4>
<p><strong>Appears in:</strong></p>
\<ul\>
<li class=""><a href="#github-com-envoyproxy-ai-gateway-api-v1alpha1-quotapolicyspec" class="">QuotaPolicySpec</a></li>
</ul>
<h5 class="anchor anchorTargetStickyNavbar_Vzrq" id="fields-68">Fields<a href="#fields-68" class="hash-link" aria-label="Direct link to Fields" title="Direct link to Fields" translate="no">​</a></h5>
<div class="api-field"><div class="api-field__left"><code class="api-field__name">costExpression</code><span class="api-field__badge api-field__badge--optional">optional</span><div class="api-field__type"><div>string</div></div></div><div class="api-field__right"><div class="api-field__description">CostExpression specifies a CEL expression for computing the quota burndown of the LLM-related request.<br>If no expression is specified the <code class="thin">total_tokens</code> value is used.<br>For example:<br> <code class="thin">input_tokens + cached_input_tokens + output_tokens</code></div></div></div>
<div class="api-field"><div class="api-field__left"><code class="api-field__name">quota</code><span class="api-field__badge api-field__badge--required">required</span><div class="api-field__type"><div><a href="#github-com-envoyproxy-ai-gateway-api-v1alpha1-quotavalue">QuotaValue</a></div></div></div><div class="api-field__right"><div class="api-field__description">Quota value applicable to all requests.<br>A response with 429 HTTP status code is sent back to the client when<br>the selected requests have exceeded the quota.</div></div></div>
<h4 class="anchor anchorTargetStickyNavbar_Vzrq" id="toolcall"><a id="github-com-envoyproxy-ai-gateway-api-v1alpha1-toolcall">ToolCall</a><a href="#toolcall" class="hash-link" aria-label="Direct link to toolcall" title="Direct link to toolcall" translate="no">​</a></h4>
<p><strong>Appears in:</strong></p>
\<ul\>
<li class=""><a href="#github-com-envoyproxy-ai-gateway-api-v1alpha1-mcpauthorizationtarget" class="">MCPAuthorizationTarget</a></li>
</ul>
<p>ToolCall represents a tool call in the MCP authorization target.</p>
<h5 class="anchor anchorTargetStickyNavbar_Vzrq" id="fields-69">Fields<a href="#fields-69" class="hash-link" aria-label="Direct link to Fields" title="Direct link to Fields" translate="no">​</a></h5>
<div class="api-field"><div class="api-field__left"><code class="api-field__name">backend</code><span class="api-field__badge api-field__badge--required">required</span><div class="api-field__type"><div>string</div></div></div><div class="api-field__right"><div class="api-field__description">Backend is the name of the backend this tool belongs to.</div></div></div>
<div class="api-field"><div class="api-field__left"><code class="api-field__name">tool</code><span class="api-field__badge api-field__badge--required">required</span><div class="api-field__type"><div>string</div></div></div><div class="api-field__right"><div class="api-field__description">Tool is the name of the tool.</div></div></div>
<h4 class="anchor anchorTargetStickyNavbar_Vzrq" id="versionedapischema"><a id="github-com-envoyproxy-ai-gateway-api-v1alpha1-versionedapischema">VersionedAPISchema</a><a href="#versionedapischema" class="hash-link" aria-label="Direct link to versionedapischema" title="Direct link to versionedapischema" translate="no">​</a></h4>
<p><strong>Appears in:</strong></p>
\<ul\>
<li class=""><a href="#github-com-envoyproxy-ai-gateway-api-v1alpha1-aiservicebackendspec" class="">AIServiceBackendSpec</a></li>
</ul>
<p>VersionedAPISchema defines the API schema of either AIGatewayRoute (the input) or AIServiceBackend (the output).</p>
<p>This allows the ai-gateway to understand the input and perform the necessary transformation
depending on the API schema pair (input, output).</p>
<p>Note that this is vendor specific, and the stability of the API schema is not guaranteed by
the ai-gateway, but by the vendor via proper versioning.</p>
<h5 class="anchor anchorTargetStickyNavbar_Vzrq" id="fields-70">Fields<a href="#fields-70" class="hash-link" aria-label="Direct link to Fields" title="Direct link to Fields" translate="no">​</a></h5>
<div class="api-field"><div class="api-field__left"><code class="api-field__name">name</code><span class="api-field__badge api-field__badge--required">required</span><div class="api-field__type"><div><a href="#github-com-envoyproxy-ai-gateway-api-v1alpha1-apischema">APISchema</a></div></div></div><div class="api-field__right"><div class="api-field__description">Name is the name of the API schema of the AIGatewayRoute or AIServiceBackend.</div></div></div>
