<div class="api-field"><div class="api-field__left"><code class="api-field__name">version</code><span class="api-field__badge api-field__badge--optional">optional</span><div class="api-field__type"><div>string</div></div></div><div class="api-field__right"><div class="api-field__description">Version is the version of the API schema.<br>When the name is set to AzureOpenAI, this version maps to <code class="thin">API Version</code> in the<br>Azure OpenAI API documentation (https://learn.microsoft.com/en-us/azure/ai-services/openai/reference#rest-api-versioning).<br>This field is ignored for OpenAI, AWSBedrock, GCPVertexAI, and Anthropic.<br>For OpenAI and Anthropic, use prefix to configure custom request paths.<br>See https://aigateway.envoyproxy.io/docs/capabilities/llm-integrations/supported-providers for details.</div></div></div>
<div class="api-field"><div class="api-field__left"><code class="api-field__name">prefix</code><span class="api-field__badge api-field__badge--optional">optional</span><div class="api-field__type"><div>string</div></div></div><div class="api-field__right"><div class="api-field__description">Prefix is the prefix for the API.<br>When the name is set to <code class="thin">OpenAI</code>, <code class="thin">chat completions</code> API endpoint will be <code class="thin">$\{this_field\}/chat/completions</code>.<br>When the name is set to <code class="thin">Anthropic</code>, the <code class="thin">messages</code> API endpoint will be <code class="thin">$\{this_field\}/messages</code>.<br>It can be with or without a leading slash (<code class="thin">/</code>).<br>This field is ignored for AWSAnthropic and GCPAnthropic.<br>This is especially useful when routing to a backend that has an OpenAI or Anthropic compatible API but has a different<br>prefix. For example, Gemini OpenAI compatible API (https://ai.google.dev/gemini-api/docs/openai) uses<br><code class="thin">/v1beta/openai</code> prefix. Another example is that Cohere AI (https://docs.cohere.com/v2/docs/compatibility-api)<br>uses <code class="thin">/compatibility/v1</code> prefix. On the other hand, DeepSeek (https://api-docs.deepseek.com/) doesn't<br>use prefix, so you can leave this field unset.<br>See https://aigateway.envoyproxy.io/docs/capabilities/llm-integrations/supported-providers for details.</div></div></div>
<h2 class="anchor anchorTargetStickyNavbar_Vzrq" id="aigatewayenvoyproxyiov1beta1">aigateway.envoyproxy.io/v1beta1<a href="#aigatewayenvoyproxyiov1beta1" class="hash-link" aria-label="Direct link to aigateway.envoyproxy.io/v1beta1" title="Direct link to aigateway.envoyproxy.io/v1beta1" translate="no">​</a></h2>
<p>Package v1beta1 contains API schema definitions for the aigateway.envoyproxy.io
API group. This is the beta version of the API, preferred over v1alpha1 for new resources.</p>
<h2 class="anchor anchorTargetStickyNavbar_Vzrq" id="resource-kinds-1">Resource Kinds<a href="#resource-kinds-1" class="hash-link" aria-label="Direct link to Resource Kinds" title="Direct link to Resource Kinds" translate="no">​</a></h2>
<h3 class="anchor anchorTargetStickyNavbar_Vzrq" id="available-kinds-1">Available Kinds<a href="#available-kinds-1" class="hash-link" aria-label="Direct link to Available Kinds" title="Direct link to Available Kinds" translate="no">​</a></h3>
\<ul\>
<li class=""><a href="#github-com-envoyproxy-ai-gateway-api-v1beta1-aigatewayroute" class="">AIGatewayRoute</a></li>
<li class=""><a href="#github-com-envoyproxy-ai-gateway-api-v1beta1-aigatewayroutelist" class="">AIGatewayRouteList</a></li>
<li class=""><a href="#github-com-envoyproxy-ai-gateway-api-v1beta1-aiservicebackend" class="">AIServiceBackend</a></li>
<li class=""><a href="#github-com-envoyproxy-ai-gateway-api-v1beta1-aiservicebackendlist" class="">AIServiceBackendList</a></li>
<li class=""><a href="#github-com-envoyproxy-ai-gateway-api-v1beta1-backendsecuritypolicy" class="">BackendSecurityPolicy</a></li>
<li class=""><a href="#github-com-envoyproxy-ai-gateway-api-v1beta1-backendsecuritypolicylist" class="">BackendSecurityPolicyList</a></li>
<li class=""><a href="#github-com-envoyproxy-ai-gateway-api-v1beta1-gatewayconfig" class="">GatewayConfig</a></li>
<li class=""><a href="#github-com-envoyproxy-ai-gateway-api-v1beta1-gatewayconfiglist" class="">GatewayConfigList</a></li>
<li class=""><a href="#github-com-envoyproxy-ai-gateway-api-v1beta1-mcproute" class="">MCPRoute</a></li>
<li class=""><a href="#github-com-envoyproxy-ai-gateway-api-v1beta1-mcproutelist" class="">MCPRouteList</a></li>
</ul>
<h3 class="anchor anchorTargetStickyNavbar_Vzrq" id="kind-definitions-1">Kind Definitions<a href="#kind-definitions-1" class="hash-link" aria-label="Direct link to Kind Definitions" title="Direct link to Kind Definitions" translate="no">​</a></h3>
<h4 class="anchor anchorTargetStickyNavbar_Vzrq" id="aigatewayroute-1"><a id="github-com-envoyproxy-ai-gateway-api-v1beta1-aigatewayroute">AIGatewayRoute</a><a href="#aigatewayroute-1" class="hash-link" aria-label="Direct link to aigatewayroute-1" title="Direct link to aigatewayroute-1" translate="no">​</a></h4>
<p><strong>Appears in:</strong></p>
\<ul\>
<li class=""><a href="#github-com-envoyproxy-ai-gateway-api-v1beta1-aigatewayroutelist" class="">AIGatewayRouteList</a></li>
</ul>
<p>AIGatewayRoute combines multiple AIServiceBackends and attaching them to Gateway(s) resources.</p>
<p>This serves as a way to define a "unified" AI API for a Gateway which allows downstream
clients to use a single schema API to interact with multiple AI backends.</p>
<p>Envoy AI Gateway will generate the following k8s resources corresponding to the AIGatewayRoute:</p>
\<ul\>
<li class="">HTTPRoute of the Gateway API as a top-level resource to bind all backends.
The name of the HTTPRoute is the same as the AIGatewayRoute.</li>
<li class="">HTTPRouteFilter of the Envoy Gateway API per namespace for automatic hostname rewrite.
The name of the HTTPRouteFilter is <code>ai-eg-host-rewrite-${AIGatewayRoute.Name}</code>.</li>
</ul>
<p>All of these resources are created in the same namespace as the AIGatewayRoute. Note that this is the implementation
detail subject to change. If you want to customize the default behavior of the Envoy AI Gateway, you can use these
resources as a reference and create your own resources. Alternatively, you can use EnvoyPatchPolicy API of the Envoy
Gateway to patch the generated resources. For example, you can configure the retry fallback behavior by attaching
BackendTrafficPolicy API of Envoy Gateway to the generated HTTPRoute.</p>
<h5 class="anchor anchorTargetStickyNavbar_Vzrq" id="fields-71">Fields<a href="#fields-71" class="hash-link" aria-label="Direct link to Fields" title="Direct link to Fields" translate="no">​</a></h5>
<div class="api-field"><div class="api-field__left"><code class="api-field__name">apiVersion</code><span class="api-field__badge api-field__badge--required">required</span><div class="api-field__type"><div>String</div></div></div><div class="api-field__right"><div class="api-field__description">We are on version <code>aigateway.envoyproxy.io/v1beta1</code> of the API.</div></div></div>
<div class="api-field"><div class="api-field__left"><code class="api-field__name">kind</code><span class="api-field__badge api-field__badge--required">required</span><div class="api-field__type"><div>String</div></div></div><div class="api-field__right"><div class="api-field__description">This is a <code>AIGatewayRoute</code> resource</div></div></div>
<div class="api-field"><div class="api-field__left"><code class="api-field__name">metadata</code><span class="api-field__badge api-field__badge--required">required</span><div class="api-field__type"><div><a href="https://kubernetes.io/docs/reference/generated/kubernetes-api/v1.29/#objectmeta-v1-meta">ObjectMeta</a></div></div></div><div class="api-field__right"><div class="api-field__description">Refer to Kubernetes API documentation for fields of <code class="thin">metadata</code>.</div></div></div>
<div class="api-field"><div class="api-field__left"><code class="api-field__name">spec</code><span class="api-field__badge api-field__badge--required">required</span><div class="api-field__type"><div><a href="#github-com-envoyproxy-ai-gateway-api-v1beta1-aigatewayroutespec">AIGatewayRouteSpec</a></div></div></div><div class="api-field__right"><div class="api-field__description">Spec defines the details of the AIGatewayRoute.</div></div></div>
<div class="api-field"><div class="api-field__left"><code class="api-field__name">status</code><span class="api-field__badge api-field__badge--required">required</span><div class="api-field__type"><div><a href="#github-com-envoyproxy-ai-gateway-api-v1beta1-aigatewayroutestatus">AIGatewayRouteStatus</a></div></div></div><div class="api-field__right"><div class="api-field__description">Status defines the status details of the AIGatewayRoute.</div></div></div>
<h4 class="anchor anchorTargetStickyNavbar_Vzrq" id="aigatewayroutelist-1"><a id="github-com-envoyproxy-ai-gateway-api-v1beta1-aigatewayroutelist">AIGatewayRouteList</a><a href="#aigatewayroutelist-1" class="hash-link" aria-label="Direct link to aigatewayroutelist-1" title="Direct link to aigatewayroutelist-1" translate="no">​</a></h4>
<p>AIGatewayRouteList contains a list of AIGatewayRoute.</p>
<h5 class="anchor anchorTargetStickyNavbar_Vzrq" id="fields-72">Fields<a href="#fields-72" class="hash-link" aria-label="Direct link to Fields" title="Direct link to Fields" translate="no">​</a></h5>
<div class="api-field"><div class="api-field__left"><code class="api-field__name">apiVersion</code><span class="api-field__badge api-field__badge--required">required</span><div class="api-field__type"><div>String</div></div></div><div class="api-field__right"><div class="api-field__description">We are on version <code>aigateway.envoyproxy.io/v1beta1</code> of the API.</div></div></div>
<div class="api-field"><div class="api-field__left"><code class="api-field__name">kind</code><span class="api-field__badge api-field__badge--required">required</span><div class="api-field__type"><div>String</div></div></div><div class="api-field__right"><div class="api-field__description">This is a <code>AIGatewayRouteList</code> resource</div></div></div>

<h4 class="anchor anchorTargetStickyNavbar_Vzrq" id="aiservicebackend-1"><a id="github-com-envoyproxy-ai-gateway-api-v1beta1-aiservicebackend">AIServiceBackend</a><a href="#aiservicebackend-1" class="hash-link" aria-label="Direct link to aiservicebackend-1" title="Direct link to aiservicebackend-1" translate="no">​</a></h4>
<p><strong>Appears in:</strong></p>
\<ul\>
<li class=""><a href="#github-com-envoyproxy-ai-gateway-api-v1beta1-aiservicebackendlist" class="">AIServiceBackendList</a></li>
</ul>
<p>AIServiceBackend is a resource that represents a single backend for AIGatewayRoute.
A backend is a service that handles traffic with a concrete API specification.</p>
<p>A AIServiceBackend is "attached" to a Backend which is either a k8s Service or a Backend resource of the Envoy Gateway.</p>
<p>When a backend with an attached AIServiceBackend is used as a routing target in the AIGatewayRoute (more precisely, the
HTTPRouteSpec defined in the AIGatewayRoute), the ai-gateway will generate the necessary configuration to do
the backend specific logic in the final HTTPRoute.</p>
<h5 class="anchor anchorTargetStickyNavbar_Vzrq" id="fields-73">Fields<a href="#fields-73" class="hash-link" aria-label="Direct link to Fields" title="Direct link to Fields" translate="no">​</a></h5>

<h4 class="anchor anchorTargetStickyNavbar_Vzrq" id="gatewayconfig-1"><a id="github-com-envoyproxy-ai-gateway-api-v1beta1-gatewayconfig">GatewayConfig</a><a href="#gatewayconfig-1" class="hash-link" aria-label="Direct link to gatewayconfig-1" title="Direct link to gatewayconfig-1" translate="no">​</a></h4>
<p><strong>Appears in:</strong></p>
\<ul\>
<li class=""><a href="#github-com-envoyproxy-ai-gateway-api-v1beta1-gatewayconfiglist" class="">GatewayConfigList</a></li>
</ul>
<p>GatewayConfig provides configuration for the AI Gateway external processor
container that is deployed alongside the Gateway.</p>
<p>A GatewayConfig is referenced by a Gateway via the annotation
"aigateway.envoyproxy.io/gateway-config". The GatewayConfig must be in the
same namespace as the Gateway that references it.</p>
<p>This allows gateway-level configuration of the external processor, including
environment variables (e.g., for tracing configuration) and resource requirements.</p>
<p>Multiple Gateways can reference the same GatewayConfig to share configuration.</p>
<p>Environment Variable Precedence:
When merging environment variables, the following precedence applies (highest to lowest):</p>
\<ol\>
<li class="">GatewayConfig.Spec.ExtProc.Kubernetes.Env (this resource)</li>
<li class="">Global controller flags (extProcExtraEnvVars)</li>
</ol>
<p>If the same environment variable name exists in both sources, the GatewayConfig
value takes precedence.</p>
<h5 class="anchor anchorTargetStickyNavbar_Vzrq" id="fields-77">Fields<a href="#fields-77" class="hash-link" aria-label="Direct link to Fields" title="Direct link to Fields" translate="no">​</a></h5>

<h4 class="anchor anchorTargetStickyNavbar_Vzrq" id="mcproute-1"><a id="github-com-envoyproxy-ai-gateway-api-v1beta1-mcproute">MCPRoute</a><a href="#mcproute-1" class="hash-link" aria-label="Direct link to mcproute-1" title="Direct link to mcproute-1" translate="no">​</a></h4>
<p><strong>Appears in:</strong></p>
\<ul\>
<li class=""><a href="#github-com-envoyproxy-ai-gateway-api-v1beta1-mcproutelist" class="">MCPRouteList</a></li>
</ul>
<p>MCPRoute defines how to route MCP requests to the backend MCP servers.</p>
<p>This serves as a way to define a "unified" AI API for a Gateway which allows downstream
clients to use a single schema API to interact with multiple MCP backends.</p>

<h3 class="anchor anchorTargetStickyNavbar_Vzrq" id="type-definitions-1">Type Definitions<a href="#type-definitions-1" class="hash-link" aria-label="Direct link to Type Definitions" title="Direct link to Type Definitions" translate="no">​</a></h3>
<h4 class="anchor anchorTargetStickyNavbar_Vzrq" id="aigatewayrouterule-1"><a id="github-com-envoyproxy-ai-gateway-api-v1beta1-aigatewayrouterule">AIGatewayRouteRule</a><a href="#aigatewayrouterule-1" class="hash-link" aria-label="Direct link to aigatewayrouterule-1" title="Direct link to aigatewayrouterule-1" translate="no">​</a></h4>
<p><strong>Appears in:</strong></p>
\<ul\>
<li class=""><a href="#github-com-envoyproxy-ai-gateway-api-v1beta1-aigatewayroutespec" class="">AIGatewayRouteSpec</a></li>
</ul>
<p>AIGatewayRouteRule is a rule that defines the routing behavior of the AIGatewayRoute.</p>
<h5 class="anchor anchorTargetStickyNavbar_Vzrq" id="fields-81">Fields<a href="#fields-81" class="hash-link" aria-label="Direct link to Fields" title="Direct link to Fields" translate="no">​</a></h5>
<div class="api-field"><div class="api-field__left"><code class="api-field__name">name</code><span class="api-field__badge api-field__badge--optional">optional</span><div class="api-field__type"><div><a href="#sigs-k8s-io-gateway-api-apis-v1-sectionname">SectionName</a></div></div></div><div class="api-field__right"><div class="api-field__description">Name is the name of the route rule. This name must be unique within the route.<br>When specified, it is copied to the generated HTTPRoute rule name.</div></div></div>
<div class="api-field"><div class="api-field__left"><code class="api-field__name">backendRefs</code><span class="api-field__badge api-field__badge--optional">optional</span><div class="api-field__type"><div><a href="#github-com-envoyproxy-ai-gateway-api-v1beta1-aigatewayrouterulebackendref">AIGatewayRouteRuleBackendRef</a> array</div></div></div><div class="api-field__right"><div class="api-field__description">BackendRefs is the list of backends that this rule will route the traffic to.<br>Each backend can have a weight that determines the traffic distribution.<br>The namespace of each backend defaults to the same namespace as the AIGatewayRoute when not specified.<br>Cross-namespace references are supported by specifying the namespace field.<br>When a namespace different than the AIGatewayRoute's namespace is specified,<br>a ReferenceGrant object is required in the referent namespace to allow that<br>namespace's owner to accept the reference.<br>BackendRefs can reference either AIServiceBackend resources (default) or InferencePool resources<br>from the Gateway API Inference Extension. When referencing InferencePool resources:<br>- Only one InferencePool backend is allowed per rule<br>- Cannot mix InferencePool with AIServiceBackend references in the same rule<br>- Fallback behavior is handled by the InferencePool's endpoint picker<br>For AIServiceBackend references, you can achieve fallback behavior by configuring multiple backends<br>combined with the BackendTrafficPolicy of Envoy Gateway.<br>Please refer to https://gateway.envoyproxy.io/docs/tasks/traffic/failover/ as well as<br>https://gateway.envoyproxy.io/docs/tasks/traffic/retry/.</div></div></div>
<div class="api-field"><div class="api-field__left"><code class="api-field__name">matches</code><span class="api-field__badge api-field__badge--optional">optional</span><div class="api-field__type"><div><a href="#github-com-envoyproxy-ai-gateway-api-v1beta1-aigatewayrouterulematch">AIGatewayRouteRuleMatch</a> array</div></div></div><div class="api-field__right"><div class="api-field__description">Matches is the list of AIGatewayRouteMatch that this rule will match the traffic to.<br>This is a subset of the HTTPRouteMatch in the Gateway API. See for the details:<br>https://gateway-api.sigs.k8s.io/reference/spec/#gateway.networking.k8s.io%2fv1.HTTPRouteMatch</div></div></div>
<div class="api-field"><div class="api-field__left"><code class="api-field__name">timeouts</code><span class="api-field__badge api-field__badge--optional">optional</span><div class="api-field__type"><div><a href="https://gateway-api.sigs.k8s.io/reference/spec/?h=httproutetimeouts#httproutetimeouts">HTTPRouteTimeouts</a></div></div></div><div class="api-field__right"><div class="api-field__description">Timeouts defines the timeouts that can be configured for an HTTP request.<br>If this field is not set, or the timeout.requestTimeout is nil, Envoy AI Gateway defaults to<br>set 60s for the request timeout as opposed to 15s of the Envoy Gateway's default value.<br>For streaming responses (like chat completions with stream=true), consider setting<br>longer timeouts as the response may take time until the completion. Timeouts.Request<br>acts as the maximum total time the gateway will wait for the entire response,<br>including all streamed chunks.</div></div></div>
<div class="api-field"><div class="api-field__left"><code class="api-field__name">streamIdleTimeout</code><span class="api-field__badge api-field__badge--optional">optional</span><div class="api-field__type"><div><a href="https://gateway-api.sigs.k8s.io/reference/spec/#gateway.networking.k8s.io/v1.Duration">Duration</a></div></div></div><div class="api-field__right"><div class="api-field__description">StreamIdleTimeout is the maximum time Envoy will wait without receiving any bytes from the upstream.<br>If the timer fires before the first response byte arrives, Envoy resets the upstream stream and a<br>retry policy can fall over to the next backend. If it fires mid-stream after<br>bytes have already arrived, the stream is cut and the client receives a 504.<br>The AI Gateway extension server sets route.retry_policy.per_try_idle_timeout to this value on<br>every xDS route generated from this rule before it is sent to the data plane.<br>Pair this field with Timeouts.Request, which acts as the overall deadline.<br>If this field is not set, no per-try idle timeout is applied.</div></div></div>
<div class="api-field"><div class="api-field__left"><code class="api-field__name">modelsOwnedBy</code><span class="api-field__badge api-field__badge--optional">optional</span><div class="api-field__type"><div>string</div></div></div><div class="api-field__right"><div class="api-field__default">Envoy AI Gateway</div><div class="api-field__description">ModelsOwnedBy represents the owner of the running models serving by the backends,<br>which will be exported as the field of <code class="thin">OwnedBy</code> in openai-compatible API <code class="thin">/models</code>.<br>This is used only when this rule contains <code class="thin">x-ai-eg-model</code> in its header matching<br>where the header value will be recognized as a <code class="thin">model</code> in <code class="thin">/models</code> endpoint.<br>All the matched models will share the same owner.<br>Default to <code class="thin">Envoy AI Gateway</code> if not set.</div></div></div>
<div class="api-field"><div class="api-field__left"><code class="api-field__name">modelsCreatedAt</code><span class="api-field__badge api-field__badge--optional">optional</span><div class="api-field__type"><div><a href="https://kubernetes.io/docs/reference/generated/kubernetes-api/v1.29/#time-v1-meta">Time</a></div></div></div><div class="api-field__right"><div class="api-field__description">ModelsCreatedAt represents the creation timestamp of the running models serving by the backends,<br>which will be exported as the field of <code class="thin">Created</code> in openai-compatible API <code class="thin">/models</code>.<br>It follows the format of RFC 3339, for example <code class="thin">2024-05-21T10:00:00Z</code>.<br>This is used only when this rule contains <code class="thin">x-ai-eg-model</code> in its header matching<br>where the header value will be recognized as a <code class="thin">model</code> in <code class="thin">/models</code> endpoint.<br>All the matched models will share the same creation time.<br>Default to the creation timestamp of the AIGatewayRoute if not set.</div></div></div>
<div class="api-field"><div class="api-field__left"><code class="api-field__name">excludeFromModelsEndpoint</code><span class="api-field__badge api-field__badge--optional">optional</span><div class="api-field__type"><div>boolean</div></div></div><div class="api-field__right"><div class="api-field__description">ExcludeFromModelsEndpoint prevents models matched by this rule from being returned by the<br>OpenAI-compatible <code class="thin">/models</code> endpoint. Requests still match this rule and apply its<br>configured transformations, including model name overrides.<br>This is useful for internal or backwards-compatible aliases, as well as duplicate model<br>definitions with different schemas.</div></div></div>
<h4 class="anchor anchorTargetStickyNavbar_Vzrq" id="aigatewayrouterulebackendref-1"><a id="github-com-envoyproxy-ai-gateway-api-v1beta1-aigatewayrouterulebackendref">AIGatewayRouteRuleBackendRef</a><a href="#aigatewayrouterulebackendref-1" class="hash-link" aria-label="Direct link to aigatewayrouterulebackendref-1" title="Direct link to aigatewayrouterulebackendref-1" translate="no">​</a></h4>
<p><strong>Appears in:</strong></p>
\<ul\>
<li class=""><a href="#github-com-envoyproxy-ai-gateway-api-v1beta1-aigatewayrouterule" class="">AIGatewayRouteRule</a></li>
</ul>
<p>AIGatewayRouteRuleBackendRef is a reference to a backend with a weight.
It can reference either an AIServiceBackend or an InferencePool resource.</p>
<h5 class="anchor anchorTargetStickyNavbar_Vzrq" id="fields-82">Fields<a href="#fields-82" class="hash-link" aria-label="Direct link to Fields" title="Direct link to Fields" translate="no">​</a></h5>
<div class="api-field"><div class="api-field__left"><code class="api-field__name">name</code><span class="api-field__badge api-field__badge--required">required</span><div class="api-field__type"><div>string</div></div></div><div class="api-field__right"><div class="api-field__description">Name is the name of the backend resource.<br>When Group and Kind are not specified, this refers to an AIServiceBackend.<br>When Group and Kind are specified, this refers to the resource of the specified type.</div></div></div>
<div class="api-field"><div class="api-field__left"><code class="api-field__name">namespace</code><span class="api-field__badge api-field__badge--optional">optional</span><div class="api-field__type"><div><a href="https://gateway-api.sigs.k8s.io/reference/spec/?h=httproutetimeouts#namespace">Namespace</a></div></div></div><div class="api-field__right"><div class="api-field__description">Namespace is the namespace of the backend resource.<br>When unspecified (or empty string), this refers to the local namespace of the AIGatewayRoute.<br>Note that when a namespace different than the local namespace is specified,<br>a ReferenceGrant object is required in the referent namespace to allow that<br>namespace's owner to accept the reference. See the ReferenceGrant<br>documentation for details.</div></div></div>
<div class="api-field"><div class="api-field__left"><code class="api-field__name">group</code><span class="api-field__badge api-field__badge--optional">optional</span><div class="api-field__type"><div>string</div></div></div><div class="api-field__right"><div class="api-field__description">Group is the group of the backend resource.<br>When not specified, defaults to aigateway.envoyproxy.io (AIServiceBackend).<br>Currently, only <code class="thin">inference.networking.k8s.io</code> is supported for InferencePool resources.</div></div></div>
<div class="api-field"><div class="api-field__left"><code class="api-field__name">kind</code><span class="api-field__badge api-field__badge--optional">optional</span><div class="api-field__type"><div>string</div></div></div><div class="api-field__right"><div class="api-field__description">Kind is the kind of the backend resource.<br>When not specified, defaults to AIServiceBackend.<br>Currently, only <code class="thin">InferencePool</code> is supported when Group is specified.</div></div></div>
<div class="api-field"><div class="api-field__left"><code class="api-field__name">modelNameOverride</code><span class="api-field__badge api-field__badge--optional">optional</span><div class="api-field__type"><div>string</div></div></div><div class="api-field__right"><div class="api-field__description">Name of the model in the backend. If provided this will override the name provided in the request.<br>This field is ignored when referencing InferencePool resources.</div></div></div>
<div class="api-field"><div class="api-field__left"><code class="api-field__name">headerMutation</code><span class="api-field__badge api-field__badge--optional">optional</span><div class="api-field__type"><div><a href="#github-com-envoyproxy-ai-gateway-api-v1beta1-httpheadermutation">HTTPHeaderMutation</a></div></div></div><div class="api-field__right"><div class="api-field__description">HeaderMutation defines the request header mutation to be applied to this backend.<br>When both route-level and backend-level HeaderMutation are defined,<br>route-level takes precedence over backend-level for conflicting operations.<br>This field is ignored when referencing InferencePool resources.</div></div></div>
<div class="api-field"><div class="api-field__left"><code class="api-field__name">bodyMutation</code><span class="api-field__badge api-field__badge--optional">optional</span><div class="api-field__type"><div><a href="#github-com-envoyproxy-ai-gateway-api-v1beta1-httpbodymutation">HTTPBodyMutation</a></div></div></div><div class="api-field__right"><div class="api-field__description">BodyMutation defines the request body mutation to be applied to this backend.<br>This allows modification of JSON fields in the request body before sending to the backend.<br>When both route-level and backend-level BodyMutation are defined,<br>route-level takes precedence over backend-level for conflicting operations.<br>This field is ignored when referencing InferencePool resources.</div></div></div>
<div class="api-field"><div class="api-field__left"><code class="api-field__name">weight</code><span class="api-field__badge api-field__badge--optional">optional</span><div class="api-field__type"><div>integer</div></div></div><div class="api-field__right"><div class="api-field__default">1</div><div class="api-field__description">Weight is the weight of the backend. This is exactly the same as the weight in<br>the BackendRef in the Gateway API. See for the details:<br>https://gateway-api.sigs.k8s.io/reference/spec/#gateway.networking.k8s.io%2fv1.BackendRef<br>Default is 1.</div></div></div>
<div class="api-field"><div class="api-field__left"><code class="api-field__name">priority</code><span class="api-field__badge api-field__badge--optional">optional</span><div class="api-field__type"><div>integer</div></div></div><div class="api-field__right"><div class="api-field__default">0</div><div class="api-field__description">Priority is the priority of the backend. This sets the priority on the underlying endpoints.<br>See: https://www.envoyproxy.io/docs/envoy/latest/intro/arch_overview/upstream/load_balancing/priority<br>Note: This will override the <code class="thin">faillback</code> property of the underlying Envoy Gateway Backend<br>This field is ignored when referencing InferencePool resources.<br>Default is 0.</div></div></div>
<h4 class="anchor anchorTargetStickyNavbar_Vzrq" id="aigatewayrouterulematch-1"><a id="github-com-envoyproxy-ai-gateway-api-v1beta1-aigatewayrouterulematch">AIGatewayRouteRuleMatch</a><a href="#aigatewayrouterulematch-1" class="hash-link" aria-label="Direct link to aigatewayrouterulematch-1" title="Direct link to aigatewayrouterulematch-1" translate="no">​</a></h4>
<p><strong>Appears in:</strong></p>
\<ul\>
<li class=""><a href="#github-com-envoyproxy-ai-gateway-api-v1beta1-aigatewayrouterule" class="">AIGatewayRouteRule</a></li>
</ul>
<h5 class="anchor anchorTargetStickyNavbar_Vzrq" id="fields-83">Fields<a href="#fields-83" class="hash-link" aria-label="Direct link to Fields" title="Direct link to Fields" translate="no">​</a></h5>
<div class="api-field"><div class="api-field__left"><code class="api-field__name">headers</code><span class="api-field__badge api-field__badge--optional">optional</span><div class="api-field__type"><div><a href="https://gateway-api.sigs.k8s.io/reference/spec/?h=httproutetimeouts#httpheadermatch">HTTPHeaderMatch</a> array</div></div></div><div class="api-field__right"><div class="api-field__description">Headers specifies HTTP request header matchers. See HeaderMatch in the Gateway API for the details:<br>https://gateway-api.sigs.k8s.io/reference/spec/#gateway.networking.k8s.io%2fv1.HTTPHeaderMatch</div></div></div>
<h4 class="anchor anchorTargetStickyNavbar_Vzrq" id="aigatewayroutespec-1"><a id="github-com-envoyproxy-ai-gateway-api-v1beta1-aigatewayroutespec">AIGatewayRouteSpec</a><a href="#aigatewayroutespec-1" class="hash-link" aria-label="Direct link to aigatewayroutespec-1" title="Direct link to aigatewayroutespec-1" translate="no">​</a></h4>
<p><strong>Appears in:</strong></p>
\<ul\>
<li class=""><a href="#github-com-envoyproxy-ai-gateway-api-v1beta1-aigatewayroute" class="">AIGatewayRoute</a></li>
</ul>
<p>AIGatewayRouteSpec details the AIGatewayRoute configuration.</p>
<h5 class="anchor anchorTargetStickyNavbar_Vzrq" id="fields-84">Fields<a href="#fields-84" class="hash-link" aria-label="Direct link to Fields" title="Direct link to Fields" translate="no">​</a></h5>
<div class="api-field"><div class="api-field__left"><code class="api-field__name">parentRefs</code><span class="api-field__badge api-field__badge--optional">optional</span><div class="api-field__type"><div><a href="https://gateway-api.sigs.k8s.io/reference/spec/?h=httproutetimeouts#parentreference">ParentReference</a> array</div></div></div><div class="api-field__right"><div class="api-field__description">ParentRefs are the names of the Gateway resources this AIGatewayRoute is being attached to.<br>Currently, each reference's Kind must be Gateway.</div></div></div>
<div class="api-field"><div class="api-field__left"><code class="api-field__name">hostnames</code><span class="api-field__badge api-field__badge--optional">optional</span><div class="api-field__type"><div>Hostname array</div></div></div><div class="api-field__right"><div class="api-field__description">Hostnames is a list of hostnames matched against the HTTP Host header to select an AIGatewayRoute<br>used to process the request. This is equivalent to the Hostnames field in the Gateway API HTTPRouteSpec.<br>When specified, the generated HTTPRoute will include these hostnames for hostname-based filtering.<br>See https://gateway-api.sigs.k8s.io/reference/spec/#gateway.networking.k8s.io%2fv1.HTTPRouteSpec<br>for the details of the Hostnames field in the Gateway API.</div></div></div>
<div class="api-field"><div class="api-field__left"><code class="api-field__name">rules</code><span class="api-field__badge api-field__badge--required">required</span><div class="api-field__type"><div><a href="#github-com-envoyproxy-ai-gateway-api-v1beta1-aigatewayrouterule">AIGatewayRouteRule</a> array</div></div></div><div class="api-field__right"><div class="api-field__description">Rules is the list of AIGatewayRouteRule that this AIGatewayRoute will match the traffic to.<br>Each rule is a subset of the HTTPRoute in the Gateway API (https://gateway-api.sigs.k8s.io/api-types/httproute/).<br>AI Gateway controller will generate a HTTPRoute based on the configuration given here with the additional<br>modifications to achieve the necessary jobs, notably inserting the AI Gateway filter responsible for<br>the transformation of the request and response, etc.<br>In the matching conditions in the AIGatewayRouteRule, <code class="thin">x-ai-eg-model</code> header is available<br>if we want to describe the routing behavior based on the model name. The model name is extracted<br>from the request content before the routing decision.<br>How multiple rules are matched is the same as the Gateway API. See for the details:<br>https://gateway-api.sigs.k8s.io/reference/spec/#gateway.networking.k8s.io%2fv1.HTTPRoute<br>At most 15 rules are allowed per AIGatewayRoute, corresponding to the Gateway API's limit on<br>HTTPRoute.spec.rules (one slot is reserved for a controller-injected catch-all rule). To<br>configure more rules on the same Gateway, split them across multiple AIGatewayRoute resources.</div></div></div>
<div class="api-field"><div class="api-field__left"><code class="api-field__name">llmRequestCosts</code><span class="api-field__badge api-field__badge--optional">optional</span><div class="api-field__type"><div><a href="#github-com-envoyproxy-ai-gateway-api-v1beta1-llmrequestcost">LLMRequestCost</a> array</div></div></div><div class="api-field__right"><div class="api-field__description">LLMRequestCosts specifies how to capture the cost of the LLM-related request, notably the token usage.<br>The AI Gateway filter will capture each specified number and store it in the Envoy's dynamic<br>metadata per HTTP request. The namespaced key is <code class="thin">io.envoy.ai_gateway</code>.<br>These route-level costs override any global defaults defined in GatewayConfig.Spec.GlobalLLMRequestCosts<br>for the same metadataKey. If a metadataKey is not defined in either place, no cost is calculated for it.<br>This allows you to define common cost formulas once at the gateway level (e.g., via GatewayConfig)<br>and only override them in specific routes when needed (e.g., premium routes with different pricing).<br>For example, let's say we have the following LLMRequestCosts configuration:<br><pre class="prism-code language-yaml"><code class="codeBlock language-yaml"><br>	llmRequestCosts:<br>	- metadataKey: llm_input_token<br>	  type: InputToken<br>	- metadataKey: llm_output_token<br>	  type: OutputToken<br>	- metadataKey: llm_total_token<br>	  type: TotalToken<br>	- metadataKey: llm_cached_input_token<br>	  type: CachedInputToken<br>- metadataKey: llm_cache_creation_input_token<br>   type: CacheCreationInputToken<br></code></pre><br>Then, with the following BackendTrafficPolicy of Envoy Gateway, you can have three<br>rate limit buckets for each unique x-tenant-id header value. One bucket is for the input token,<br>the other is for the output token, and the last one is for the total token.<br>Each bucket will be reduced by the corresponding token usage captured by the AI Gateway filter.<br><pre class="prism-code language-yaml"><code class="codeBlock language-yaml"><br>	apiVersion: gateway.envoyproxy.io/v1alpha1<br>	kind: BackendTrafficPolicy<br>	metadata:<br>	  name: some-example-token-rate-limit<br>	  namespace: default<br>	spec:<br>	  targetRefs:<br>	  - group: gateway.networking.k8s.io<br>	     kind: HTTPRoute<br>	     name: usage-rate-limit<br>	  rateLimit:<br>	    type: Global<br>	    global:<br>	      rules:<br>	        - clientSelectors:<br>	            # Do the rate limiting based on the x-tenant-id header.<br>	            - headers:<br>	                - name: x-tenant-id<br>	                  type: Distinct<br>	          limit:<br>	            # Configures the number of <code class="thin">tokens</code> allowed per hour.<br>	            requests: 10000<br>	            unit: Hour<br>	          cost:<br>	            request:<br>	              from: Number<br>	              # Setting the request cost to zero allows to only check the rate limit budget,<br>	              # and not consume the budget on the request path.<br>	              number: 0<br>	            # This specifies the cost of the response retrieved from the dynamic metadata set by the AI Gateway filter.<br>	            # The extracted value will be used to consume the rate limit budget, and subsequent requests will be rate limited<br>	            # if the budget is exhausted.<br>	            response:<br>	              from: Metadata<br>	              metadata:<br>	                namespace: io.envoy.ai_gateway<br>	                key: llm_input_token<br>	        - clientSelectors:<br>	            - headers:<br>	                - name: x-tenant-id<br>	                  type: Distinct<br>	          limit:<br>	            requests: 10000<br>	            unit: Hour<br>	          cost:<br>	            request:<br>	              from: Number<br>	              number: 0<br>	            response:<br>	              from: Metadata<br>	              metadata:<br>	                namespace: io.envoy.ai_gateway<br>	                key: llm_output_token<br>	        - clientSelectors:<br>	            - headers:<br>	                - name: x-tenant-id<br>	                  type: Distinct<br>	          limit:<br>	            requests: 10000<br>	            unit: Hour<br>	          cost:<br>	            request:<br>	              from: Number<br>	              number: 0<br>	            response:<br>	              from: Metadata<br>	              metadata:<br>	                namespace: io.envoy.ai_gateway<br>	                key: llm_total_token<br></code></pre><br>Note that when multiple AIGatewayRoute resources are attached to the same Gateway, and<br>different costs are configured for the same metadata key, each route's rule is carried in<br>the filter configuration with the route identity; the data plane selects the matching rule<br>per request (by route), so each route can define its own cost for the same metadata key.</div></div></div>
<h4 class="anchor anchorTargetStickyNavbar_Vzrq" id="aigatewayroutestatus-1"><a id="github-com-envoyproxy-ai-gateway-api-v1beta1-aigatewayroutestatus">AIGatewayRouteStatus</a><a href="#aigatewayroutestatus-1" class="hash-link" aria-label="Direct link to aigatewayroutestatus-1" title="Direct link to aigatewayroutestatus-1" translate="no">​</a></h4>
<p><strong>Appears in:</strong></p>
\<ul\>
<li class=""><a href="#github-com-envoyproxy-ai-gateway-api-v1beta1-aigatewayroute" class="">AIGatewayRoute</a></li>
</ul>
<p>AIGatewayRouteStatus contains the conditions by the reconciliation result.</p>
<h5 class="anchor anchorTargetStickyNavbar_Vzrq" id="fields-85">Fields<a href="#fields-85" class="hash-link" aria-label="Direct link to Fields" title="Direct link to Fields" translate="no">​</a></h5>
<div class="api-field"><div class="api-field__left"><code class="api-field__name">conditions</code><span class="api-field__badge api-field__badge--required">required</span><div class="api-field__type"><div><a href="https://kubernetes.io/docs/reference/generated/kubernetes-api/v1.29/#condition-v1-meta">Condition</a> array</div></div></div><div class="api-field__right"><div class="api-field__description">Conditions is the list of conditions by the reconciliation result.<br>Currently, at most one condition is set.<br>Known .status.conditions.type are: <code class="thin">Accepted</code>, <code class="thin">NotAccepted</code>.</div></div></div>
<h4 class="anchor anchorTargetStickyNavbar_Vzrq" id="aiservicebackendspec-1"><a id="github-com-envoyproxy-ai-gateway-api-v1beta1-aiservicebackendspec">AIServiceBackendSpec</a><a href="#aiservicebackendspec-1" class="hash-link" aria-label="Direct link to aiservicebackendspec-1" title="Direct link to aiservicebackendspec-1" translate="no">​</a></h4>
<p><strong>Appears in:</strong></p>
\<ul\>
<li class=""><a href="#github-com-envoyproxy-ai-gateway-api-v1beta1-aiservicebackend" class="">AIServiceBackend</a></li>
</ul>
<p>AIServiceBackendSpec details the AIServiceBackend configuration.</p>
<h5 class="anchor anchorTargetStickyNavbar_Vzrq" id="fields-86">Fields<a href="#fields-86" class="hash-link" aria-label="Direct link to Fields" title="Direct link to Fields" translate="no">​</a></h5>
<div class="api-field"><div class="api-field__left"><code class="api-field__name">schema</code><span class="api-field__badge api-field__badge--required">required</span><div class="api-field__type"><div><a href="#github-com-envoyproxy-ai-gateway-api-v1beta1-versionedapischema">VersionedAPISchema</a></div></div></div><div class="api-field__right"><div class="api-field__description">APISchema specifies the API schema of the output format of requests from<br>Envoy that this AIServiceBackend can accept as incoming requests.<br>Based on this schema, the ai-gateway will perform the necessary transformation for<br>the pair of AIGatewayRouteSpec.APISchema and AIServiceBackendSpec.APISchema.<br>This is required to be set.</div></div></div>
<div class="api-field"><div class="api-field__left"><code class="api-field__name">backendRef</code><span class="api-field__badge api-field__badge--required">required</span><div class="api-field__type"><div><a href="https://gateway-api.sigs.k8s.io/references/spec/#gateway.networking.k8s.io/v1.BackendObjectReference">BackendObjectReference</a></div></div></div><div class="api-field__right"><div class="api-field__description">BackendRef is the reference to the Backend resource that this AIServiceBackend corresponds to.<br>A backend must be a Backend resource of Envoy Gateway. Note that k8s Service will be supported<br>as a backend in the future. See https://github.com/envoyproxy/ai-gateway/issues/902 for more details.<br>This is required to be set.</div></div></div>
<div class="api-field"><div class="api-field__left"><code class="api-field__name">headerMutation</code><span class="api-field__badge api-field__badge--optional">optional</span><div class="api-field__type"><div><a href="#github-com-envoyproxy-ai-gateway-api-v1beta1-httpheadermutation">HTTPHeaderMutation</a></div></div></div><div class="api-field__right"><div class="api-field__description">HeaderMutation defines the mutation of HTTP headers that will be applied to the request<br>before sending it to the backend.</div></div></div>
<div class="api-field"><div class="api-field__left"><code class="api-field__name">bodyMutation</code><span class="api-field__badge api-field__badge--optional">optional</span><div class="api-field__type"><div><a href="#github-com-envoyproxy-ai-gateway-api-v1beta1-httpbodymutation">HTTPBodyMutation</a></div></div></div><div class="api-field__right"><div class="api-field__description">BodyMutation defines the mutation of HTTP request body JSON fields that will be applied to the request<br>before sending it to the backend.</div></div></div>
<div class="api-field"><div class="api-field__left"><code class="api-field__name">headerValueFilters</code><span class="api-field__badge api-field__badge--optional">optional</span><div class="api-field__type"><div><a href="#github-com-envoyproxy-ai-gateway-api-v1beta1-httpheadervaluefilter">HTTPHeaderValueFilter</a> array</div></div></div><div class="api-field__right"><div class="api-field__description">HeaderValueFilters filter individual values out of multi-valued request headers before sending<br>the request to this backend, so that one value an upstream provider does not accept does not<br>fail the whole request. At most one filter per header name.<br>Only GCPAnthropic and AWSAnthropic backends honor this, and today only for the<br><code class="thin">anthropic-beta</code> header — a filter naming any other header is accepted but has no effect.<br>Setting it on a backend with any other schema is rejected.</div></div></div>
<h4 class="anchor anchorTargetStickyNavbar_Vzrq" id="aiservicebackendstatus-1"><a id="github-com-envoyproxy-ai-gateway-api-v1beta1-aiservicebackendstatus">AIServiceBackendStatus</a><a href="#aiservicebackendstatus-1" class="hash-link" aria-label="Direct link to aiservicebackendstatus-1" title="Direct link to aiservicebackendstatus-1" translate="no">​</a></h4>
<p><strong>Appears in:</strong></p>
\<ul\>
<li class=""><a href="#github-com-envoyproxy-ai-gateway-api-v1beta1-aiservicebackend" class="">AIServiceBackend</a></li>
</ul>
<p>AIServiceBackendStatus contains the conditions by the reconciliation result.</p>
<h5 class="anchor anchorTargetStickyNavbar_Vzrq" id="fields-87">Fields<a href="#fields-87" class="hash-link" aria-label="Direct link to Fields" title="Direct link to Fields" translate="no">​</a></h5>
<div class="api-field"><div class="api-field__left"><code class="api-field__name">conditions</code><span class="api-field__badge api-field__badge--required">required</span><div class="api-field__type"><div><a href="https://kubernetes.io/docs/reference/generated/kubernetes-api/v1.29/#condition-v1-meta">Condition</a> array</div></div></div><div class="api-field__right"><div class="api-field__description">Conditions is the list of conditions by the reconciliation result.<br>Currently, at most one condition is set.<br>Known .status.conditions.type are: <code class="thin">Accepted</code>, <code class="thin">NotAccepted</code>.</div></div></div>
<h4 class="anchor anchorTargetStickyNavbar_Vzrq" id="apischema-1"><a id="github-com-envoyproxy-ai-gateway-api-v1beta1-apischema">APISchema</a><a href="#apischema-1" class="hash-link" aria-label="Direct link to apischema-1" title="Direct link to apischema-1" translate="no">​</a></h4>
<p><strong>Underlying type:</strong> string</p>
<p><strong>Appears in:</strong></p>
\<ul\>
<li class=""><a href="#github-com-envoyproxy-ai-gateway-api-v1beta1-versionedapischema" class="">VersionedAPISchema</a></li>
</ul>
<p>APISchema defines the API schema.</p>
<h5 class="anchor anchorTargetStickyNavbar_Vzrq" id="possible-values-4">Possible Values<a href="#possible-values-4" class="hash-link" aria-label="Direct link to Possible Values" title="Direct link to Possible Values" translate="no">​</a></h5>
<div class="api-field" data-type="enum"><div class="api-field__left"><code class="api-field__name">OpenAI</code></div><div class="api-field__right"><div class="api-field__description">APISchemaOpenAI is the OpenAI schema.<br>https://github.com/openai/openai-openapi<br></div></div></div>
<div class="api-field" data-type="enum"><div class="api-field__left"><code class="api-field__name">Cohere</code></div><div class="api-field__right"><div class="api-field__description">APISchemaCohere is the Cohere schema.<br>https://docs.cohere.com/v2<br></div></div></div>
<div class="api-field" data-type="enum"><div class="api-field__left"><code class="api-field__name">AWSBedrock</code></div><div class="api-field__right"><div class="api-field__description">APISchemaAWSBedrock is the AWS Bedrock schema.<br>https://docs.aws.amazon.com/bedrock/latest/APIReference/API_Operations_Amazon_Bedrock_Runtime.html<br></div></div></div>
<div class="api-field" data-type="enum"><div class="api-field__left"><code class="api-field__name">AzureOpenAI</code></div><div class="api-field__right"><div class="api-field__description">APISchemaAzureOpenAI APISchemaAzure is the Azure OpenAI schema.<br>https://learn.microsoft.com/en-us/azure/ai-services/openai/reference#api-specs<br></div></div></div>
<div class="api-field" data-type="enum"><div class="api-field__left"><code class="api-field__name">GCPVertexAI</code></div><div class="api-field__right"><div class="api-field__description">APISchemaGCPVertexAI is the schema followed by Gemini models hosted on GCP's Vertex AI platform.<br>Note: Using this schema requires a BackendSecurityPolicy to be configured and attached,<br>as the transformation will use the gcp-region and project-name from the BackendSecurityPolicy.<br>https://cloud.google.com/vertex-ai/docs/reference/rest/v1/projects.locations.endpoints/generateContent?hl=en<br></div></div></div>
<div class="api-field" data-type="enum"><div class="api-field__left"><code class="api-field__name">GCPAnthropic</code></div><div class="api-field__right"><div class="api-field__description">APISchemaGCPAnthropic is the schema for Anthropic models hosted on GCP's Vertex AI platform.<br>Returns native Anthropic format responses for seamless integration.<br>https://docs.anthropic.com/en/api/claude-on-vertex-ai<br></div></div></div>
<div class="api-field" data-type="enum"><div class="api-field__left"><code class="api-field__name">Anthropic</code></div><div class="api-field__right"><div class="api-field__description">APISchemaAnthropic is the native Anthropic API schema.<br>https://docs.claude.com/en/home<br></div></div></div>
<div class="api-field" data-type="enum"><div class="api-field__left"><code class="api-field__name">AWSAnthropic</code></div><div class="api-field__right"><div class="api-field__description">APISchemaAWSAnthropic is the schema for Anthropic models hosted on AWS Bedrock.<br>Uses the native Anthropic Messages API format for requests and responses.<br>When used with /v1/chat/completions endpoint, translates OpenAI format to Anthropic.<br>When used with /v1/messages endpoint, passes through native Anthropic format.<br>https://aws.amazon.com/bedrock/anthropic/<br>https://docs.claude.com/en/api/claude-on-amazon-bedrock<br></div></div></div>
<div class="api-field" data-type="enum"><div class="api-field__left"><code class="api-field__name">AWSOpenAI</code></div><div class="api-field__right"><div class="api-field__description">APISchemaAWSOpenAI is the OpenAI-compatible API schema provided by AWS.<br></div></div></div>
<div class="api-field" data-type="enum"><div class="api-field__left"><code class="api-field__name">TypeSafe</code></div><div class="api-field__right"><div class="api-field__description">APISchemaTypeSafe is the native TypeSafe AI schema used by the Jev System One model.<br>Requests to /v1/systemone are passed through unchanged, and the version defaults to v1.<br>https://docs.typesafe.ai/api.md<br></div></div></div>
<h4 class="anchor anchorTargetStickyNavbar_Vzrq" id="awscredentialsfile-1"><a id="github-com-envoyproxy-ai-gateway-api-v1beta1-awscredentialsfile">AWSCredentialsFile</a><a href="#awscredentialsfile-1" class="hash-link" aria-label="Direct link to awscredentialsfile-1" title="Direct link to awscredentialsfile-1" translate="no">​</a></h4>
<p><strong>Appears in:</strong></p>
\<ul\>
<li class=""><a href="#github-com-envoyproxy-ai-gateway-api-v1beta1-backendsecuritypolicyawscredentials" class="">BackendSecurityPolicyAWSCredentials</a></li>
</ul>
<p>AWSCredentialsFile specifies the credentials file to use for the AWS provider.
Envoy reads the secret file, and the profile to use is specified by the Profile field.</p>
<h5 class="anchor anchorTargetStickyNavbar_Vzrq" id="fields-88">Fields<a href="#fields-88" class="hash-link" aria-label="Direct link to Fields" title="Direct link to Fields" translate="no">​</a></h5>
<div class="api-field"><div class="api-field__left"><code class="api-field__name">secretRef</code><span class="api-field__badge api-field__badge--required">required</span><div class="api-field__type"><div><a href="https://gateway-api.sigs.k8s.io/references/spec/#gateway.networking.k8s.io/v1.SecretObjectReference">SecretObjectReference</a></div></div></div><div class="api-field__right"><div class="api-field__description">SecretRef is the reference to the credential file.<br>The secret should contain the AWS credentials file keyed on <code class="thin">credentials</code>.</div></div></div>
<div class="api-field"><div class="api-field__left"><code class="api-field__name">profile</code><span class="api-field__badge api-field__badge--required">required</span><div class="api-field__type"><div>string</div></div></div><div class="api-field__right"><div class="api-field__default">default</div><div class="api-field__description">Profile is the profile to use in the credentials file.</div></div></div>
<h4 class="anchor anchorTargetStickyNavbar_Vzrq" id="awsoidcexchangetoken-1"><a id="github-com-envoyproxy-ai-gateway-api-v1beta1-awsoidcexchangetoken">AWSOIDCExchangeToken</a><a href="#awsoidcexchangetoken-1" class="hash-link" aria-label="Direct link to awsoidcexchangetoken-1" title="Direct link to awsoidcexchangetoken-1" translate="no">​</a></h4>
<p><strong>Appears in:</strong></p>
\<ul\>
<li class=""><a href="#github-com-envoyproxy-ai-gateway-api-v1beta1-backendsecuritypolicyawscredentials" class="">BackendSecurityPolicyAWSCredentials</a></li>
</ul>
<p>AWSOIDCExchangeToken specifies credentials to obtain oidc token from a sso server.
For AWS, the controller will query STS to obtain AWS AccessKeyId, SecretAccessKey, and SessionToken,
and store them in a temporary credentials file.</p>
<h5 class="anchor anchorTargetStickyNavbar_Vzrq" id="fields-89">Fields<a href="#fields-89" class="hash-link" aria-label="Direct link to Fields" title="Direct link to Fields" translate="no">​</a></h5>
<div class="api-field"><div class="api-field__left"><code class="api-field__name">oidc</code><span class="api-field__badge api-field__badge--required">required</span><div class="api-field__type"><div><a href="https://gateway.envoyproxy.io/docs/api/extension_types/#oidc">OIDC</a></div></div></div><div class="api-field__right"><div class="api-field__description">OIDC is used to obtain oidc tokens via an SSO server which will be used to exchange for provider credentials.</div></div></div>
<div class="api-field"><div class="api-field__left"><code class="api-field__name">grantType</code><span class="api-field__badge api-field__badge--optional">optional</span><div class="api-field__type"><div>string</div></div></div><div class="api-field__right"><div class="api-field__description">GrantType is the method application gets access token.</div></div></div>
<div class="api-field"><div class="api-field__left"><code class="api-field__name">aud</code><span class="api-field__badge api-field__badge--optional">optional</span><div class="api-field__type"><div>string</div></div></div><div class="api-field__right"><div class="api-field__description">Aud defines the audience that this ID Token is intended for.</div></div></div>
<div class="api-field"><div class="api-field__left"><code class="api-field__name">awsRoleArn</code><span class="api-field__badge api-field__badge--required">required</span><div class="api-field__type"><div>string</div></div></div><div class="api-field__right"><div class="api-field__description">AwsRoleArn is the AWS IAM Role with the permission to use specific resources in AWS account<br>which maps to the temporary AWS security credentials exchanged using the authentication token issued by OIDC provider.</div></div></div>
<h4 class="anchor anchorTargetStickyNavbar_Vzrq" id="azureoidcexchangetoken-1"><a id="github-com-envoyproxy-ai-gateway-api-v1beta1-azureoidcexchangetoken">AzureOIDCExchangeToken</a><a href="#azureoidcexchangetoken-1" class="hash-link" aria-label="Direct link to azureoidcexchangetoken-1" title="Direct link to azureoidcexchangetoken-1" translate="no">​</a></h4>
<p><strong>Appears in:</strong></p>
\<ul\>
<li class=""><a href="#github-com-envoyproxy-ai-gateway-api-v1beta1-backendsecuritypolicyazurecredentials" class="">BackendSecurityPolicyAzureCredentials</a></li>
</ul>
<p>AzureOIDCExchangeToken specifies credentials to obtain oidc token from a sso server.
For Azure, the controller will query Azure Entra ID to get an Azure Access Token,
and store them in a secret.</p>
<h5 class="anchor anchorTargetStickyNavbar_Vzrq" id="fields-90">Fields<a href="#fields-90" class="hash-link" aria-label="Direct link to Fields" title="Direct link to Fields" translate="no">​</a></h5>
<div class="api-field"><div class="api-field__left"><code class="api-field__name">oidc</code><span class="api-field__badge api-field__badge--required">required</span><div class="api-field__type"><div><a href="https://gateway.envoyproxy.io/docs/api/extension_types/#oidc">OIDC</a></div></div></div><div class="api-field__right"><div class="api-field__description">OIDC is used to obtain oidc tokens via an SSO server which will be used to exchange for provider credentials.</div></div></div>
<div class="api-field"><div class="api-field__left"><code class="api-field__name">grantType</code><span class="api-field__badge api-field__badge--optional">optional</span><div class="api-field__type"><div>string</div></div></div><div class="api-field__right"><div class="api-field__description">GrantType is the method application gets access token.</div></div></div>
<div class="api-field"><div class="api-field__left"><code class="api-field__name">aud</code><span class="api-field__badge api-field__badge--optional">optional</span><div class="api-field__type"><div>string</div></div></div><div class="api-field__right"><div class="api-field__description">Aud defines the audience that this ID Token is intended for.</div></div></div>
<h4 class="anchor anchorTargetStickyNavbar_Vzrq" id="backendsecuritypolicyapikey-1"><a id="github-com-envoyproxy-ai-gateway-api-v1beta1-backendsecuritypolicyapikey">BackendSecurityPolicyAPIKey</a><a href="#backendsecuritypolicyapikey-1" class="hash-link" aria-label="Direct link to backendsecuritypolicyapikey-1" title="Direct link to backendsecuritypolicyapikey-1" translate="no">​</a></h4>
<p><strong>Appears in:</strong></p>
\<ul\>
<li class=""><a href="#github-com-envoyproxy-ai-gateway-api-v1beta1-backendsecuritypolicyspec" class="">BackendSecurityPolicySpec</a></li>
</ul>
<p>BackendSecurityPolicyAPIKey specifies the API key.</p>
<h5 class="anchor anchorTargetStickyNavbar_Vzrq" id="fields-91">Fields<a href="#fields-91" class="hash-link" aria-label="Direct link to Fields" title="Direct link to Fields" translate="no">​</a></h5>
<div class="api-field"><div class="api-field__left"><code class="api-field__name">secretRef</code><span class="api-field__badge api-field__badge--required">required</span><div class="api-field__type"><div><a href="https://gateway-api.sigs.k8s.io/references/spec/#gateway.networking.k8s.io/v1.SecretObjectReference">SecretObjectReference</a></div></div></div><div class="api-field__right"><div class="api-field__description">SecretRef is the reference to the secret containing the API key.<br>ai-gateway must be given the permission to read this secret.<br>The key of the secret should be <code class="thin">apiKey</code>.</div></div></div>
<h4 class="anchor anchorTargetStickyNavbar_Vzrq" id="backendsecuritypolicyawscredentials-1"><a id="github-com-envoyproxy-ai-gateway-api-v1beta1-backendsecuritypolicyawscredentials">BackendSecurityPolicyAWSCredentials</a><a href="#backendsecuritypolicyawscredentials-1" class="hash-link" aria-label="Direct link to backendsecuritypolicyawscredentials-1" title="Direct link to backendsecuritypolicyawscredentials-1" translate="no">​</a></h4>
<p><strong>Appears in:</strong></p>
\<ul\>
<li class=""><a href="#github-com-envoyproxy-ai-gateway-api-v1beta1-backendsecuritypolicyspec" class="">BackendSecurityPolicySpec</a></li>
</ul>
<p>BackendSecurityPolicyAWSCredentials contains the supported authentication mechanisms to access AWS.</p>
<p>When neither CredentialsFile nor OIDCExchangeToken is specified, the AWS SDK&#x27;s default credential chain
will be used. This automatically supports various authentication methods in the following order:</p>
\<ol\>
<li class="">Environment variables (AWS_ACCESS_KEY_ID, AWS_SECRET_ACCESS_KEY, AWS_SESSION_TOKEN)</li>
<li class="">EKS Pod Identity - automatically rotates credentials for pods in EKS clusters</li>
<li class="">IAM Roles for Service Accounts (IRSA) - injects credentials via mounted service account tokens</li>
<li class="">EC2 instance metadata (IAM instance roles)</li>
<li class="">ECS task roles</li>
</ol>
<p>The default credential chain is recommended for Kubernetes deployments as it supports automatic
credential rotation without manual configuration. Credentials are refreshed automatically when
they approach expiration (typically hourly for IRSA and Pod Identity).</p>
<h5 class="anchor anchorTargetStickyNavbar_Vzrq" id="fields-92">Fields<a href="#fields-92" class="hash-link" aria-label="Direct link to Fields" title="Direct link to Fields" translate="no">​</a></h5>
<div class="api-field"><div class="api-field__left"><code class="api-field__name">region</code><span class="api-field__badge api-field__badge--required">required</span><div class="api-field__type"><div>string</div></div></div><div class="api-field__right"><div class="api-field__description">Region specifies the AWS region associated with the policy.</div></div></div>
<div class="api-field"><div class="api-field__left"><code class="api-field__name">credentialsFile</code><span class="api-field__badge api-field__badge--optional">optional</span><div class="api-field__type"><div><a href="#github-com-envoyproxy-ai-gateway-api-v1beta1-awscredentialsfile">AWSCredentialsFile</a></div></div></div><div class="api-field__right"><div class="api-field__description">CredentialsFile specifies the credentials file to use for the AWS provider.<br>When specified, this takes precedence over the default credential chain.</div></div></div>
<div class="api-field"><div class="api-field__left"><code class="api-field__name">oidcExchangeToken</code><span class="api-field__badge api-field__badge--optional">optional</span><div class="api-field__type"><div><a href="#github-com-envoyproxy-ai-gateway-api-v1beta1-awsoidcexchangetoken">AWSOIDCExchangeToken</a></div></div></div><div class="api-field__right"><div class="api-field__description">OIDCExchangeToken specifies the oidc configurations used to obtain an oidc token. The oidc token will be<br>used to obtain temporary credentials to access AWS.<br>When specified, this takes precedence over the default credential chain.</div></div></div>
<h4 class="anchor anchorTargetStickyNavbar_Vzrq" id="backendsecuritypolicyanthropicapikey-1"><a id="github-com-envoyproxy-ai-gateway-api-v1beta1-backendsecuritypolicyanthropicapikey">BackendSecurityPolicyAnthropicAPIKey</a><a href="#backendsecuritypolicyanthropicapikey-1" class="hash-link" aria-label="Direct link to backendsecuritypolicyanthropicapikey-1" title="Direct link to backendsecuritypolicyanthropicapikey-1" translate="no">​</a></h4>

\<ul\>
<li class=""><a href="#github-com-envoyproxy-ai-gateway-api-v1beta1-backendsecuritypolicyspec" class="">BackendSecurityPolicySpec</a></li>
</ul>
<p>BackendSecurityPolicyAnthropicAPIKey specifies the Anthropic API key.</p>
<h5 class="anchor anchorTargetStickyNavbar_Vzrq" id="fields-93">Fields<a href="#fields-93" class="hash-link" aria-label="Direct link to Fields" title="Direct link to Fields" translate="no">​</a></h5>
<div class="api-field"><div class="api-field__left"><code class="api-field__name">secretRef</code><span class="api-field__badge api-field__badge--required">required</span><div class="api-field__type"><div><a href="https://gateway-api.sigs.k8s.io/references/spec/#gateway.networking.k8s.io/v1.SecretObjectReference">SecretObjectReference</a></div></div></div><div class="api-field__right"><div class="api-field__description">SecretRef is the reference to the secret containing the Anthropic API key.<br>ai-gateway must be given the permission to read this secret.<br>The key of the secret should be <code class="thin">apiKey</code>.</div></div></div>
<h4 class="anchor anchorTargetStickyNavbar_Vzrq" id="backendsecuritypolicyazureapikey-1"><a id="github-com-envoyproxy-ai-gateway-api-v1beta1-backendsecuritypolicyazureapikey">BackendSecurityPolicyAzureAPIKey</a><a href="#backendsecuritypolicyazureapikey-1" class="hash-link" aria-label="Direct link to backendsecuritypolicyazureapikey-1" title="Direct link to backendsecuritypolicyazureapikey-1" translate="no">​</a></h4>
<p><strong>Appears in:</strong></p>
\<ul\>
<li class=""><a href="#github-com-envoyproxy-ai-gateway-api-v1beta1-backendsecuritypolicyspec" class="">BackendSecurityPolicySpec</a></li>
</ul>
<p>BackendSecurityPolicyAzureAPIKey specifies the Azure OpenAI API key.</p>
<h5 class="anchor anchorTargetStickyNavbar_Vzrq" id="fields-94">Fields<a href="#fields-94" class="hash-link" aria-label="Direct link to Fields" title="Direct link to Fields" translate="no">​</a></h5>
<div class="api-field"><div class="api-field__left"><code class="api-field__name">secretRef</code><span class="api-field__badge api-field__badge--required">required</span><div class="api-field__type"><div><a href="https://gateway-api.sigs.k8s.io/references/spec/#gateway.networking.k8s.io/v1.SecretObjectReference">SecretObjectReference</a></div></div></div><div class="api-field__right"><div class="api-field__description">SecretRef is the reference to the secret containing the Azure API key.<br>ai-gateway must be given the permission to read this secret.<br>The key of the secret should be <code class="thin">apiKey</code>.</div></div></div>
<h4 class="anchor anchorTargetStickyNavbar_Vzrq" id="backendsecuritypolicyazurecredentials-1"><a id="github-com-envoyproxy-ai-gateway-api-v1beta1-backendsecuritypolicyazurecredentials">BackendSecurityPolicyAzureCredentials</a><a href="#backendsecuritypolicyazurecredentials-1" class="hash-link" aria-label="Direct link to backendsecuritypolicyazurecredentials-1" title="Direct link to backendsecuritypolicyazurecredentials-1" translate="no">​</a></h4>
<p><strong>Appears in:</strong></p>
\<ul\>
<li class=""><a href="#github-com-envoyproxy-ai-gateway-api-v1beta1-backendsecuritypolicyspec" class="">BackendSecurityPolicySpec</a></li>
</ul>
<p>BackendSecurityPolicyAzureCredentials contains the supported authentication mechanisms to access Azure.
Only one of ClientSecretRef or OIDCExchangeToken must be specified. Credentials will not be generated if
neither are set.</p>
<h5 class="anchor anchorTargetStickyNavbar_Vzrq" id="fields-95">Fields<a href="#fields-95" class="hash-link" aria-label="Direct link to Fields" title="Direct link to Fields" translate="no">​</a></h5>
<div class="api-field"><div class="api-field__left"><code class="api-field__name">clientID</code><span class="api-field__badge api-field__badge--required">required</span><div class="api-field__type"><div>string</div></div></div><div class="api-field__right"><div class="api-field__description">ClientID is a unique identifier for an application in Azure.</div></div></div>
<div class="api-field"><div class="api-field__left"><code class="api-field__name">tenantID</code><span class="api-field__badge api-field__badge--required">required</span><div class="api-field__type"><div>string</div></div></div><div class="api-field__right"><div class="api-field__description">TenantId is a unique identifier for an Azure Active Directory instance.</div></div></div>
<div class="api-field"><div class="api-field__left"><code class="api-field__name">clientSecretRef</code><span class="api-field__badge api-field__badge--optional">optional</span><div class="api-field__type"><div><a href="https://gateway-api.sigs.k8s.io/references/spec/#gateway.networking.k8s.io/v1.SecretObjectReference">SecretObjectReference</a></div></div></div><div class="api-field__right"><div class="api-field__description">ClientSecretRef is the reference to the secret containing the Azure client secret.<br>ai-gateway must be given the permission to read this secret.<br>The key of secret should be <code class="thin">client-secret</code>.</div></div></div>
<div class="api-field"><div class="api-field__left"><code class="api-field__name">oidcExchangeToken</code><span class="api-field__badge api-field__badge--optional">optional</span><div class="api-field__type"><div><a href="#github-com-envoyproxy-ai-gateway-api-v1beta1-azureoidcexchangetoken">AzureOIDCExchangeToken</a></div></div></div><div class="api-field__right"><div class="api-field__description">OIDCExchangeToken specifies the oidc configurations used to obtain an oidc token. The oidc token will be<br>used to obtain temporary credentials to access Azure.</div></div></div>
<h4 class="anchor anchorTargetStickyNavbar_Vzrq" id="backendsecuritypolicycredentialoverride"><a id="github-com-envoyproxy-ai-gateway-api-v1beta1-backendsecuritypolicycredentialoverride">BackendSecurityPolicyCredentialOverride</a><a href="#backendsecuritypolicycredentialoverride" class="hash-link" aria-label="Direct link to backendsecuritypolicycredentialoverride" title="Direct link to backendsecuritypolicycredentialoverride" translate="no">​</a></h4>
<p><strong>Appears in:</strong></p>
\<ul\>
<li class=""><a href="#github-com-envoyproxy-ai-gateway-api-v1beta1-backendsecuritypolicyspec" class="">BackendSecurityPolicySpec</a></li>
</ul>
<p>BackendSecurityPolicyCredentialOverride configures per-request credential sourcing.
A trusted upstream filter (ext_authz or a preceding ext_proc) resolves the caller&#x27;s
credential and delivers it via one of the two sources below.
Exactly one source must be configured.</p>
<h5 class="anchor anchorTargetStickyNavbar_Vzrq" id="fields-96">Fields<a href="#fields-96" class="hash-link" aria-label="Direct link to Fields" title="Direct link to Fields" translate="no">​</a></h5>
<div class="api-field"><div class="api-field__left"><code class="api-field__name">fromRequestHeaders</code><span class="api-field__badge api-field__badge--optional">optional</span><div class="api-field__type"><div><a href="#github-com-envoyproxy-ai-gateway-api-v1beta1-credentialoverridefromrequestheaders">CredentialOverrideFromRequestHeaders</a></div></div></div><div class="api-field__right"><div class="api-field__description">FromRequestHeaders sources the credential from a request header injected by a trusted<br>ingress filter. The gateway strips the header before forwarding to the upstream backend.<br>Because any client can set request headers, this source is only safe when a trusted filter<br>removes any client-supplied copy and writes the canonical value.</div></div></div>
<div class="api-field"><div class="api-field__left"><code class="api-field__name">fromDynamicMetadata</code><span class="api-field__badge api-field__badge--optional">optional</span><div class="api-field__type"><div><a href="#github-com-envoyproxy-ai-gateway-api-v1beta1-credentialoverridefromdynamicmetadata">CredentialOverrideFromDynamicMetadata</a></div></div></div><div class="api-field__right"><div class="api-field__description">FromDynamicMetadata sources the credential from Envoy dynamic metadata set by a trusted<br>filter (e.g. ext_authz). Metadata cannot be forged by the client and is the preferred source.</div></div></div>
<h4 class="anchor anchorTargetStickyNavbar_Vzrq" id="backendsecuritypolicygcpcredentials-1"><a id="github-com-envoyproxy-ai-gateway-api-v1beta1-backendsecuritypolicygcpcredentials">BackendSecurityPolicyGCPCredentials</a><a href="#backendsecuritypolicygcpcredentials-1" class="hash-link" aria-label="Direct link to backendsecuritypolicygcpcredentials-1" title="Direct link to backendsecuritypolicygcpcredentials-1" translate="no">​</a></h4>
<p><strong>Appears in:</strong></p>
\<ul\>
<li class=""><a href="#github-com-envoyproxy-ai-gateway-api-v1beta1-backendsecuritypolicyspec" class="">BackendSecurityPolicySpec</a></li>
</ul>
<p>BackendSecurityPolicyGCPCredentials contains the supported authentication mechanisms to access GCP.</p>
<p>When neither CredentialsFile nor WorkloadIdentityFederationConfig is specified, Application Default
Credentials (ADC) will be used. This supports GKE Workload Identity, environment variables, and
default service account credentials when running on GCP.</p>
<h5 class="anchor anchorTargetStickyNavbar_Vzrq" id="fields-97">Fields<a href="#fields-97" class="hash-link" aria-label="Direct link to Fields" title="Direct link to Fields" translate="no">​</a></h5>
<div class="api-field"><div class="api-field__left"><code class="api-field__name">projectName</code><span class="api-field__badge api-field__badge--required">required</span><div class="api-field__type"><div>string</div></div></div><div class="api-field__right"><div class="api-field__description">ProjectName is the GCP project name.</div></div></div>
<div class="api-field"><div class="api-field__left"><code class="api-field__name">region</code><span class="api-field__badge api-field__badge--required">required</span><div class="api-field__type"><div>string</div></div></div><div class="api-field__right"><div class="api-field__description">Region is the GCP region associated with the policy.</div></div></div>
<div class="api-field"><div class="api-field__left"><code class="api-field__name">credentialsFile</code><span class="api-field__badge api-field__badge--optional">optional</span><div class="api-field__type"><div><a href="#github-com-envoyproxy-ai-gateway-api-v1beta1-gcpcredentialsfile">GCPCredentialsFile</a></div></div></div><div class="api-field__right"><div class="api-field__description">CredentialsFile specifies the service account credentials file to use for the GCP provider.</div></div></div>
<div class="api-field"><div class="api-field__left"><code class="api-field__name">workloadIdentityFederationConfig</code><span class="api-field__badge api-field__badge--optional">optional</span><div class="api-field__type"><div><a href="#github-com-envoyproxy-ai-gateway-api-v1beta1-gcpworkloadidentityfederationconfig">GCPWorkloadIdentityFederationConfig</a></div></div></div><div class="api-field__right"><div class="api-field__description">WorkloadIdentityFederationConfig is the configuration for the GCP Workload Identity Federation.</div></div></div>
<h4 class="anchor anchorTargetStickyNavbar_Vzrq" id="backendsecuritypolicyoidc-1"><a id="github-com-envoyproxy-ai-gateway-api-v1beta1-backendsecuritypolicyoidc">BackendSecurityPolicyOIDC</a><a href="#backendsecuritypolicyoidc-1" class="hash-link" aria-label="Direct link to backendsecuritypolicyoidc-1" title="Direct link to backendsecuritypolicyoidc-1" translate="no">​</a></h4>

<li class=""><a href="#github-com-envoyproxy-ai-gateway-api-v1beta1-gcpworkloadidentityprovider" class="">GCPWorkloadIdentityProvider</a></li>
</ul>
<p>BackendSecurityPolicyOIDC specifies OIDC related fields.</p>
<h5 class="anchor anchorTargetStickyNavbar_Vzrq" id="fields-98">Fields<a href="#fields-98" class="hash-link" aria-label="Direct link to Fields" title="Direct link to Fields" translate="no">​</a></h5>

<h4 class="anchor anchorTargetStickyNavbar_Vzrq" id="backendsecuritypolicyspec-1"><a id="github-com-envoyproxy-ai-gateway-api-v1beta1-backendsecuritypolicyspec">BackendSecurityPolicySpec</a><a href="#backendsecuritypolicyspec-1" class="hash-link" aria-label="Direct link to backendsecuritypolicyspec-1" title="Direct link to backendsecuritypolicyspec-1" translate="no">​</a></h4>

<p>BackendSecurityPolicySpec specifies authentication rules on access the provider from the Gateway.
Only one mechanism to access a backend(s) can be specified.</p>
<p>Only one type of BackendSecurityPolicy can be defined.</p>
