The API key will be injected into the <code class="thin">x-api-key</code> header.<br>https://docs.claude.com/en/api/overview#authentication</div></div></div>
<div class="api-field"><div class="api-field__left"><code class="api-field__name">credentialOverride</code><span class="api-field__badge api-field__badge--optional">optional</span><div class="api-field__type"><div><a href="#github-com-envoyproxy-ai-gateway-api-v1beta1-backendsecuritypolicycredentialoverride">BackendSecurityPolicyCredentialOverride</a></div></div></div><div class="api-field__right"><div class="api-field__description">CredentialOverride, when set, sources the upstream credential per-request instead of using<br>the static credential configured above.<br>For AWSCredentials the credential is the three-part SigV4 input rather than a single string;<br>see the source types for how each carries it.</div></div></div>
<h4 class="anchor anchorTargetStickyNavbar_Vzrq" id="backendsecuritypolicystatus-1"><a id="github-com-envoyproxy-ai-gateway-api-v1beta1-backendsecuritypolicystatus">BackendSecurityPolicyStatus</a><a href="#backendsecuritypolicystatus-1" class="hash-link" aria-label="Direct link to backendsecuritypolicystatus-1" title="Direct link to backendsecuritypolicystatus-1" translate="no">​</a></h4>
<p><strong>Appears in:</strong></p>
\<ul\>
<li class=""><a href="#github-com-envoyproxy-ai-gateway-api-v1beta1-backendsecuritypolicy" class="">BackendSecurityPolicy</a></li>
</ul>
<p>BackendSecurityPolicyStatus contains the conditions by the reconciliation result.</p>
<h5 class="anchor anchorTargetStickyNavbar_Vzrq" id="fields-100">Fields<a href="#fields-100" class="hash-link" aria-label="Direct link to Fields" title="Direct link to Fields" translate="no">​</a></h5>
<div class="api-field"><div class="api-field__left"><code class="api-field__name">conditions</code><span class="api-field__badge api-field__badge--required">required</span><div class="api-field__type"><div><a href="https://kubernetes.io/docs/reference/generated/kubernetes-api/v1.29/#condition-v1-meta">Condition</a> array</div></div></div><div class="api-field__right"><div class="api-field__description">Conditions is the list of conditions by the reconciliation result.<br>Currently, at most one condition is set.<br>Known .status.conditions.type are: <code class="thin">Accepted</code>, <code class="thin">NotAccepted</code>.</div></div></div>
<h4 class="anchor anchorTargetStickyNavbar_Vzrq" id="backendsecuritypolicytype-1"><a id="github-com-envoyproxy-ai-gateway-api-v1beta1-backendsecuritypolicytype">BackendSecurityPolicyType</a><a href="#backendsecuritypolicytype-1" class="hash-link" aria-label="Direct link to backendsecuritypolicytype-1" title="Direct link to backendsecuritypolicytype-1" translate="no">​</a></h4>
<p><strong>Underlying type:</strong> string</p>
<p><strong>Appears in:</strong></p>
\<ul\>
<li class=""><a href="#github-com-envoyproxy-ai-gateway-api-v1beta1-backendsecuritypolicyspec" class="">BackendSecurityPolicySpec</a></li>
</ul>
<p>BackendSecurityPolicyType specifies the type of auth mechanism used to access a backend.</p>
<h5 class="anchor anchorTargetStickyNavbar_Vzrq" id="possible-values-5">Possible Values<a href="#possible-values-5" class="hash-link" aria-label="Direct link to Possible Values" title="Direct link to Possible Values" translate="no">​</a></h5>
<div class="api-field" data-type="enum"><div class="api-field__left"><code class="api-field__name">APIKey</code></div><div class="api-field__right"></div></div>
<div class="api-field" data-type="enum"><div class="api-field__left"><code class="api-field__name">AWSCredentials</code></div><div class="api-field__right"></div></div>
<div class="api-field" data-type="enum"><div class="api-field__left"><code class="api-field__name">AzureAPIKey</code></div><div class="api-field__right"></div></div>
<div class="api-field" data-type="enum"><div class="api-field__left"><code class="api-field__name">AnthropicAPIKey</code></div><div class="api-field__right"></div></div>
<div class="api-field" data-type="enum"><div class="api-field__left"><code class="api-field__name">AzureCredentials</code></div><div class="api-field__right"></div></div>
<div class="api-field" data-type="enum"><div class="api-field__left"><code class="api-field__name">GCPCredentials</code></div><div class="api-field__right"></div></div>
<h4 class="anchor anchorTargetStickyNavbar_Vzrq" id="credentialoverridefromdynamicmetadata"><a id="github-com-envoyproxy-ai-gateway-api-v1beta1-credentialoverridefromdynamicmetadata">CredentialOverrideFromDynamicMetadata</a><a href="#credentialoverridefromdynamicmetadata" class="hash-link" aria-label="Direct link to credentialoverridefromdynamicmetadata" title="Direct link to credentialoverridefromdynamicmetadata" translate="no">​</a></h4>
<p><strong>Appears in:</strong></p>
\<ul\>
<li class=""><a href="#github-com-envoyproxy-ai-gateway-api-v1beta1-backendsecuritypolicycredentialoverride" class="">BackendSecurityPolicyCredentialOverride</a></li>
</ul>
<p>CredentialOverrideFromDynamicMetadata sources the per-request credential from Envoy
dynamic metadata produced by a trusted filter running earlier in the filter chain.</p>
<h5 class="anchor anchorTargetStickyNavbar_Vzrq" id="fields-101">Fields<a href="#fields-101" class="hash-link" aria-label="Direct link to Fields" title="Direct link to Fields" translate="no">​</a></h5>
<div class="api-field"><div class="api-field__left"><code class="api-field__name">namespace</code><span class="api-field__badge api-field__badge--required">required</span><div class="api-field__type"><div>string</div></div></div><div class="api-field__right"><div class="api-field__description">Namespace is the Envoy metadata namespace written by the trusted filter.<br>Example: <code class="thin">envoy.filters.http.ext_authz</code><br>List the namespace in the Gateway's GatewayConfig under<br>extProc.metadataForwardingNamespaces. Envoy forwards only the namespaces listed there,<br>and only untyped metadata is read.</div></div></div>
<div class="api-field"><div class="api-field__left"><code class="api-field__name">key</code><span class="api-field__badge api-field__badge--optional">optional</span><div class="api-field__type"><div>string</div></div></div><div class="api-field__right"><div class="api-field__description">Key is the metadata key within the namespace. Defaults to the x-aigw-* name for the auth type.<br>For every type except AWSCredentials the value must be a string holding the credential.<br>For AWSCredentials it must be a struct, since SigV4 needs three inputs:<br>  accessKeyId<br>  secretAccessKey<br>  sessionToken     (optional; omit for long-lived credentials)<br>Field names are fixed. accessKeyId without secretAccessKey, or the reverse, fails the<br>request instead of falling back. Defaults to <code class="thin">x-aigw-aws-credentials</code>.</div></div></div>
<div class="api-field"><div class="api-field__left"><code class="api-field__name">fallbackToConfigured</code><span class="api-field__badge api-field__badge--optional">optional</span><div class="api-field__type"><div>boolean</div></div></div><div class="api-field__right"><div class="api-field__default">true</div><div class="api-field__description">FallbackToConfigured controls behaviour when the metadata key is absent.<br>true (default) falls back to the static credential configured in the policy.<br>false returns a 401 to the client without forwarding the request.</div></div></div>
<h4 class="anchor anchorTargetStickyNavbar_Vzrq" id="credentialoverridefromrequestheaders"><a id="github-com-envoyproxy-ai-gateway-api-v1beta1-credentialoverridefromrequestheaders">CredentialOverrideFromRequestHeaders</a><a href="#credentialoverridefromrequestheaders" class="hash-link" aria-label="Direct link to credentialoverridefromrequestheaders" title="Direct link to credentialoverridefromrequestheaders" translate="no">​</a></h4>
<p><strong>Appears in:</strong></p>
\<ul\>
<li class=""><a href="#github-com-envoyproxy-ai-gateway-api-v1beta1-backendsecuritypolicycredentialoverride" class="">BackendSecurityPolicyCredentialOverride</a></li>
</ul>
<p>CredentialOverrideFromRequestHeaders sources the per-request credential from a request header.</p>
<h5 class="anchor anchorTargetStickyNavbar_Vzrq" id="fields-102">Fields<a href="#fields-102" class="hash-link" aria-label="Direct link to Fields" title="Direct link to Fields" translate="no">​</a></h5>
<div class="api-field"><div class="api-field__left"><code class="api-field__name">header</code><span class="api-field__badge api-field__badge--optional">optional</span><div class="api-field__type"><div>string</div></div></div><div class="api-field__right"><div class="api-field__description">Header is the name of the request header that carries the credential.<br>Defaults to the x-aigw-* header for the configured auth type:<br>  APIKey          → x-aigw-api-key<br>  AnthropicAPIKey → x-aigw-anthropic-api-key<br>  AzureAPIKey     → x-aigw-azure-api-key<br>  AzureCredentials → x-aigw-azure-access-token<br>  GCPCredentials  → x-aigw-gcp-access-token<br>For AWSCredentials this is a prefix, not a single header name, since SigV4 needs three<br>inputs. Defaults to <code class="thin">x-aigw-aws-</code>, yielding:<br>  x-aigw-aws-access-key-id<br>  x-aigw-aws-secret-access-key<br>  x-aigw-aws-session-token     (optional; omit for long-lived credentials)<br>All three are stripped before the backend sees them. An access key ID without a secret<br>access key, or the reverse, fails the request instead of falling back.<br>Prefer FromDynamicMetadata for AWSCredentials. Headers are client-settable, and a secret<br>access key on the wire is a worse leak than an API key.</div></div></div>
<div class="api-field"><div class="api-field__left"><code class="api-field__name">fallbackToConfigured</code><span class="api-field__badge api-field__badge--optional">optional</span><div class="api-field__type"><div>boolean</div></div></div><div class="api-field__right"><div class="api-field__default">true</div><div class="api-field__description">FallbackToConfigured controls behaviour when the header is absent.<br>true (default) falls back to the static credential configured in the policy.<br>false returns a 401 to the client without forwarding the request.</div></div></div>
<h4 class="anchor anchorTargetStickyNavbar_Vzrq" id="gcpcredentialsfile-1"><a id="github-com-envoyproxy-ai-gateway-api-v1beta1-gcpcredentialsfile">GCPCredentialsFile</a><a href="#gcpcredentialsfile-1" class="hash-link" aria-label="Direct link to gcpcredentialsfile-1" title="Direct link to gcpcredentialsfile-1" translate="no">​</a></h4>
<p><strong>Appears in:</strong></p>
\<ul\>
<li class=""><a href="#github-com-envoyproxy-ai-gateway-api-v1beta1-backendsecuritypolicygcpcredentials" class="">BackendSecurityPolicyGCPCredentials</a></li>
</ul>
<p>GCPCredentialsFile specifies the service account key json file to authenticate with GCP provider.</p>
<h5 class="anchor anchorTargetStickyNavbar_Vzrq" id="fields-103">Fields<a href="#fields-103" class="hash-link" aria-label="Direct link to Fields" title="Direct link to Fields" translate="no">​</a></h5>
<div class="api-field"><div class="api-field__left"><code class="api-field__name">secretRef</code><span class="api-field__badge api-field__badge--required">required</span><div class="api-field__type"><div><a href="https://gateway-api.sigs.k8s.io/references/spec/#gateway.networking.k8s.io/v1.SecretObjectReference">SecretObjectReference</a></div></div></div><div class="api-field__right"><div class="api-field__description">SecretRef is the reference to the credential file.<br>The secret should contain the GCP service account credentials file keyed on <code class="thin">service_account.json</code>.</div></div></div>
<h4 class="anchor anchorTargetStickyNavbar_Vzrq" id="gcpoidcexchangetoken-1"><a id="github-com-envoyproxy-ai-gateway-api-v1beta1-gcpoidcexchangetoken">GCPOIDCExchangeToken</a><a href="#gcpoidcexchangetoken-1" class="hash-link" aria-label="Direct link to gcpoidcexchangetoken-1" title="Direct link to gcpoidcexchangetoken-1" translate="no">​</a></h4>

\<ul\>
<li class=""><a href="#github-com-envoyproxy-ai-gateway-api-v1beta1-gcpworkloadidentityfederationconfig" class="">GCPWorkloadIdentityFederationConfig</a></li>
</ul>
<h5 class="anchor anchorTargetStickyNavbar_Vzrq" id="fields-104">Fields<a href="#fields-104" class="hash-link" aria-label="Direct link to Fields" title="Direct link to Fields" translate="no">​</a></h5>
<div class="api-field"><div class="api-field__left"><code class="api-field__name">oidc</code><span class="api-field__badge api-field__badge--required">required</span><div class="api-field__type"><div><a href="https://gateway.envoyproxy.io/docs/api/extension_types/#oidc">OIDC</a></div></div></div><div class="api-field__right"><div class="api-field__description">OIDC is used to obtain oidc tokens via an SSO server which will be used to exchange for provider credentials.</div></div></div>
<div class="api-field"><div class="api-field__left"><code class="api-field__name">grantType</code><span class="api-field__badge api-field__badge--optional">optional</span><div class="api-field__type"><div>string</div></div></div><div class="api-field__right"><div class="api-field__description">GrantType is the method application gets access token.</div></div></div>
<div class="api-field"><div class="api-field__left"><code class="api-field__name">aud</code><span class="api-field__badge api-field__badge--optional">optional</span><div class="api-field__type"><div>string</div></div></div><div class="api-field__right"><div class="api-field__description">Aud defines the audience that this ID Token is intended for.</div></div></div>

<div class="api-field"><div class="api-field__left"><code class="api-field__name">serviceAccountName</code><span class="api-field__badge api-field__badge--required">required</span><div class="api-field__type"><div>string</div></div></div><div class="api-field__right"><div class="api-field__description">ServiceAccountName is the name of the service account to impersonate.</div></div></div>
<h4 class="anchor anchorTargetStickyNavbar_Vzrq" id="gcpworkloadidentityfederationconfig-1"><a id="github-com-envoyproxy-ai-gateway-api-v1beta1-gcpworkloadidentityfederationconfig">GCPWorkloadIdentityFederationConfig</a><a href="#gcpworkloadidentityfederationconfig-1" class="hash-link" aria-label="Direct link to gcpworkloadidentityfederationconfig-1" title="Direct link to gcpworkloadidentityfederationconfig-1" translate="no">​</a></h4>

<div class="api-field"><div class="api-field__left"><code class="api-field__name">projectID</code><span class="api-field__badge api-field__badge--required">required</span><div class="api-field__type"><div>string</div></div></div><div class="api-field__right"><div class="api-field__description">ProjectID is the GCP project ID.</div></div></div>
<div class="api-field"><div class="api-field__left"><code class="api-field__name">workloadIdentityProviderName</code><span class="api-field__badge api-field__badge--required">required</span><div class="api-field__type"><div>string</div></div></div><div class="api-field__right"><div class="api-field__description">WorkloadIdentityProviderName is the name of the external identity provider as registered on Google Cloud Platform.</div></div></div>
<div class="api-field"><div class="api-field__left"><code class="api-field__name">oidcExchangeToken</code><span class="api-field__badge api-field__badge--required">required</span><div class="api-field__type"><div><a href="#github-com-envoyproxy-ai-gateway-api-v1beta1-gcpoidcexchangetoken">GCPOIDCExchangeToken</a></div></div></div><div class="api-field__right"><div class="api-field__description">OIDCExchangeToken specifies the oidc configurations used to obtain an oidc token. The oidc token will be<br>used to obtain temporary credentials to access GCP.</div></div></div>
<div class="api-field"><div class="api-field__left"><code class="api-field__name">workloadIdentityPoolName</code><span class="api-field__badge api-field__badge--required">required</span><div class="api-field__type"><div>string</div></div></div><div class="api-field__right"><div class="api-field__description">WorkloadIdentityPoolName is the name of the workload identity pool defined in GCP.<br>https://cloud.google.com/iam/docs/workload-identity-federation?hl=en</div></div></div>
<div class="api-field"><div class="api-field__left"><code class="api-field__name">serviceAccountImpersonation</code><span class="api-field__badge api-field__badge--optional">optional</span><div class="api-field__type"><div><a href="#github-com-envoyproxy-ai-gateway-api-v1beta1-gcpserviceaccountimpersonationconfig">GCPServiceAccountImpersonationConfig</a></div></div></div><div class="api-field__right"><div class="api-field__description">ServiceAccountImpersonation is the service account impersonation configuration.<br>This is used to impersonate a service account when getting access token.</div></div></div>
<h4 class="anchor anchorTargetStickyNavbar_Vzrq" id="gatewayconfigextproc-1"><a id="github-com-envoyproxy-ai-gateway-api-v1beta1-gatewayconfigextproc">GatewayConfigExtProc</a><a href="#gatewayconfigextproc-1" class="hash-link" aria-label="Direct link to gatewayconfigextproc-1" title="Direct link to gatewayconfigextproc-1" translate="no">​</a></h4>

</ul>

<h5 class="anchor anchorTargetStickyNavbar_Vzrq" id="fields-107">Fields<a href="#fields-107" class="hash-link" aria-label="Direct link to Fields" title="Direct link to Fields" translate="no">​</a></h5>
<div class="api-field"><div class="api-field__left"><code class="api-field__name">kubernetes</code><span class="api-field__badge api-field__badge--optional">optional</span><div class="api-field__type"><div><a href="https://gateway.envoyproxy.io/docs/api/extension_types/#kubernetescontainerspec">KubernetesContainerSpec</a></div></div></div><div class="api-field__right"><div class="api-field__description">Kubernetes defines the configuration for running the external processor as a Kubernetes container.</div></div></div>
<div class="api-field"><div class="api-field__left"><code class="api-field__name">metadataForwardingNamespaces</code><span class="api-field__badge api-field__badge--optional">optional</span><div class="api-field__type"><div>string array</div></div></div><div class="api-field__right"><div class="api-field__description">MetadataForwardingNamespaces lists the untyped dynamic metadata namespaces Envoy forwards<br>to the external processor for this Gateway's AI traffic. A BackendSecurityPolicy using<br>credentialOverride.fromDynamicMetadata receives metadata only from a namespace listed here.<br>Under Envoy Gateway's mergeGateways, every Gateway in the class shares one Envoy, so a<br>namespace any one of them declares is forwarded for all of their AI traffic.</div></div></div>
<h4 class="anchor anchorTargetStickyNavbar_Vzrq" id="gatewayconfigforwardproxy-1"><a id="github-com-envoyproxy-ai-gateway-api-v1beta1-gatewayconfigforwardproxy">GatewayConfigForwardProxy</a><a href="#gatewayconfigforwardproxy-1" class="hash-link" aria-label="Direct link to gatewayconfigforwardproxy-1" title="Direct link to gatewayconfigforwardproxy-1" translate="no">​</a></h4>
<p><strong>Appears in:</strong></p>
\<ul\>
<li class=""><a href="#github-com-envoyproxy-ai-gateway-api-v1beta1-gatewayconfigspec" class="">GatewayConfigSpec</a></li>
</ul>
<p>GatewayConfigForwardProxy configures an HTTP CONNECT forward proxy for upstream egress.</p>
<h5 class="anchor anchorTargetStickyNavbar_Vzrq" id="fields-108">Fields<a href="#fields-108" class="hash-link" aria-label="Direct link to Fields" title="Direct link to Fields" translate="no">​</a></h5>
<div class="api-field"><div class="api-field__left"><code class="api-field__name">address</code><span class="api-field__badge api-field__badge--required">required</span><div class="api-field__type"><div>string</div></div></div><div class="api-field__right"><div class="api-field__description">Address is the <code class="thin">host:port</code> of the HTTP CONNECT proxy. The host may be a hostname or an<br>IP address; the port is required. Upstream connections are tunnelled through this proxy<br>via Envoy's http_11_proxy transport socket, preserving the upstream TLS session.</div></div></div>
<h4 class="anchor anchorTargetStickyNavbar_Vzrq" id="gatewayconfigspec-1"><a id="github-com-envoyproxy-ai-gateway-api-v1beta1-gatewayconfigspec">GatewayConfigSpec</a><a href="#gatewayconfigspec-1" class="hash-link" aria-label="Direct link to gatewayconfigspec-1" title="Direct link to gatewayconfigspec-1" translate="no">​</a></h4>
<p><strong>Appears in:</strong></p>
\<ul\>
<li class=""><a href="#github-com-envoyproxy-ai-gateway-api-v1beta1-gatewayconfig" class="">GatewayConfig</a></li>
</ul>
<p>GatewayConfigSpec defines the configuration for the AI Gateway.</p>
<h5 class="anchor anchorTargetStickyNavbar_Vzrq" id="fields-109">Fields<a href="#fields-109" class="hash-link" aria-label="Direct link to Fields" title="Direct link to Fields" translate="no">​</a></h5>
<div class="api-field"><div class="api-field__left"><code class="api-field__name">extProc</code><span class="api-field__badge api-field__badge--optional">optional</span><div class="api-field__type"><div><a href="#github-com-envoyproxy-ai-gateway-api-v1beta1-gatewayconfigextproc">GatewayConfigExtProc</a></div></div></div><div class="api-field__right"><div class="api-field__description">ExtProc defines the configuration for the external processor container.</div></div></div>
<div class="api-field"><div class="api-field__left"><code class="api-field__name">globalLLMRequestCosts</code><span class="api-field__badge api-field__badge--optional">optional</span><div class="api-field__type"><div><a href="#github-com-envoyproxy-ai-gateway-api-v1beta1-llmrequestcost">LLMRequestCost</a> array</div></div></div><div class="api-field__right"><div class="api-field__description">GlobalLLMRequestCosts defines default LLM request costs that apply to all<br>routes referencing this GatewayConfig. These costs can be overridden on a<br>per-route basis via AIGatewayRoute.Spec.LLMRequestCosts.<br>When a request matches a route, the cost calculation proceeds as follows:<br> 1. If the route defines LLMRequestCosts with a matching metadataKey, use that.<br> 2. Otherwise, fall back to the global cost with that metadataKey (if defined here).<br> 3. If neither exists, the cost is not calculated for that metadataKey.<br>This allows you to define common cost formulas once at the gateway level<br>(e.g., billing_charges = input_tokens + output_tokens) and only override<br>them in specific routes when needed (e.g., premium routes with different pricing).</div></div></div>
<div class="api-field"><div class="api-field__left"><code class="api-field__name">forwardProxy</code><span class="api-field__badge api-field__badge--optional">optional</span><div class="api-field__type"><div><a href="#github-com-envoyproxy-ai-gateway-api-v1beta1-gatewayconfigforwardproxy">GatewayConfigForwardProxy</a></div></div></div><div class="api-field__right"><div class="api-field__description">ForwardProxy routes all upstream AI/LLM traffic from Gateways referencing this<br>GatewayConfig through an HTTP CONNECT forward proxy. This is intended for data planes<br>in locked-down networks where direct egress to providers is not permitted and all<br>outbound traffic must traverse a proxy.</div></div></div>
<h4 class="anchor anchorTargetStickyNavbar_Vzrq" id="gatewayconfigstatus-1"><a id="github-com-envoyproxy-ai-gateway-api-v1beta1-gatewayconfigstatus">GatewayConfigStatus</a><a href="#gatewayconfigstatus-1" class="hash-link" aria-label="Direct link to gatewayconfigstatus-1" title="Direct link to gatewayconfigstatus-1" translate="no">​</a></h4>

\<ul\>

</ul>
<p>GatewayConfigStatus defines the observed state of GatewayConfig.</p>
<h5 class="anchor anchorTargetStickyNavbar_Vzrq" id="fields-110">Fields<a href="#fields-110" class="hash-link" aria-label="Direct link to Fields" title="Direct link to Fields" translate="no">​</a></h5>
<div class="api-field"><div class="api-field__left"><code class="api-field__name">conditions</code><span class="api-field__badge api-field__badge--optional">optional</span><div class="api-field__type"><div><a href="https://kubernetes.io/docs/reference/generated/kubernetes-api/v1.29/#condition-v1-meta">Condition</a> array</div></div></div><div class="api-field__right"><div class="api-field__description">Conditions describe the current conditions of the GatewayConfig.</div></div></div>
<h4 class="anchor anchorTargetStickyNavbar_Vzrq" id="httpbodyfield-1"><a id="github-com-envoyproxy-ai-gateway-api-v1beta1-httpbodyfield">HTTPBodyField</a><a href="#httpbodyfield-1" class="hash-link" aria-label="Direct link to httpbodyfield-1" title="Direct link to httpbodyfield-1" translate="no">​</a></h4>
<p><strong>Appears in:</strong></p>
\<ul\>
<li class=""><a href="#github-com-envoyproxy-ai-gateway-api-v1beta1-httpbodymutation" class="">HTTPBodyMutation</a></li>
</ul>
<p>HTTPBodyField represents a JSON field name and value for body mutation</p>
<h5 class="anchor anchorTargetStickyNavbar_Vzrq" id="fields-111">Fields<a href="#fields-111" class="hash-link" aria-label="Direct link to Fields" title="Direct link to Fields" translate="no">​</a></h5>
<div class="api-field"><div class="api-field__left"><code class="api-field__name">path</code><span class="api-field__badge api-field__badge--required">required</span><div class="api-field__type"><div>string</div></div></div><div class="api-field__right"><div class="api-field__description">Path is the top-level field name to set in the request body.<br>Examples: <code class="thin">service_tier</code>, <code class="thin">max_tokens</code>, <code class="thin">temperature</code></div></div></div>
<div class="api-field"><div class="api-field__left"><code class="api-field__name">value</code><span class="api-field__badge api-field__badge--required">required</span><div class="api-field__type"><div>string</div></div></div><div class="api-field__right"><div class="api-field__description">Value is the JSON value to set at the specified field. This can be any valid JSON value:<br>string, number, boolean, object, array, or null.<br>The value will be parsed as JSON and inserted at the specified field.<br>Examples:<br>  - <code class="thin">\</code>scale\`<code class="thin"> (string)<br>  - </code>42<code class="thin"> (number)<br>  - </code>true<code class="thin"> (boolean)<br>  - </code>\{\<code class="thin">key\</code>: \<code class="thin">value\</code>\}<code class="thin"> (object)<br>  - </code>[1, 2, 3]<code class="thin"> (array)<br>  - </code>null` (null)</div></div></div>
<h4 class="anchor anchorTargetStickyNavbar_Vzrq" id="httpbodymutation-1"><a id="github-com-envoyproxy-ai-gateway-api-v1beta1-httpbodymutation">HTTPBodyMutation</a><a href="#httpbodymutation-1" class="hash-link" aria-label="Direct link to httpbodymutation-1" title="Direct link to httpbodymutation-1" translate="no">​</a></h4>
<p><strong>Appears in:</strong></p>
\<ul\>
<li class=""><a href="#github-com-envoyproxy-ai-gateway-api-v1beta1-aigatewayrouterulebackendref" class="">AIGatewayRouteRuleBackendRef</a></li>
<li class=""><a href="#github-com-envoyproxy-ai-gateway-api-v1beta1-aiservicebackendspec" class="">AIServiceBackendSpec</a></li>
</ul>
<p>HTTPBodyMutation defines the mutation of HTTP request body JSON fields that will be applied to the request</p>
<h5 class="anchor anchorTargetStickyNavbar_Vzrq" id="fields-112">Fields<a href="#fields-112" class="hash-link" aria-label="Direct link to Fields" title="Direct link to Fields" translate="no">​</a></h5>
<div class="api-field"><div class="api-field__left"><code class="api-field__name">set</code><span class="api-field__badge api-field__badge--optional">optional</span><div class="api-field__type"><div><a href="#github-com-envoyproxy-ai-gateway-api-v1beta1-httpbodyfield">HTTPBodyField</a> array</div></div></div><div class="api-field__right"><div class="api-field__description">Set overwrites/adds the request body with the given JSON field (name, value)<br>before sending to the backend. Only top-level fields are currently supported.<br>Input:<br>  \{<br>    <code class="thin">model</code>: <code class="thin">gpt-4</code>,<br>    <code class="thin">service_tier</code>: <code class="thin">default</code><br>  \}<br>Config:<br>  set:<br>  - path: <code class="thin">service_tier</code><br>    value: <code class="thin">scale</code><br>Output:<br>  \{<br>    <code class="thin">model</code>: <code class="thin">gpt-4</code>,<br>    <code class="thin">service_tier</code>: <code class="thin">scale</code><br>  \}</div></div></div>
<div class="api-field"><div class="api-field__left"><code class="api-field__name">remove</code><span class="api-field__badge api-field__badge--optional">optional</span><div class="api-field__type"><div>string array</div></div></div><div class="api-field__right"><div class="api-field__description">Remove the given JSON field(s) from the HTTP request body before sending to the backend.<br>The value of Remove is a list of top-level field names to remove.<br>Input:<br>  \{<br>    <code class="thin">model</code>: <code class="thin">gpt-4</code>,<br>    <code class="thin">service_tier</code>: <code class="thin">default</code>,<br>    <code class="thin">internal_flag</code>: true<br>  \}<br>Config:<br>  remove: [<code class="thin">service_tier</code>, <code class="thin">internal_flag</code>]<br>Output:<br>  \{<br>    <code class="thin">model</code>: <code class="thin">gpt-4</code><br>  \}</div></div></div>
<h4 class="anchor anchorTargetStickyNavbar_Vzrq" id="httpheadermutation-1"><a id="github-com-envoyproxy-ai-gateway-api-v1beta1-httpheadermutation">HTTPHeaderMutation</a><a href="#httpheadermutation-1" class="hash-link" aria-label="Direct link to httpheadermutation-1" title="Direct link to httpheadermutation-1" translate="no">​</a></h4>
<p><strong>Appears in:</strong></p>
\<ul\>
<li class=""><a href="#github-com-envoyproxy-ai-gateway-api-v1beta1-aigatewayrouterulebackendref" class="">AIGatewayRouteRuleBackendRef</a></li>
<li class=""><a href="#github-com-envoyproxy-ai-gateway-api-v1beta1-aiservicebackendspec" class="">AIServiceBackendSpec</a></li>
</ul>
<p>HTTPHeaderMutation defines the mutation of HTTP headers that will be applied to the request</p>
<h5 class="anchor anchorTargetStickyNavbar_Vzrq" id="fields-113">Fields<a href="#fields-113" class="hash-link" aria-label="Direct link to Fields" title="Direct link to Fields" translate="no">​</a></h5>
<div class="api-field"><div class="api-field__left"><code class="api-field__name">set</code><span class="api-field__badge api-field__badge--optional">optional</span><div class="api-field__type"><div><a href="https://gateway-api.sigs.k8s.io/reference/spec/?h=httproutetimeouts#httpheader">HTTPHeader</a> array</div></div></div><div class="api-field__right"><div class="api-field__description">Set overwrites/adds the request with the given header (name, value)<br>before the action.<br>Input:<br>  GET /foo HTTP/1.1<br>  my-header: foo<br>Config:<br>  set:<br>  - name: <code class="thin">my-header</code><br>    value: <code class="thin">bar</code><br>Output:<br>  GET /foo HTTP/1.1<br>  my-header: bar</div></div></div>
<div class="api-field"><div class="api-field__left"><code class="api-field__name">remove</code><span class="api-field__badge api-field__badge--optional">optional</span><div class="api-field__type"><div>string array</div></div></div><div class="api-field__right"><div class="api-field__description">Remove the given header(s) from the HTTP request before the action. The<br>value of Remove is a list of HTTP header names. Note that the header<br>names are case-insensitive (see<br>https://datatracker.ietf.org/doc/html/rfc2616#section-4.2).<br>Input:<br>  GET /foo HTTP/1.1<br>  my-header1: foo<br>  my-header2: bar<br>  my-header3: baz<br>Config:<br>  remove: [<code class="thin">my-header1</code>, <code class="thin">my-header3</code>]<br>Output:<br>  GET /foo HTTP/1.1<br>  my-header2: bar</div></div></div>
<h4 class="anchor anchorTargetStickyNavbar_Vzrq" id="httpheadervaluefilter"><a id="github-com-envoyproxy-ai-gateway-api-v1beta1-httpheadervaluefilter">HTTPHeaderValueFilter</a><a href="#httpheadervaluefilter" class="hash-link" aria-label="Direct link to httpheadervaluefilter" title="Direct link to httpheadervaluefilter" translate="no">​</a></h4>
<p><strong>Appears in:</strong></p>
\<ul\>
<li class=""><a href="#github-com-envoyproxy-ai-gateway-api-v1beta1-aiservicebackendspec" class="">AIServiceBackendSpec</a></li>
</ul>
<p>HTTPHeaderValueFilter filters individual values out of a multi-valued request header before the
request is forwarded to this backend.</p>
<p>This drops (or restricts to) specific values from a comma-separated header while forwarding the
rest untouched, so one unsupported value does not fail the whole request.</p>
<p>Only comma-delimited headers are supported. Values are matched exactly, after surrounding
whitespace is trimmed. To remove a header entirely, use HeaderMutation.Remove instead.</p>
<h5 class="anchor anchorTargetStickyNavbar_Vzrq" id="fields-114">Fields<a href="#fields-114" class="hash-link" aria-label="Direct link to Fields" title="Direct link to Fields" translate="no">​</a></h5>
<div class="api-field"><div class="api-field__left"><code class="api-field__name">name</code><span class="api-field__badge api-field__badge--required">required</span><div class="api-field__type"><div><a href="#sigs-k8s-io-gateway-api-apis-v1-httpheadername">HTTPHeaderName</a></div></div></div><div class="api-field__right"><div class="api-field__description">Name is the name of the header whose values are filtered. Header names are<br>case-insensitive (see https://datatracker.ietf.org/doc/html/rfc2616#section-4.2).</div></div></div>
<div class="api-field"><div class="api-field__left"><code class="api-field__name">mode</code><span class="api-field__badge api-field__badge--optional">optional</span><div class="api-field__type"><div><a href="#github-com-envoyproxy-ai-gateway-api-v1beta1-httpheadervaluefiltermode">HTTPHeaderValueFilterMode</a></div></div></div><div class="api-field__right"><div class="api-field__default">Denylist</div><div class="api-field__description">Mode selects the filtering semantics. Defaults to Denylist.</div></div></div>
<div class="api-field"><div class="api-field__left"><code class="api-field__name">values</code><span class="api-field__badge api-field__badge--optional">optional</span><div class="api-field__type"><div>string array</div></div></div><div class="api-field__right"><div class="api-field__description">Values is the list of header values to drop (Denylist) or keep (Allowlist).<br>An empty list leaves the header unchanged.</div></div></div>
<h4 class="anchor anchorTargetStickyNavbar_Vzrq" id="httpheadervaluefiltermode"><a id="github-com-envoyproxy-ai-gateway-api-v1beta1-httpheadervaluefiltermode">HTTPHeaderValueFilterMode</a><a href="#httpheadervaluefiltermode" class="hash-link" aria-label="Direct link to httpheadervaluefiltermode" title="Direct link to httpheadervaluefiltermode" translate="no">​</a></h4>
<p><strong>Underlying type:</strong> string</p>
<p><strong>Appears in:</strong></p>
\<ul\>
<li class=""><a href="#github-com-envoyproxy-ai-gateway-api-v1beta1-httpheadervaluefilter" class="">HTTPHeaderValueFilter</a></li>
</ul>
<p>HTTPHeaderValueFilterMode specifies the filtering semantics of an HTTPHeaderValueFilter.</p>
<h5 class="anchor anchorTargetStickyNavbar_Vzrq" id="possible-values-6">Possible Values<a href="#possible-values-6" class="hash-link" aria-label="Direct link to Possible Values" title="Direct link to Possible Values" translate="no">​</a></h5>
<div class="api-field" data-type="enum"><div class="api-field__left"><code class="api-field__name">Denylist</code></div><div class="api-field__right"><div class="api-field__description">HTTPHeaderValueFilterModeDenylist drops the values listed in Values, keeping everything else.<br>New values the gateway has not been configured for pass through untouched.<br></div></div></div>
<div class="api-field" data-type="enum"><div class="api-field__left"><code class="api-field__name">Allowlist</code></div><div class="api-field__right"><div class="api-field__description">HTTPHeaderValueFilterModeAllowlist keeps only the values listed in Values, dropping everything<br>else. New values the gateway has not been configured for are dropped.<br></div></div></div>
<h4 class="anchor anchorTargetStickyNavbar_Vzrq" id="jwks-1"><a id="github-com-envoyproxy-ai-gateway-api-v1beta1-jwks">JWKS</a><a href="#jwks-1" class="hash-link" aria-label="Direct link to jwks-1" title="Direct link to jwks-1" translate="no">​</a></h4>
<p><strong>Appears in:</strong></p>
\<ul\>
<li class=""><a href="#github-com-envoyproxy-ai-gateway-api-v1beta1-mcprouteoauth" class="">MCPRouteOAuth</a></li>
</ul>
<p>JWKS defines how to obtain JSON Web Key Sets (JWKS) either from a remote HTTP/HTTPS endpoint or from a local source.</p>
<h5 class="anchor anchorTargetStickyNavbar_Vzrq" id="fields-115">Fields<a href="#fields-115" class="hash-link" aria-label="Direct link to Fields" title="Direct link to Fields" translate="no">​</a></h5>
<div class="api-field"><div class="api-field__left"><code class="api-field__name">remoteJWKS</code><span class="api-field__badge api-field__badge--optional">optional</span><div class="api-field__type"><div><a href="https://gateway.envoyproxy.io/docs/api/extension_types/#remotejwks">RemoteJWKS</a></div></div></div><div class="api-field__right"><div class="api-field__description">RemoteJWKS defines how to fetch and cache JSON Web Key Sets (JWKS) from a remote<br>HTTP/HTTPS endpoint.</div></div></div>
<div class="api-field"><div class="api-field__left"><code class="api-field__name">localJWKS</code><span class="api-field__badge api-field__badge--optional">optional</span><div class="api-field__type"><div><a href="https://gateway.envoyproxy.io/docs/api/extension_types/#localjwks">LocalJWKS</a></div></div></div><div class="api-field__right"><div class="api-field__description">LocalJWKS defines how to get the JSON Web Key Sets (JWKS) from a local source.</div></div></div>
<h4 class="anchor anchorTargetStickyNavbar_Vzrq" id="jwtsource-1"><a id="github-com-envoyproxy-ai-gateway-api-v1beta1-jwtsource">JWTSource</a><a href="#jwtsource-1" class="hash-link" aria-label="Direct link to jwtsource-1" title="Direct link to jwtsource-1" translate="no">​</a></h4>
<p><strong>Appears in:</strong></p>
\<ul\>
<li class=""><a href="#github-com-envoyproxy-ai-gateway-api-v1beta1-mcpauthorizationsource" class="">MCPAuthorizationSource</a></li>
</ul>
<p>JWTSource defines the MCP authorization source for JWT tokens.
At least one of scopes or claims must be provided.
Scopes and claims are AND-ed: when both are specified, both sets must match.</p>
<h5 class="anchor anchorTargetStickyNavbar_Vzrq" id="fields-116">Fields<a href="#fields-116" class="hash-link" aria-label="Direct link to Fields" title="Direct link to Fields" translate="no">​</a></h5>
<div class="api-field"><div class="api-field__left"><code class="api-field__name">scopes</code><span class="api-field__badge api-field__badge--optional">optional</span><div class="api-field__type"><div>JWTScope array</div></div></div><div class="api-field__right"><div class="api-field__description">Scopes defines the list of JWT scopes required for the rule.<br>If multiple scopes are specified, all scopes must be present in the JWT for the rule to match.</div></div></div>
<div class="api-field"><div class="api-field__left"><code class="api-field__name">claims</code><span class="api-field__badge api-field__badge--optional">optional</span><div class="api-field__type"><div>JWTClaim array</div></div></div><div class="api-field__right"><div class="api-field__description">Claims defines the list of JWT claims required for the rule. Each claim must exist on the token<br>and have at least one of the expected values. Use to enforce tenant or subject-based access.</div></div></div>
<h4 class="anchor anchorTargetStickyNavbar_Vzrq" id="llmrequestcost-1"><a id="github-com-envoyproxy-ai-gateway-api-v1beta1-llmrequestcost">LLMRequestCost</a><a href="#llmrequestcost-1" class="hash-link" aria-label="Direct link to llmrequestcost-1" title="Direct link to llmrequestcost-1" translate="no">​</a></h4>
<p><strong>Appears in:</strong></p>
\<ul\>
<li class=""><a href="#github-com-envoyproxy-ai-gateway-api-v1beta1-aigatewayroutespec" class="">AIGatewayRouteSpec</a></li>
<li class=""><a href="#github-com-envoyproxy-ai-gateway-api-v1beta1-gatewayconfigspec" class="">GatewayConfigSpec</a></li>
</ul>
<p>LLMRequestCost configures each request cost.</p>
<h5 class="anchor anchorTargetStickyNavbar_Vzrq" id="fields-117">Fields<a href="#fields-117" class="hash-link" aria-label="Direct link to Fields" title="Direct link to Fields" translate="no">​</a></h5>
<div class="api-field"><div class="api-field__left"><code class="api-field__name">metadataKey</code><span class="api-field__badge api-field__badge--required">required</span><div class="api-field__type"><div>string</div></div></div><div class="api-field__right"><div class="api-field__description">MetadataKey is the key of the metadata to store this cost of the request.</div></div></div>
<div class="api-field"><div class="api-field__left"><code class="api-field__name">type</code><span class="api-field__badge api-field__badge--required">required</span><div class="api-field__type"><div><a href="#github-com-envoyproxy-ai-gateway-api-v1beta1-llmrequestcosttype">LLMRequestCostType</a></div></div></div><div class="api-field__right"><div class="api-field__description">Type specifies the type of the request cost. The default is <code class="thin">OutputToken</code>,<br>and it uses <code class="thin">output token</code> as the cost. The other types are <code class="thin">InputToken</code>, <code class="thin">TotalToken</code>,<br><code class="thin">CachedInputToken</code>, <code class="thin">CacheCreationInputToken</code>, <code class="thin">ReasoningToken</code>, and <code class="thin">CEL</code>.</div></div></div>
<div class="api-field"><div class="api-field__left"><code class="api-field__name">cel</code><span class="api-field__badge api-field__badge--optional">optional</span><div class="api-field__type"><div>string</div></div></div><div class="api-field__right"><div class="api-field__description">CEL is the CEL expression to calculate the cost of the request.<br>The CEL expression must return a signed or unsigned integer. If the<br>return value is negative, it will be error.<br>The expression can use the following variables:<br>	* model: the model name extracted from the request content. Type: string.<br>	* backend: the backend name in the form of <code class="thin">name.namespace</code>. Type: string.<br>	* input_tokens: the number of input tokens. Type: unsigned integer.<br>	* cached_input_tokens: the number of cached read input tokens. Type: unsigned integer.<br>	* cache_creation_input_tokens: the number of cache creation input tokens. Type: unsigned integer.<br>	* output_tokens: the number of output tokens. Type: unsigned integer.<br>	* total_tokens: the total number of tokens. Type: unsigned integer.<br>	* reasoning_tokens: the number of reasoning tokens. Type: unsigned integer.<br>For example, the following expressions are valid:<br>	* <code class="thin">model == 'llama' ?  input_tokens + output_token * 0.5 : total_tokens</code><br>	* <code class="thin">backend == 'foo.default' ?  input_tokens + output_tokens : total_tokens</code><br>	* <code class="thin">backend == 'bar.default' ?  (input_tokens - cached_input_tokens) + cached_input_tokens * 0.1 + cache_creation_input_tokens * 1.25 + output_tokens : total_tokens</code><br>	* <code class="thin">input_tokens + output_tokens + total_tokens</code><br>	* <code class="thin">input_tokens * output_tokens</code></div></div></div>
<h4 class="anchor anchorTargetStickyNavbar_Vzrq" id="llmrequestcosttype-1"><a id="github-com-envoyproxy-ai-gateway-api-v1beta1-llmrequestcosttype">LLMRequestCostType</a><a href="#llmrequestcosttype-1" class="hash-link" aria-label="Direct link to llmrequestcosttype-1" title="Direct link to llmrequestcosttype-1" translate="no">​</a></h4>
<p><strong>Underlying type:</strong> string</p>
<p><strong>Appears in:</strong></p>
\<ul\>
<li class=""><a href="#github-com-envoyproxy-ai-gateway-api-v1beta1-llmrequestcost" class="">LLMRequestCost</a></li>
</ul>
<p>LLMRequestCostType specifies the type of the LLMRequestCost.</p>
<h5 class="anchor anchorTargetStickyNavbar_Vzrq" id="possible-values-7">Possible Values<a href="#possible-values-7" class="hash-link" aria-label="Direct link to Possible Values" title="Direct link to Possible Values" translate="no">​</a></h5>
<div class="api-field" data-type="enum"><div class="api-field__left"><code class="api-field__name">InputToken</code></div><div class="api-field__right"><div class="api-field__description">LLMRequestCostTypeInputToken is the cost type of the input token.<br></div></div></div>
<div class="api-field" data-type="enum"><div class="api-field__left"><code class="api-field__name">CachedInputToken</code></div><div class="api-field__right"><div class="api-field__description">LLMRequestCostTypeCachedInputToken is the cost type of the cached input token.<br></div></div></div>
<div class="api-field" data-type="enum"><div class="api-field__left"><code class="api-field__name">CacheCreationInputToken</code></div><div class="api-field__right"><div class="api-field__description">LLMRequestCostTypeCacheCreationInputToken is the cost type of the cached input token.<br></div></div></div>
<div class="api-field" data-type="enum"><div class="api-field__left"><code class="api-field__name">OutputToken</code></div><div class="api-field__right"><div class="api-field__description">LLMRequestCostTypeOutputToken is the cost type of the output token.<br></div></div></div>
<div class="api-field" data-type="enum"><div class="api-field__left"><code class="api-field__name">TotalToken</code></div><div class="api-field__right"><div class="api-field__description">LLMRequestCostTypeTotalToken is the cost type of the total token.<br></div></div></div>
<div class="api-field" data-type="enum"><div class="api-field__left"><code class="api-field__name">ReasoningToken</code></div><div class="api-field__right"><div class="api-field__description">LLMRequestCostTypeReasoningToken is the cost type of the reasoning token.<br></div></div></div>
<div class="api-field" data-type="enum"><div class="api-field__left"><code class="api-field__name">CEL</code></div><div class="api-field__right"><div class="api-field__description">LLMRequestCostTypeCEL is for calculating the cost using the CEL expression.<br></div></div></div>
<h4 class="anchor anchorTargetStickyNavbar_Vzrq" id="mcpauthorizationsource-1"><a id="github-com-envoyproxy-ai-gateway-api-v1beta1-mcpauthorizationsource">MCPAuthorizationSource</a><a href="#mcpauthorizationsource-1" class="hash-link" aria-label="Direct link to mcpauthorizationsource-1" title="Direct link to mcpauthorizationsource-1" translate="no">​</a></h4>
<p><strong>Appears in:</strong></p>
\<ul\>
<li class=""><a href="#github-com-envoyproxy-ai-gateway-api-v1beta1-mcprouteauthorizationrule" class="">MCPRouteAuthorizationRule</a></li>
</ul>
<p>MCPAuthorizationSource defines the source of an authorization rule.</p>
<h5 class="anchor anchorTargetStickyNavbar_Vzrq" id="fields-118">Fields<a href="#fields-118" class="hash-link" aria-label="Direct link to Fields" title="Direct link to Fields" translate="no">​</a></h5>
<div class="api-field"><div class="api-field__left"><code class="api-field__name">jwt</code><span class="api-field__badge api-field__badge--required">required</span><div class="api-field__type"><div><a href="#github-com-envoyproxy-ai-gateway-api-v1beta1-jwtsource">JWTSource</a></div></div></div><div class="api-field__right"><div class="api-field__description">JWT defines the JWT scopes required for this rule to match.</div></div></div>
<h4 class="anchor anchorTargetStickyNavbar_Vzrq" id="mcpauthorizationtarget-1"><a id="github-com-envoyproxy-ai-gateway-api-v1beta1-mcpauthorizationtarget">MCPAuthorizationTarget</a><a href="#mcpauthorizationtarget-1" class="hash-link" aria-label="Direct link to mcpauthorizationtarget-1" title="Direct link to mcpauthorizationtarget-1" translate="no">​</a></h4>
<p><strong>Appears in:</strong></p>
\<ul\>
<li class=""><a href="#github-com-envoyproxy-ai-gateway-api-v1beta1-mcprouteauthorizationrule" class="">MCPRouteAuthorizationRule</a></li>
</ul>
<p>MCPAuthorizationTarget defines the target of an authorization rule.</p>
<h5 class="anchor anchorTargetStickyNavbar_Vzrq" id="fields-119">Fields<a href="#fields-119" class="hash-link" aria-label="Direct link to Fields" title="Direct link to Fields" translate="no">​</a></h5>
<div class="api-field"><div class="api-field__left"><code class="api-field__name">tools</code><span class="api-field__badge api-field__badge--required">required</span><div class="api-field__type"><div><a href="#github-com-envoyproxy-ai-gateway-api-v1beta1-toolcall">ToolCall</a> array</div></div></div><div class="api-field__right"><div class="api-field__description">Tools defines the list of tools this rule applies to.</div></div></div>
<h4 class="anchor anchorTargetStickyNavbar_Vzrq" id="mcpbackendapikey-1"><a id="github-com-envoyproxy-ai-gateway-api-v1beta1-mcpbackendapikey">MCPBackendAPIKey</a><a href="#mcpbackendapikey-1" class="hash-link" aria-label="Direct link to mcpbackendapikey-1" title="Direct link to mcpbackendapikey-1" translate="no">​</a></h4>
<p><strong>Appears in:</strong></p>
\<ul\>
<li class=""><a href="#github-com-envoyproxy-ai-gateway-api-v1beta1-mcpbackendsecuritypolicy" class="">MCPBackendSecurityPolicy</a></li>
</ul>
<p>MCPBackendAPIKey defines the configuration for the API Key Authentication to a backend.
When both <code>header</code> and <code>queryParam</code> are unspecified, the API key will be injected into the "Authorization" header by default.</p>
<h5 class="anchor anchorTargetStickyNavbar_Vzrq" id="fields-120">Fields<a href="#fields-120" class="hash-link" aria-label="Direct link to Fields" title="Direct link to Fields" translate="no">​</a></h5>
<div class="api-field"><div class="api-field__left"><code class="api-field__name">secretRef</code><span class="api-field__badge api-field__badge--optional">optional</span><div class="api-field__type"><div><a href="https://gateway-api.sigs.k8s.io/references/spec/#gateway.networking.k8s.io/v1.SecretObjectReference">SecretObjectReference</a></div></div></div><div class="api-field__right"><div class="api-field__description">secretRef is the Kubernetes secret which contains the API keys.<br>The key of the secret should be <code class="thin">apiKey</code>.</div></div></div>
<div class="api-field"><div class="api-field__left"><code class="api-field__name">inline</code><span class="api-field__badge api-field__badge--optional">optional</span><div class="api-field__type"><div>string</div></div></div><div class="api-field__right"><div class="api-field__description">Inline contains the API key as an inline string.</div></div></div>
<div class="api-field"><div class="api-field__left"><code class="api-field__name">header</code><span class="api-field__badge api-field__badge--optional">optional</span><div class="api-field__type"><div>string</div></div></div><div class="api-field__right"><div class="api-field__description">Header is the HTTP header to inject the API key into. If not specified,<br>defaults to <code class="thin">Authorization</code>.<br>When the header is <code class="thin">Authorization</code>, the injected header value will be<br>prefixed with <code class="thin">Bearer </code>.<br>Either one of Header or QueryParam can be specified to inject the API key.</div></div></div>
<div class="api-field"><div class="api-field__left"><code class="api-field__name">queryParam</code><span class="api-field__badge api-field__badge--optional">optional</span><div class="api-field__type"><div>string</div></div></div><div class="api-field__right"><div class="api-field__description">QueryParam is the HTTP query parameter to inject the API key into.<br>For example, if QueryParam is set to <code class="thin">api_key</code>, and the API key is <code class="thin">mysecretkey</code>, the request URL will be modified to include<br><code class="thin">?api_key=mysecretkey</code>.<br>Either one of Header or QueryParam can be specified to inject the API key.<br>Note: Embedding credentials in URLs (including query parameters) is generally not recommended because URLs can be exposed in logs<br>and intermediary systems; prefer header-based injection when possible.</div></div></div>
<div class="api-field"><div class="api-field__left"><code class="api-field__name">injectionPolicy</code><span class="api-field__badge api-field__badge--optional">optional</span><div class="api-field__type"><div><a href="#github-com-envoyproxy-ai-gateway-api-v1beta1-mcpbackendapikeyinjectionpolicy">MCPBackendAPIKeyInjectionPolicy</a></div></div></div><div class="api-field__right"><div class="api-field__default">Always</div><div class="api-field__description">InjectionPolicy controls when the configured API key is written onto the target header.<br>Always (the default) writes the credential, replacing any existing value, including<br>values populated by forwardHeaders.<br>IfNotPresent writes the API key only if the target header is absent, so a<br>caller-supplied token forwarded onto the same header is preserved.<br>InjectionPolicy applies only to header injection. It must not be set to IfNotPresent<br>when queryParam is used, because query-parameter injection always rewrites the backend URL.</div></div></div>
<h4 class="anchor anchorTargetStickyNavbar_Vzrq" id="mcpbackendapikeyinjectionpolicy"><a id="github-com-envoyproxy-ai-gateway-api-v1beta1-mcpbackendapikeyinjectionpolicy">MCPBackendAPIKeyInjectionPolicy</a><a href="#mcpbackendapikeyinjectionpolicy" class="hash-link" aria-label="Direct link to mcpbackendapikeyinjectionpolicy" title="Direct link to mcpbackendapikeyinjectionpolicy" translate="no">​</a></h4>
<p><strong>Underlying type:</strong> string</p>
<p><strong>Appears in:</strong></p>
\<ul\>
<li class=""><a href="#github-com-envoyproxy-ai-gateway-api-v1beta1-mcpbackendapikey" class="">MCPBackendAPIKey</a></li>
</ul>
<p>MCPBackendAPIKeyInjectionPolicy controls when the configured API key is written onto the request.</p>
<h5 class="anchor anchorTargetStickyNavbar_Vzrq" id="possible-values-8">Possible Values<a href="#possible-values-8" class="hash-link" aria-label="Direct link to Possible Values" title="Direct link to Possible Values" translate="no">​</a></h5>
<div class="api-field" data-type="enum"><div class="api-field__left"><code class="api-field__name">Always</code></div><div class="api-field__right"><div class="api-field__description">MCPBackendAPIKeyInjectionAlways writes the configured key onto the target header,<br>replacing any existing value. This is the default and preserves existing behavior.<br></div></div></div>
<div class="api-field" data-type="enum"><div class="api-field__left"><code class="api-field__name">IfNotPresent</code></div><div class="api-field__right"><div class="api-field__description">MCPBackendAPIKeyInjectionIfNotPresent writes the configured key only when the target<br>header is absent, so a value already set (for example by forwardHeaders) is preserved.<br></div></div></div>
<h4 class="anchor anchorTargetStickyNavbar_Vzrq" id="mcpbackendsecuritypolicy-1"><a id="github-com-envoyproxy-ai-gateway-api-v1beta1-mcpbackendsecuritypolicy">MCPBackendSecurityPolicy</a><a href="#mcpbackendsecuritypolicy-1" class="hash-link" aria-label="Direct link to mcpbackendsecuritypolicy-1" title="Direct link to mcpbackendsecuritypolicy-1" translate="no">​</a></h4>
<p><strong>Appears in:</strong></p>
\<ul\>
<li class=""><a href="#github-com-envoyproxy-ai-gateway-api-v1beta1-mcproutebackendref" class="">MCPRouteBackendRef</a></li>
</ul>
<p>MCPBackendSecurityPolicy defines the security policy for a backend MCP server.</p>
<h5 class="anchor anchorTargetStickyNavbar_Vzrq" id="fields-121">Fields<a href="#fields-121" class="hash-link" aria-label="Direct link to Fields" title="Direct link to Fields" translate="no">​</a></h5>
<div class="api-field"><div class="api-field__left"><code class="api-field__name">apiKey</code><span class="api-field__badge api-field__badge--optional">optional</span><div class="api-field__type"><div><a href="#github-com-envoyproxy-ai-gateway-api-v1beta1-mcpbackendapikey">MCPBackendAPIKey</a></div></div></div><div class="api-field__right"><div class="api-field__description">APIKey is a mechanism to access a backend. The API key will be injected into the request headers.</div></div></div>
<h4 class="anchor anchorTargetStickyNavbar_Vzrq" id="mcpbackendselector-1"><a id="github-com-envoyproxy-ai-gateway-api-v1beta1-mcpbackendselector">MCPBackendSelector</a><a href="#mcpbackendselector-1" class="hash-link" aria-label="Direct link to mcpbackendselector-1" title="Direct link to mcpbackendselector-1" translate="no">​</a></h4>
<p><strong>Appears in:</strong></p>
\<ul\>
<li class=""><a href="#github-com-envoyproxy-ai-gateway-api-v1beta1-mcproutespec" class="">MCPRouteSpec</a></li>
</ul>
<p>MCPBackendSelector defines which of a MCPRoute&#x27;s backends a request may fan out to.</p>
<h5 class="anchor anchorTargetStickyNavbar_Vzrq" id="fields-122">Fields<a href="#fields-122" class="hash-link" aria-label="Direct link to Fields" title="Direct link to Fields" translate="no">​</a></h5>
<div class="api-field"><div class="api-field__left"><code class="api-field__name">defaultAction</code><span class="api-field__badge api-field__badge--optional">optional</span><div class="api-field__type"><div><a href="#github-com-envoyproxy-gateway-api-v1alpha1-authorizationaction">AuthorizationAction</a></div></div></div><div class="api-field__right"><div class="api-field__default">Deny</div><div class="api-field__description">DefaultAction is the action to take when no rules match. If unspecified, defaults to Deny.</div></div></div>
<div class="api-field"><div class="api-field__left"><code class="api-field__name">rules</code><span class="api-field__badge api-field__badge--optional">optional</span><div class="api-field__type"><div><a href="#github-com-envoyproxy-ai-gateway-api-v1beta1-mcpbackendselectorrule">MCPBackendSelectorRule</a> array</div></div></div><div class="api-field__right"><div class="api-field__description">Rules defines a list of backend selection rules.<br>These rules are evaluated in order, the first matching rule will be applied,<br>and the rest will be skipped.<br>If no rules are defined, DefaultAction is applied to every candidate backend<br>(defaults to Deny).</div></div></div>
<h4 class="anchor anchorTargetStickyNavbar_Vzrq" id="mcpbackendselectorrule-1"><a id="github-com-envoyproxy-ai-gateway-api-v1beta1-mcpbackendselectorrule">MCPBackendSelectorRule</a><a href="#mcpbackendselectorrule-1" class="hash-link" aria-label="Direct link to mcpbackendselectorrule-1" title="Direct link to mcpbackendselectorrule-1" translate="no">​</a></h4>
<p><strong>Appears in:</strong></p>
\<ul\>
<li class=""><a href="#github-com-envoyproxy-ai-gateway-api-v1beta1-mcpbackendselector" class="">MCPBackendSelector</a></li>
</ul>
<p>MCPBackendSelectorRule defines a single backend selection rule.</p>
<h5 class="anchor anchorTargetStickyNavbar_Vzrq" id="fields-123">Fields<a href="#fields-123" class="hash-link" aria-label="Direct link to Fields" title="Direct link to Fields" translate="no">​</a></h5>
<div class="api-field"><div class="api-field__left"><code class="api-field__name">cel</code><span class="api-field__badge api-field__badge--optional">optional</span><div class="api-field__type"><div>string</div></div></div><div class="api-field__right"><div class="api-field__description">CEL specifies a Common Expression Language (CEL) expression, evaluated once for<br>each backend already listed in backendRefs. It does not parse a list of backends<br>out of the request. Each evaluation binds request.mcp.backend to the name of the<br>one candidate backend under test, so the expression should answer <code class="thin">is this backend<br>allowed</code>, not <code class="thin">which backends should be used</code>. The expression must return a<br>boolean; evaluation errors or non-boolean results are treated as <code class="thin">no match</code>. A<br>match means the rule applies, and Action below decides whether that's an Allow or<br>a Deny for that backend.<br>Example CEL expressions:<br>	* <code class="thin">request.mcp.backend in request.auth.jwt.claims.mcp_backends</code><br>	* <code class="thin">(</code>,<code class="thin"> + request.headers[</code>x-ai-eg-mcp-backend-subset<code class="thin">] + </code>,<code class="thin">).contains(</code>,<code class="thin"> + request.mcp.backend + </code>,<code class="thin">)</code><br>Available attributes are the same as documented on MCPRouteAuthorizationRule.CEL,<br>except request.mcp.method, request.mcp.tool, and request.mcp.params are not<br>populated (no MCP method has been selected yet at backend-selection time).<br>Note: The CEL expression support is experimental, and the attributes<br>available to the expression may change in future releases.</div></div></div>
<div class="api-field"><div class="api-field__left"><code class="api-field__name">action</code><span class="api-field__badge api-field__badge--optional">optional</span><div class="api-field__type"><div><a href="#github-com-envoyproxy-gateway-api-v1alpha1-authorizationaction">AuthorizationAction</a></div></div></div><div class="api-field__right"><div class="api-field__default">Allow</div><div class="api-field__description">Action is the decision for matching backends. If unspecified, defaults to Allow.</div></div></div>
<h4 class="anchor anchorTargetStickyNavbar_Vzrq" id="mcpheaderforward-1"><a id="github-com-envoyproxy-ai-gateway-api-v1beta1-mcpheaderforward">MCPHeaderForward</a><a href="#mcpheaderforward-1" class="hash-link" aria-label="Direct link to mcpheaderforward-1" title="Direct link to mcpheaderforward-1" translate="no">​</a></h4>
<p><strong>Appears in:</strong></p>
\<ul\>
<li class=""><a href="#github-com-envoyproxy-ai-gateway-api-v1beta1-mcproutebackendref" class="">MCPRouteBackendRef</a></li>
</ul>
<p>MCPHeaderForward specifies a header to extract from the incoming request and forward to a backend.</p>
<h5 class="anchor anchorTargetStickyNavbar_Vzrq" id="fields-124">Fields<a href="#fields-124" class="hash-link" aria-label="Direct link to Fields" title="Direct link to Fields" translate="no">​</a></h5>
<div class="api-field"><div class="api-field__left"><code class="api-field__name">name</code><span class="api-field__badge api-field__badge--required">required</span><div class="api-field__type"><div>string</div></div></div><div class="api-field__right"><div class="api-field__description">Name is the header name to extract from the incoming client request.</div></div></div>
<div class="api-field"><div class="api-field__left"><code class="api-field__name">backendHeader</code><span class="api-field__badge api-field__badge--optional">optional</span><div class="api-field__type"><div>string</div></div></div><div class="api-field__right"><div class="api-field__description">BackendHeader is the header name to use when forwarding to the backend.<br>If not specified, the original header name is used.</div></div></div>
<h4 class="anchor anchorTargetStickyNavbar_Vzrq" id="mcppromptfilter"><a id="github-com-envoyproxy-ai-gateway-api-v1beta1-mcppromptfilter">MCPPromptFilter</a><a href="#mcppromptfilter" class="hash-link" aria-label="Direct link to mcppromptfilter" title="Direct link to mcppromptfilter" translate="no">​</a></h4>
<p><strong>Appears in:</strong></p>
\<ul\>
<li class=""><a href="#github-com-envoyproxy-ai-gateway-api-v1beta1-mcproutebackendref" class="">MCPRouteBackendRef</a></li>
</ul>
<p>MCPPromptFilter filters prompts using include and exclude patterns with exact matches or regular expressions.
Exclude rules take precedence over include rules (deny-wins). When both include and exclude are specified,
a prompt must match an include rule AND not match any exclude rule to be allowed.</p>
<h5 class="anchor anchorTargetStickyNavbar_Vzrq" id="fields-125">Fields<a href="#fields-125" class="hash-link" aria-label="Direct link to Fields" title="Direct link to Fields" translate="no">​</a></h5>
<div class="api-field"><div class="api-field__left"><code class="api-field__name">include</code><span class="api-field__badge api-field__badge--optional">optional</span><div class="api-field__type"><div>string array</div></div></div><div class="api-field__right"><div class="api-field__description">Include is a list of prompt names to include. Only the specified prompts will be available.</div></div></div>
<div class="api-field"><div class="api-field__left"><code class="api-field__name">includeRegex</code><span class="api-field__badge api-field__badge--optional">optional</span><div class="api-field__type"><div>string array</div></div></div><div class="api-field__right"><div class="api-field__description">IncludeRegex is a list of RE2-compatible regular expressions that, when matched, include the prompt.<br>Only prompts matching these patterns will be available.</div></div></div>
<div class="api-field"><div class="api-field__left"><code class="api-field__name">exclude</code><span class="api-field__badge api-field__badge--optional">optional</span><div class="api-field__type"><div>string array</div></div></div><div class="api-field__right"><div class="api-field__description">Exclude is a list of prompt names to exclude. The specified prompts will not be available.<br>Exclude rules take precedence over include rules.</div></div></div>
<div class="api-field"><div class="api-field__left"><code class="api-field__name">excludeRegex</code><span class="api-field__badge api-field__badge--optional">optional</span><div class="api-field__type"><div>string array</div></div></div><div class="api-field__right"><div class="api-field__description">ExcludeRegex is a list of RE2-compatible regular expressions that, when matched, exclude the prompt.<br>Prompts matching these patterns will not be available. Exclude rules take precedence over include rules.</div></div></div>
<h4 class="anchor anchorTargetStickyNavbar_Vzrq" id="mcprouteauthorization-1"><a id="github-com-envoyproxy-ai-gateway-api-v1beta1-mcprouteauthorization">MCPRouteAuthorization</a><a href="#mcprouteauthorization-1" class="hash-link" aria-label="Direct link to mcprouteauthorization-1" title="Direct link to mcprouteauthorization-1" translate="no">​</a></h4>
<p><strong>Appears in:</strong></p>
\<ul\>
<li class=""><a href="#github-com-envoyproxy-ai-gateway-api-v1beta1-mcproutesecuritypolicy" class="">MCPRouteSecurityPolicy</a></li>
</ul>
<p>MCPRouteAuthorization defines the authorization configuration for a MCPRoute.</p>
<h5 class="anchor anchorTargetStickyNavbar_Vzrq" id="fields-126">Fields<a href="#fields-126" class="hash-link" aria-label="Direct link to Fields" title="Direct link to Fields" translate="no">​</a></h5>
<div class="api-field"><div class="api-field__left"><code class="api-field__name">defaultAction</code><span class="api-field__badge api-field__badge--optional">optional</span><div class="api-field__type"><div><a href="#github-com-envoyproxy-gateway-api-v1alpha1-authorizationaction">AuthorizationAction</a></div></div></div><div class="api-field__right"><div class="api-field__default">Deny</div><div class="api-field__description">DefaultAction is the action to take when no rules match. If unspecified, defaults to Deny.</div></div></div>
<div class="api-field"><div class="api-field__left"><code class="api-field__name">rules</code><span class="api-field__badge api-field__badge--optional">optional</span><div class="api-field__type"><div><a href="#github-com-envoyproxy-ai-gateway-api-v1beta1-mcprouteauthorizationrule">MCPRouteAuthorizationRule</a> array</div></div></div><div class="api-field__right"><div class="api-field__description">Rules defines a list of authorization rules.<br>These rules are evaluated in order, the first matching rule will be applied,<br>and the rest will be skipped.<br>If no rules are defined, the default action will be applied to all requests.</div></div></div>
<h4 class="anchor anchorTargetStickyNavbar_Vzrq" id="mcprouteauthorizationrule-1"><a id="github-com-envoyproxy-ai-gateway-api-v1beta1-mcprouteauthorizationrule">MCPRouteAuthorizationRule</a><a href="#mcprouteauthorizationrule-1" class="hash-link" aria-label="Direct link to mcprouteauthorizationrule-1" title="Direct link to mcprouteauthorizationrule-1" translate="no">​</a></h4>
<p><strong>Appears in:</strong></p>
\<ul\>
<li class=""><a href="#github-com-envoyproxy-ai-gateway-api-v1beta1-mcprouteauthorization" class="">MCPRouteAuthorization</a></li>
</ul>
<p>MCPRouteAuthorizationRule defines an authorization rule for MCPRoute based on the MCP authorization spec.
Reference: <a href="https://modelcontextprotocol.io/specification/draft/basic/authorization#scope-challenge-handling" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" class="">https://modelcontextprotocol.io/specification/draft/basic/authorization#scope-challenge-handling</a></p>
<h5 class="anchor anchorTargetStickyNavbar_Vzrq" id="fields-127">Fields<a href="#fields-127" class="hash-link" aria-label="Direct link to Fields" title="Direct link to Fields" translate="no">​</a></h5>
<div class="api-field"><div class="api-field__left"><code class="api-field__name">source</code><span class="api-field__badge api-field__badge--required">required</span><div class="api-field__type"><div><a href="#github-com-envoyproxy-ai-gateway-api-v1beta1-mcpauthorizationsource">MCPAuthorizationSource</a></div></div></div><div class="api-field__right"><div class="api-field__description">Source defines the authorization source for this rule.<br>If not specified, the rule will match all sources.</div></div></div>
<div class="api-field"><div class="api-field__left"><code class="api-field__name">target</code><span class="api-field__badge api-field__badge--required">required</span><div class="api-field__type"><div><a href="#github-com-envoyproxy-ai-gateway-api-v1beta1-mcpauthorizationtarget">MCPAuthorizationTarget</a></div></div></div><div class="api-field__right"><div class="api-field__description">Target defines the authorization target for this rule.<br>If not specified, the rule will match all targets.</div></div></div>
<div class="api-field"><div class="api-field__left"><code class="api-field__name">cel</code><span class="api-field__badge api-field__badge--optional">optional</span><div class="api-field__type"><div>string</div></div></div><div class="api-field__right"><div class="api-field__description">CEL specifies a Common Expression Language (CEL) expression evaluated for this rule.<br>The expression must return a boolean; evaluation errors or non-boolean results<br>are treated as <code class="thin">no match</code>.<br>Example CEL expressions:<br>	* <code class="thin">request.method == </code>POST`<code class="thin"><br>	* </code>request.headers[<code class="thin">x-custom-header</code>] == <code class="thin">AllowedValue</code><code class="thin"><br>	* </code>request.mcp.tool in [<code class="thin">toolA</code>, <code class="thin">toolB</code>]<code class="thin"><br>Available attributes in the CEL expression:<br>	* request.method: HTTP method such as GET or POST. Type: string.<br>	* request.headers: map of headers with lowercased keys, first value only. Type: map[string]string.<br>	* request.headers_all: map of headers with lowercased keys, all values. Type: map[string][]string.<br>	* request.path: request path such as /mcp. Type: string.<br>	* request.auth.jwt.claims: JWT claims when a bearer JWT is present. Type: map[string]any.<br>	* request.auth.jwt.scopes: JWT scopes when a bearer JWT is present. Type: []string.<br>	* request.mcp.method: MCP method such as tools/list or tools/call. Type: string.<br>	* request.mcp.backend: upstream backend name (for example, </code>kiwi<code class="thin"> or </code>github<code class="thin">). Type: string.<br>	* request.mcp.tool: tool name without backend prefix (for example, </code>list_issues<code class="thin">). Type: string.<br>	* request.mcp.params: parameters of the MCP method, including keys like </code>_meta<code class="thin"> and </code>arguments`. Type: object.<br>Note: The CEL expression support is experimental, and the attributes<br>available to the expression may change in future releases.</div></div></div>
<div class="api-field"><div class="api-field__left"><code class="api-field__name">action</code><span class="api-field__badge api-field__badge--optional">optional</span><div class="api-field__type"><div><a href="#github-com-envoyproxy-gateway-api-v1alpha1-authorizationaction">AuthorizationAction</a></div></div></div><div class="api-field__right"><div class="api-field__default">Allow</div><div class="api-field__description">Action is the authorization decision for matching requests. If unspecified, defaults to Allow.</div></div></div>
<h4 class="anchor anchorTargetStickyNavbar_Vzrq" id="mcproutebackendref-1"><a id="github-com-envoyproxy-ai-gateway-api-v1beta1-mcproutebackendref">MCPRouteBackendRef</a><a href="#mcproutebackendref-1" class="hash-link" aria-label="Direct link to mcproutebackendref-1" title="Direct link to mcproutebackendref-1" translate="no">​</a></h4>
<p><strong>Appears in:</strong></p>
\<ul\>
<li class=""><a href="#github-com-envoyproxy-ai-gateway-api-v1beta1-mcproutespec" class="">MCPRouteSpec</a></li>
</ul>
<p>MCPRouteBackendRef wraps a EG&#x27;s BackendObjectReference to reference an MCP server.</p>
<h5 class="anchor anchorTargetStickyNavbar_Vzrq" id="fields-128">Fields<a href="#fields-128" class="hash-link" aria-label="Direct link to Fields" title="Direct link to Fields" translate="no">​</a></h5>
<div class="api-field"><div class="api-field__left"><code class="api-field__name">group</code><span class="api-field__badge api-field__badge--optional">optional</span><div class="api-field__type"><div><a href="#sigs-k8s-io-gateway-api-apis-v1-group">Group</a></div></div></div><div class="api-field__right"><div class="api-field__description">Group is the group of the referent. For example, <code class="thin">gateway.networking.k8s.io</code>.<br>When unspecified or empty string, core API group is inferred.</div></div></div>
<div class="api-field"><div class="api-field__left"><code class="api-field__name">kind</code><span class="api-field__badge api-field__badge--optional">optional</span><div class="api-field__type"><div><a href="#sigs-k8s-io-gateway-api-apis-v1-kind">Kind</a></div></div></div><div class="api-field__right"><div class="api-field__default">Service</div><div class="api-field__description">Kind is the Kubernetes resource kind of the referent. For example<br><code class="thin">Service</code>.<br>Defaults to <code class="thin">Service</code> when not specified.<br>ExternalName services can refer to CNAME DNS records that may live<br>outside of the cluster and as such are difficult to reason about in<br>terms of conformance. They also may not be safe to forward to (see<br>CVE-2021-25740 for more information). Implementations SHOULD NOT<br>support ExternalName Services.<br>Support: Core (Services with a type other than ExternalName)<br>Support: Implementation-specific (Services with type ExternalName)</div></div></div>
<div class="api-field"><div class="api-field__left"><code class="api-field__name">name</code><span class="api-field__badge api-field__badge--required">required</span><div class="api-field__type"><div><a href="#sigs-k8s-io-gateway-api-apis-v1-objectname">ObjectName</a></div></div></div><div class="api-field__right"><div class="api-field__description">Name is the name of the referent.</div></div></div>
<div class="api-field"><div class="api-field__left"><code class="api-field__name">namespace</code><span class="api-field__badge api-field__badge--optional">optional</span><div class="api-field__type"><div><a href="https://gateway-api.sigs.k8s.io/reference/spec/?h=httproutetimeouts#namespace">Namespace</a></div></div></div><div class="api-field__right"><div class="api-field__description">Namespace is the namespace of the backend. When unspecified, the local<br>namespace is inferred.<br>Note that when a namespace different than the local namespace is specified,<br>a ReferenceGrant object is required in the referent namespace to allow that<br>namespace's owner to accept the reference. See the ReferenceGrant<br>documentation for details.<br>Support: Core</div></div></div>
<div class="api-field"><div class="api-field__left"><code class="api-field__name">port</code><span class="api-field__badge api-field__badge--optional">optional</span><div class="api-field__type"><div><a href="#sigs-k8s-io-gateway-api-apis-v1-portnumber">PortNumber</a></div></div></div><div class="api-field__right"><div class="api-field__description">Port specifies the destination port number to use for this resource.<br>Port is required when the referent is a Kubernetes Service. In this<br>case, the port number is the service port number, not the target port.<br>For other resources, destination port might be derived from the referent<br>resource or this field.</div></div></div>
<div class="api-field"><div class="api-field__left"><code class="api-field__name">path</code><span class="api-field__badge api-field__badge--optional">optional</span><div class="api-field__type"><div>string</div></div></div><div class="api-field__right"><div class="api-field__default">/mcp</div><div class="api-field__description">Path is the HTTP endpoint path of the backend MCP server.<br>If not specified, the default is <code class="thin">/mcp</code>.</div></div></div>
<div class="api-field"><div class="api-field__left"><code class="api-field__name">toolSelector</code><span class="api-field__badge api-field__badge--optional">optional</span><div class="api-field__type"><div><a href="#github-com-envoyproxy-ai-gateway-api-v1beta1-mcptoolfilter">MCPToolFilter</a></div></div></div><div class="api-field__right"><div class="api-field__description">ToolSelector filters the tools exposed by this MCP server.<br>Supports exact matches and RE2-compatible regular expressions for both include and exclude patterns.<br>If not specified, all tools from the MCP server are exposed.</div></div></div>
<div class="api-field"><div class="api-field__left"><code class="api-field__name">promptSelector</code><span class="api-field__badge api-field__badge--optional">optional</span><div class="api-field__type"><div><a href="#github-com-envoyproxy-ai-gateway-api-v1beta1-mcppromptfilter">MCPPromptFilter</a></div></div></div><div class="api-field__right"><div class="api-field__description">PromptSelector filters the prompts exposed by this MCP server, analogous to ToolSelector.<br>If not specified, all prompts from the MCP server are exposed.<br>This is also used to determine which prompt names can be safely exposed unprefixed when<br>this backend's effective PrefixMode is Never: only prompts named in Include are validated<br>for cross-backend uniqueness and exposed bare. If unset while PrefixMode is Never, this<br>backend's prompts are not exposed bare (they keep the <code class="thin"><backendname>__</backendname></code> prefix) because<br>their names cannot be proven unique at admission time.</div></div></div>
