<div class="api-field"><div class="api-field__left"><code class="api-field__name">securityPolicy</code><span class="api-field__badge api-field__badge--optional">optional</span><div class="api-field__type"><div><a href="#github-com-envoyproxy-ai-gateway-api-v1beta1-mcpbackendsecuritypolicy">MCPBackendSecurityPolicy</a></div></div></div><div class="api-field__right"><div class="api-field__description">SecurityPolicy is the security policy to apply to this MCP server.</div></div></div>
<div class="api-field"><div class="api-field__left"><code class="api-field__name">forwardHeaders</code><span class="api-field__badge api-field__badge--optional">optional</span><div class="api-field__type"><div><a href="#github-com-envoyproxy-ai-gateway-api-v1beta1-mcpheaderforward">MCPHeaderForward</a> array</div></div></div><div class="api-field__right"><div class="api-field__description">ForwardHeaders specifies HTTP headers to extract from the incoming client request<br>and forward to this backend MCP server.<br>This enables per-user authentication passthrough (e.g., personal access tokens)<br>without requiring OAuth configuration.<br>Each entry specifies a header name to extract and an optional rename for the backend.</div></div></div>
<div class="api-field"><div class="api-field__left"><code class="api-field__name">prefixMode</code><span class="api-field__badge api-field__badge--optional">optional</span><div class="api-field__type"><div><a href="#github-com-envoyproxy-ai-gateway-api-v1beta1-mcprouteprefixmode">MCPRoutePrefixMode</a></div></div></div><div class="api-field__right"><div class="api-field__description">PrefixMode controls how tool and prompt names from this backend are prefixed.<br>Takes precedence over the route-level PrefixMode when set.</div></div></div>
<h4 class="anchor anchorTargetStickyNavbar_Vzrq" id="mcproutebackendtrafficpolicy-1"><a id="github-com-envoyproxy-ai-gateway-api-v1beta1-mcproutebackendtrafficpolicy">MCPRouteBackendTrafficPolicy</a><a href="#mcproutebackendtrafficpolicy-1" class="hash-link" aria-label="Direct link to mcproutebackendtrafficpolicy-1" title="Direct link to mcproutebackendtrafficpolicy-1" translate="no">​</a></h4>
<p><strong>Appears in:</strong></p>
\<ul\>
<li class=""><a href="#github-com-envoyproxy-ai-gateway-api-v1beta1-mcproutespec" class="">MCPRouteSpec</a></li>
</ul>
<p>MCPRouteBackendTrafficPolicy configures how the Envoy Gateway BackendTrafficPolicy
resources auto-generated for a MCPRoute interact with other BackendTrafficPolicy
resources (e.g. an operator-defined rate limiting policy) targeting the same HTTPRoute.</p>
<h5 class="anchor anchorTargetStickyNavbar_Vzrq" id="fields-129">Fields<a href="#fields-129" class="hash-link" aria-label="Direct link to Fields" title="Direct link to Fields" translate="no">​</a></h5>
<div class="api-field"><div class="api-field__left"><code class="api-field__name">mergeType</code><span class="api-field__badge api-field__badge--optional">optional</span><div class="api-field__type"><div><a href="#github-com-envoyproxy-gateway-api-v1alpha1-mergetype">MergeType</a></div></div></div><div class="api-field__right"><div class="api-field__description">MergeType determines how the auto-generated BackendTrafficPolicy (for example, the<br>OAuth protected resource metadata policy) is merged with other BackendTrafficPolicy<br>configurations targeting the same route or a parent Gateway/Listener.<br>When set, the auto-generated BackendTrafficPolicy will merge with the closest parent<br>BackendTrafficPolicy in the route's attachment hierarchy instead of overriding it<br>entirely, per Envoy Gateway's merge semantics.<br>If unset, no merging occurs, and the auto-generated BackendTrafficPolicy fully<br>overrides any other BackendTrafficPolicy targeting the same route.<br>See: https://gateway.envoyproxy.io/docs/tasks/traffic/backend-traffic-policy/</div></div></div>
<h4 class="anchor anchorTargetStickyNavbar_Vzrq" id="mcprouteoauth-1"><a id="github-com-envoyproxy-ai-gateway-api-v1beta1-mcprouteoauth">MCPRouteOAuth</a><a href="#mcprouteoauth-1" class="hash-link" aria-label="Direct link to mcprouteoauth-1" title="Direct link to mcprouteoauth-1" translate="no">​</a></h4>
<p><strong>Appears in:</strong></p>
\<ul\>
<li class=""><a href="#github-com-envoyproxy-ai-gateway-api-v1beta1-mcproutesecuritypolicy" class="">MCPRouteSecurityPolicy</a></li>
</ul>
<p>MCPRouteOAuth defines a MCP spec compatible OAuth authentication configuration for a MCPRoute.
Reference: <a href="https://modelcontextprotocol.io/specification/2025-06-18/basic/authorization" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" class="">https://modelcontextprotocol.io/specification/2025-06-18/basic/authorization</a></p>
<h5 class="anchor anchorTargetStickyNavbar_Vzrq" id="fields-130">Fields<a href="#fields-130" class="hash-link" aria-label="Direct link to Fields" title="Direct link to Fields" translate="no">​</a></h5>
<div class="api-field"><div class="api-field__left"><code class="api-field__name">issuer</code><span class="api-field__badge api-field__badge--required">required</span><div class="api-field__type"><div>string</div></div></div><div class="api-field__right"><div class="api-field__description">Issuer is the authorization server's issuer identity.</div></div></div>
<div class="api-field"><div class="api-field__left"><code class="api-field__name">audiences</code><span class="api-field__badge api-field__badge--optional">optional</span><div class="api-field__type"><div>string array</div></div></div><div class="api-field__right"><div class="api-field__description">Audiences is a list of JWT audiences allowed access.<br>It is recommended to set this field for token audience validation, as it is a security best practice to prevent token misuse.<br>Reference: https://modelcontextprotocol.io/specification/2025-06-18/basic/authorization#token-audience-binding-and-validation</div></div></div>
<div class="api-field"><div class="api-field__left"><code class="api-field__name">jwks</code><span class="api-field__badge api-field__badge--optional">optional</span><div class="api-field__type"><div><a href="#github-com-envoyproxy-ai-gateway-api-v1beta1-jwks">JWKS</a></div></div></div><div class="api-field__right"><div class="api-field__description">JWKS defines how a JSON Web Key Sets (JWKS) can be obtained to verify the access tokens presented by the clients.<br>If not specified, the JWKS URI will be discovered from the OAuth 2.0 Authorization Server Metadata<br>as per RFC 8414 by querying the <code class="thin">/.well-known/oauth-authorization-server</code> endpoint on the Issuer.</div></div></div>
<div class="api-field"><div class="api-field__left"><code class="api-field__name">authorizationServerMetadataUrl</code><span class="api-field__badge api-field__badge--optional">optional</span><div class="api-field__type"><div>string</div></div></div><div class="api-field__right"><div class="api-field__description">AuthorizationServerMetadataURL is the URL the controller fetches the OAuth 2.0 Authorization<br>Server Metadata document from, as defined in RFC 8414. When set, it replaces the well-known<br>URIs derived from Issuer.<br>Set this when the metadata lives somewhere the issuer does not lead to, for example an issuer<br>of <code class="thin">https://example.com/api/idp/authn</code> whose document is served only at<br><code class="thin">https://example.com/api/idp/v4/authn/.well-known/openid-configuration</code>.<br>Issuer is unaffected by this field. It continues to identify the authorization server in the<br>protected resource metadata the gateway publishes, and to derive the well-known URIs when this<br>field is unset. When JWKS is not set, the JWKS URI is discovered from the document fetched here.</div></div></div>
<div class="api-field"><div class="api-field__left"><code class="api-field__name">protectedResourceMetadata</code><span class="api-field__badge api-field__badge--required">required</span><div class="api-field__type"><div><a href="#github-com-envoyproxy-ai-gateway-api-v1beta1-protectedresourcemetadata">ProtectedResourceMetadata</a></div></div></div><div class="api-field__right"><div class="api-field__description">ProtectedResourceMetadata defines the OAuth 2.0 Resource Server Metadata as per RFC 8414.<br>This is used to expose the metadata endpoint for mcp clients to discover the authorization servers,<br>supported scopes, and JWKS URI.</div></div></div>
<div class="api-field"><div class="api-field__left"><code class="api-field__name">claimToHeaders</code><span class="api-field__badge api-field__badge--optional">optional</span><div class="api-field__type"><div>ClaimToHeader array</div></div></div><div class="api-field__right"><div class="api-field__description">ClaimToHeaders specifies JWT claims to extract and forward as HTTP headers to backend MCP servers.<br>This enables backends to access user identity for authorization, auditing, or personalization.<br>Security considerations:<br>- Any client-provided headers matching the configured header names will be stripped to prevent forgery<br>- Only the specified claims are extracted; the full JWT is not forwarded to backends<br>- Consider using a header prefix (e.g., <code class="thin">X-Jwt-Claim-</code>) to avoid conflicts with other headers</div></div></div>
<h4 class="anchor anchorTargetStickyNavbar_Vzrq" id="mcprouteprefixmode"><a id="github-com-envoyproxy-ai-gateway-api-v1beta1-mcprouteprefixmode">MCPRoutePrefixMode</a><a href="#mcprouteprefixmode" class="hash-link" aria-label="Direct link to mcprouteprefixmode" title="Direct link to mcprouteprefixmode" translate="no">​</a></h4>
<p><strong>Underlying type:</strong> string</p>
<p><strong>Appears in:</strong></p>
\<ul\>
<li class=""><a href="#github-com-envoyproxy-ai-gateway-api-v1beta1-mcproutebackendref" class="">MCPRouteBackendRef</a></li>
<li class=""><a href="#github-com-envoyproxy-ai-gateway-api-v1beta1-mcproutespec" class="">MCPRouteSpec</a></li>
</ul>
<p>MCPRoutePrefixMode controls how tool and prompt names are prefixed when exposed to clients.</p>
<p>PrefixMode can be set at the route level (MCPRouteSpec) and overridden per backend
(MCPRouteBackendRef); the per-backend value takes precedence when set. A single route can
therefore mix Always and Never backends. This is intentional: it lets an operator keep the
route-level default (Always is recommended for most routes, since it guarantees a collision-free
catalog without any extra configuration) while opting specific, well-known backends into bare
names, e.g. to match tool names an existing client or interactive MCP App already hardcodes.
Per-backend override is meant as a targeted escape hatch, not a replacement for choosing one
naming policy for the route as a whole.</p>
<h5 class="anchor anchorTargetStickyNavbar_Vzrq" id="possible-values-9">Possible Values<a href="#possible-values-9" class="hash-link" aria-label="Direct link to Possible Values" title="Direct link to Possible Values" translate="no">​</a></h5>
<div class="api-field" data-type="enum"><div class="api-field__left"><code class="api-field__name">Always</code></div><div class="api-field__right"><div class="api-field__description">MCPRoutePrefixModeAlways prefixes tool/prompt names with <code class="thin"><backendname>__</backendname></code>.<br>This is the default and preserves existing behavior.<br></div></div></div>
<div class="api-field" data-type="enum"><div class="api-field__left"><code class="api-field__name">Never</code></div><div class="api-field__right"><div class="api-field__description">MCPRoutePrefixModeNever exposes tool/prompt names unprefixed.<br>Tool names must be unique across all Never-mode backends on the route.<br></div></div></div>
<h4 class="anchor anchorTargetStickyNavbar_Vzrq" id="mcproutesecuritypolicy-1"><a id="github-com-envoyproxy-ai-gateway-api-v1beta1-mcproutesecuritypolicy">MCPRouteSecurityPolicy</a><a href="#mcproutesecuritypolicy-1" class="hash-link" aria-label="Direct link to mcproutesecuritypolicy-1" title="Direct link to mcproutesecuritypolicy-1" translate="no">​</a></h4>
<p><strong>Appears in:</strong></p>
\<ul\>
<li class=""><a href="#github-com-envoyproxy-ai-gateway-api-v1beta1-mcproutespec" class="">MCPRouteSpec</a></li>
</ul>
<p>MCPRouteSecurityPolicy defines the security policy for a MCPRoute.</p>
<h5 class="anchor anchorTargetStickyNavbar_Vzrq" id="fields-131">Fields<a href="#fields-131" class="hash-link" aria-label="Direct link to Fields" title="Direct link to Fields" translate="no">​</a></h5>
<div class="api-field"><div class="api-field__left"><code class="api-field__name">oauth</code><span class="api-field__badge api-field__badge--optional">optional</span><div class="api-field__type"><div><a href="#github-com-envoyproxy-ai-gateway-api-v1beta1-mcprouteoauth">MCPRouteOAuth</a></div></div></div><div class="api-field__right"><div class="api-field__description">OAuth defines the configuration for the MCP spec compatible OAuth authentication.</div></div></div>
<div class="api-field"><div class="api-field__left"><code class="api-field__name">apiKeyAuth</code><span class="api-field__badge api-field__badge--optional">optional</span><div class="api-field__type"><div><a href="https://gateway.envoyproxy.io/docs/api/extension_types/#apikeyauth">APIKeyAuth</a></div></div></div><div class="api-field__right"><div class="api-field__description">APIKeyAuth defines the configuration for the API Key Authentication.</div></div></div>
<div class="api-field"><div class="api-field__left"><code class="api-field__name">extAuth</code><span class="api-field__badge api-field__badge--optional">optional</span><div class="api-field__type"><div><a href="#github-com-envoyproxy-gateway-api-v1alpha1-extauth">ExtAuth</a></div></div></div><div class="api-field__right"><div class="api-field__description">ExtAuth defines the configuration for External Authorization.</div></div></div>
<div class="api-field"><div class="api-field__left"><code class="api-field__name">authorization</code><span class="api-field__badge api-field__badge--optional">optional</span><div class="api-field__type"><div><a href="#github-com-envoyproxy-ai-gateway-api-v1beta1-mcprouteauthorization">MCPRouteAuthorization</a></div></div></div><div class="api-field__right"><div class="api-field__description">Authorization defines the configuration for the MCP spec compatible authorization.</div></div></div>
<div class="api-field"><div class="api-field__left"><code class="api-field__name">mergeType</code><span class="api-field__badge api-field__badge--optional">optional</span><div class="api-field__type"><div><a href="#github-com-envoyproxy-gateway-api-v1alpha1-mergetype">MergeType</a></div></div></div><div class="api-field__right"><div class="api-field__description">MergeType determines how the auto-generated SecurityPolicy for this MCPRoute (which<br>enforces OAuth/JWT, API key, or external authorization) is merged with other<br>SecurityPolicy configurations targeting the same route or a parent Gateway/Listener.<br>When set, the auto-generated SecurityPolicy will merge with the closest parent<br>SecurityPolicy in the route's attachment hierarchy (for example, one targeting a<br>Gateway, Gateway listener, ListenerSet, or ListenerSet listener) instead of<br>overriding it entirely, per Envoy Gateway's merge semantics.<br>If unset, no merging occurs, and the auto-generated SecurityPolicy fully overrides<br>any other SecurityPolicy targeting the same route.<br>See: https://gateway.envoyproxy.io/docs/tasks/security/apikey-authn/</div></div></div>
<h4 class="anchor anchorTargetStickyNavbar_Vzrq" id="mcproutespec-1"><a id="github-com-envoyproxy-ai-gateway-api-v1beta1-mcproutespec">MCPRouteSpec</a><a href="#mcproutespec-1" class="hash-link" aria-label="Direct link to mcproutespec-1" title="Direct link to mcproutespec-1" translate="no">​</a></h4>
<p><strong>Appears in:</strong></p>
\<ul\>
<li class=""><a href="#github-com-envoyproxy-ai-gateway-api-v1beta1-mcproute" class="">MCPRoute</a></li>
</ul>
<p>MCPRouteSpec details the MCPRoute configuration.</p>
<h5 class="anchor anchorTargetStickyNavbar_Vzrq" id="fields-132">Fields<a href="#fields-132" class="hash-link" aria-label="Direct link to Fields" title="Direct link to Fields" translate="no">​</a></h5>
<div class="api-field"><div class="api-field__left"><code class="api-field__name">parentRefs</code><span class="api-field__badge api-field__badge--required">required</span><div class="api-field__type"><div><a href="https://gateway-api.sigs.k8s.io/reference/spec/?h=httproutetimeouts#parentreference">ParentReference</a> array</div></div></div><div class="api-field__right"><div class="api-field__description">ParentRefs are the names of the Gateway resources this MCPRoute is being attached to.<br>Cross namespace references are not supported. In other words, the Gateway resources must be in the<br>same namespace as the MCPRoute. Currently, each reference's Kind must be Gateway.</div></div></div>
<div class="api-field"><div class="api-field__left"><code class="api-field__name">path</code><span class="api-field__badge api-field__badge--optional">optional</span><div class="api-field__type"><div>string</div></div></div><div class="api-field__right"><div class="api-field__default">/mcp</div><div class="api-field__description">Path is the HTTP endpoint path that serves MCP requests over the Streamable HTTP transport.<br>If not specified, the default is <code class="thin">/mcp</code>.</div></div></div>
<div class="api-field"><div class="api-field__left"><code class="api-field__name">headers</code><span class="api-field__badge api-field__badge--optional">optional</span><div class="api-field__type"><div><a href="https://gateway-api.sigs.k8s.io/reference/spec/?h=httproutetimeouts#httpheadermatch">HTTPHeaderMatch</a> array</div></div></div><div class="api-field__right"><div class="api-field__description">Headers are HTTP headers that must match for this route to be selected.<br>Multiple match values are ANDed together, meaning, a request must match all the specified headers to select the route.</div></div></div>
<div class="api-field"><div class="api-field__left"><code class="api-field__name">hostnames</code><span class="api-field__badge api-field__badge--optional">optional</span><div class="api-field__type"><div>Hostname array</div></div></div><div class="api-field__right"><div class="api-field__description">Hostnames is a list of hostnames matched against the HTTP Host header to select this MCPRoute.<br>This is equivalent to the Hostnames field in the Gateway API HTTPRouteSpec. When specified, the<br>generated HTTPRoute includes these hostnames, so the MCP route lands in the per-host virtual host<br>instead of the wildcard vhost (the same scoping AIGatewayRoute.Hostnames provides).<br>See https://gateway-api.sigs.k8s.io/reference/api-types/httproute/#hostnames<br>for the details of the Hostnames field in the Gateway API.</div></div></div>
<div class="api-field"><div class="api-field__left"><code class="api-field__name">backendRefs</code><span class="api-field__badge api-field__badge--required">required</span><div class="api-field__type"><div><a href="#github-com-envoyproxy-ai-gateway-api-v1beta1-mcproutebackendref">MCPRouteBackendRef</a> array</div></div></div><div class="api-field__right"><div class="api-field__description">BackendRefs is a list of backend references to the MCP servers.<br>These MCP servers will be aggregated and exposed as a single MCP endpoint to the clients.<br>From the client's perspective, they only need to configure a single MCP server URL, e.g. <code class="thin">https://api.example.com/mcp</code>,<br>and the Envoy AI Gateway will route the requests to the appropriate MCP server based on the requests.<br>All names must be unique within this list to avoid potential tools, resources, etc. name collisions.<br>Also, cross-namespace references are not supported. In other words, the backend MCP servers must be in the<br>same namespace as the MCPRoute.</div></div></div>
<div class="api-field"><div class="api-field__left"><code class="api-field__name">securityPolicy</code><span class="api-field__badge api-field__badge--optional">optional</span><div class="api-field__type"><div><a href="#github-com-envoyproxy-ai-gateway-api-v1beta1-mcproutesecuritypolicy">MCPRouteSecurityPolicy</a></div></div></div><div class="api-field__right"><div class="api-field__description">SecurityPolicy defines the security policy for this MCPRoute.</div></div></div>
<div class="api-field"><div class="api-field__left"><code class="api-field__name">backendTrafficPolicy</code><span class="api-field__badge api-field__badge--optional">optional</span><div class="api-field__type"><div><a href="#github-com-envoyproxy-ai-gateway-api-v1beta1-mcproutebackendtrafficpolicy">MCPRouteBackendTrafficPolicy</a></div></div></div><div class="api-field__right"><div class="api-field__description">BackendTrafficPolicy configures the auto-generated Envoy Gateway BackendTrafficPolicy<br>resources for this MCPRoute, for example the OAuth protected resource metadata policy<br>created when SecurityPolicy.OAuth is configured.</div></div></div>
<div class="api-field"><div class="api-field__left"><code class="api-field__name">backendSelector</code><span class="api-field__badge api-field__badge--optional">optional</span><div class="api-field__type"><div><a href="#github-com-envoyproxy-ai-gateway-api-v1beta1-mcpbackendselector">MCPBackendSelector</a></div></div></div><div class="api-field__right"><div class="api-field__description">BackendSelector restricts which of this route's backends a given request may fan<br>out to, evaluated once per candidate backend when a client session is initialized.<br>If unspecified, all backends on the route are considered.<br>Security note: a backendSelector rule whose CEL expression reads request.auth.jwt<br>(claims or scopes) only reflects verified JWT data when SecurityPolicy.OAuth is<br>configured for this route; the XValidation below enforces that pairing so that<br>unauthenticated or forged bearer tokens can't influence backend selection.</div></div></div>
<div class="api-field"><div class="api-field__left"><code class="api-field__name">prefixMode</code><span class="api-field__badge api-field__badge--optional">optional</span><div class="api-field__type"><div><a href="#github-com-envoyproxy-ai-gateway-api-v1beta1-mcprouteprefixmode">MCPRoutePrefixMode</a></div></div></div><div class="api-field__right"><div class="api-field__description">PrefixMode controls how tool and prompt names from all backends on this route are prefixed.</div></div></div>
<h4 class="anchor anchorTargetStickyNavbar_Vzrq" id="mcproutestatus-1"><a id="github-com-envoyproxy-ai-gateway-api-v1beta1-mcproutestatus">MCPRouteStatus</a><a href="#mcproutestatus-1" class="hash-link" aria-label="Direct link to mcproutestatus-1" title="Direct link to mcproutestatus-1" translate="no">​</a></h4>
<p><strong>Appears in:</strong></p>
\<ul\>
<li class=""><a href="#github-com-envoyproxy-ai-gateway-api-v1beta1-mcproute" class="">MCPRoute</a></li>
</ul>
<p>MCPRouteStatus contains the conditions by the reconciliation result.</p>
<h5 class="anchor anchorTargetStickyNavbar_Vzrq" id="fields-133">Fields<a href="#fields-133" class="hash-link" aria-label="Direct link to Fields" title="Direct link to Fields" translate="no">​</a></h5>
<div class="api-field"><div class="api-field__left"><code class="api-field__name">conditions</code><span class="api-field__badge api-field__badge--required">required</span><div class="api-field__type"><div><a href="https://kubernetes.io/docs/reference/generated/kubernetes-api/v1.29/#condition-v1-meta">Condition</a> array</div></div></div><div class="api-field__right"><div class="api-field__description">Conditions is the list of conditions by the reconciliation result.<br>Currently, at most one condition is set.<br>Known .status.conditions.type are: <code class="thin">Accepted</code>, <code class="thin">NotAccepted</code>.</div></div></div>
<h4 class="anchor anchorTargetStickyNavbar_Vzrq" id="mcptoolfilter-1"><a id="github-com-envoyproxy-ai-gateway-api-v1beta1-mcptoolfilter">MCPToolFilter</a><a href="#mcptoolfilter-1" class="hash-link" aria-label="Direct link to mcptoolfilter-1" title="Direct link to mcptoolfilter-1" translate="no">​</a></h4>
<p><strong>Appears in:</strong></p>
\<ul\>
<li class=""><a href="#github-com-envoyproxy-ai-gateway-api-v1beta1-mcproutebackendref" class="">MCPRouteBackendRef</a></li>
</ul>
<p>MCPToolFilter filters tools using include and exclude patterns with exact matches or regular expressions.
Exclude rules take precedence over include rules (deny-wins). When both include and exclude are specified,
a tool must match an include rule AND not match any exclude rule to be allowed.</p>
<h5 class="anchor anchorTargetStickyNavbar_Vzrq" id="fields-134">Fields<a href="#fields-134" class="hash-link" aria-label="Direct link to Fields" title="Direct link to Fields" translate="no">​</a></h5>
<div class="api-field"><div class="api-field__left"><code class="api-field__name">include</code><span class="api-field__badge api-field__badge--optional">optional</span><div class="api-field__type"><div>string array</div></div></div><div class="api-field__right"><div class="api-field__description">Include is a list of tool names to include. Only the specified tools will be available.</div></div></div>
<div class="api-field"><div class="api-field__left"><code class="api-field__name">includeRegex</code><span class="api-field__badge api-field__badge--optional">optional</span><div class="api-field__type"><div>string array</div></div></div><div class="api-field__right"><div class="api-field__description">IncludeRegex is a list of RE2-compatible regular expressions that, when matched, include the tool.<br>Only tools matching these patterns will be available.</div></div></div>
<div class="api-field"><div class="api-field__left"><code class="api-field__name">exclude</code><span class="api-field__badge api-field__badge--optional">optional</span><div class="api-field__type"><div>string array</div></div></div><div class="api-field__right"><div class="api-field__description">Exclude is a list of tool names to exclude. The specified tools will not be available.<br>Exclude rules take precedence over include rules.</div></div></div>
<div class="api-field"><div class="api-field__left"><code class="api-field__name">excludeRegex</code><span class="api-field__badge api-field__badge--optional">optional</span><div class="api-field__type"><div>string array</div></div></div><div class="api-field__right"><div class="api-field__description">ExcludeRegex is a list of RE2-compatible regular expressions that, when matched, exclude the tool.<br>Tools matching these patterns will not be available. Exclude rules take precedence over include rules.</div></div></div>
<h4 class="anchor anchorTargetStickyNavbar_Vzrq" id="protectedresourcemetadata-1"><a id="github-com-envoyproxy-ai-gateway-api-v1beta1-protectedresourcemetadata">ProtectedResourceMetadata</a><a href="#protectedresourcemetadata-1" class="hash-link" aria-label="Direct link to protectedresourcemetadata-1" title="Direct link to protectedresourcemetadata-1" translate="no">​</a></h4>
<p><strong>Appears in:</strong></p>
\<ul\>
<li class=""><a href="#github-com-envoyproxy-ai-gateway-api-v1beta1-mcprouteoauth" class="">MCPRouteOAuth</a></li>
</ul>
<p>ProtectedResourceMetadata represents the Protected Resource Metadata	of the MCP server as per RFC 9728.</p>
<p>References:</p>
\<ul\>
<li class=""><a href="https://modelcontextprotocol.io/specification/2025-06-18/basic/authorization#authorization-server-location" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" class="">https://modelcontextprotocol.io/specification/2025-06-18/basic/authorization#authorization-server-location</a></li>
<li class=""><a href="https://datatracker.ietf.org/doc/html/rfc9728#name-protected-resource-metadata" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" class="">https://datatracker.ietf.org/doc/html/rfc9728#name-protected-resource-metadata</a></li>
</ul>
<h5 class="anchor anchorTargetStickyNavbar_Vzrq" id="fields-135">Fields<a href="#fields-135" class="hash-link" aria-label="Direct link to Fields" title="Direct link to Fields" translate="no">​</a></h5>
<div class="api-field"><div class="api-field__left"><code class="api-field__name">resource</code><span class="api-field__badge api-field__badge--required">required</span><div class="api-field__type"><div>string</div></div></div><div class="api-field__right"><div class="api-field__description">Resource is the identifier of the protected resource.<br>This should match the MCPRoute's URL. For example, if the MCPRoute's URL is<br><code class="thin">https://api.example.com/mcp</code>, the Resource should be <code class="thin">https://api.example.com/mcp</code>.</div></div></div>
<div class="api-field"><div class="api-field__left"><code class="api-field__name">resourceName</code><span class="api-field__badge api-field__badge--optional">optional</span><div class="api-field__type"><div>string</div></div></div><div class="api-field__right"><div class="api-field__description">ResourceName is a human-readable name for the protected resource.</div></div></div>
<div class="api-field"><div class="api-field__left"><code class="api-field__name">scopesSupported</code><span class="api-field__badge api-field__badge--optional">optional</span><div class="api-field__type"><div>string array</div></div></div><div class="api-field__right"><div class="api-field__description">ScopesSupported defines the minimal set of scopes required for the basic functionality of the MCPRoute.<br>It should avoid broad or overly permissive scopes to prevent clients from requesting tokens with excessive privileges.<br>If an operation requires additional scopes that are not present in the access token, the client will receive a<br>403 Forbidden response that includes the required scopes in the <code class="thin">scope</code> field of the <code class="thin">WWW-Authenticate</code> header.<br>This enables incremental privilege elevation through targeted <code class="thin">WWW-Authenticate: scope=</code>...`` challenges when<br>privileged operations are first attempted.</div></div></div>
<div class="api-field"><div class="api-field__left"><code class="api-field__name">resourceSigningAlgValuesSupported</code><span class="api-field__badge api-field__badge--optional">optional</span><div class="api-field__type"><div>string array</div></div></div><div class="api-field__right"><div class="api-field__description">ResourceSigningAlgValuesSupported is a list of JWS signing algorithms supported by the resource server.<br>These algorithms are used in the <code class="thin">alg</code> field of the JOSE header in signed tokens.</div></div></div>
<div class="api-field"><div class="api-field__left"><code class="api-field__name">resourceDocumentation</code><span class="api-field__badge api-field__badge--optional">optional</span><div class="api-field__type"><div>string</div></div></div><div class="api-field__right"><div class="api-field__description">ResourceDocumentation is a URL that provides human-readable documentation for the resource.</div></div></div>
<div class="api-field"><div class="api-field__left"><code class="api-field__name">resourcePolicyUri</code><span class="api-field__badge api-field__badge--optional">optional</span><div class="api-field__type"><div>string</div></div></div><div class="api-field__right"><div class="api-field__description">ResourcePolicyURI is a URL that points to the resource server's policy document.</div></div></div>
<h4 class="anchor anchorTargetStickyNavbar_Vzrq" id="toolcall-1"><a id="github-com-envoyproxy-ai-gateway-api-v1beta1-toolcall">ToolCall</a><a href="#toolcall-1" class="hash-link" aria-label="Direct link to toolcall-1" title="Direct link to toolcall-1" translate="no">​</a></h4>
<p><strong>Appears in:</strong></p>
\<ul\>
<li class=""><a href="#github-com-envoyproxy-ai-gateway-api-v1beta1-mcpauthorizationtarget" class="">MCPAuthorizationTarget</a></li>
</ul>
<p>ToolCall represents a tool call in the MCP authorization target.</p>
<h5 class="anchor anchorTargetStickyNavbar_Vzrq" id="fields-136">Fields<a href="#fields-136" class="hash-link" aria-label="Direct link to Fields" title="Direct link to Fields" translate="no">​</a></h5>
<div class="api-field"><div class="api-field__left"><code class="api-field__name">backend</code><span class="api-field__badge api-field__badge--required">required</span><div class="api-field__type"><div>string</div></div></div><div class="api-field__right"><div class="api-field__description">Backend is the name of the backend this tool belongs to.</div></div></div>
<div class="api-field"><div class="api-field__left"><code class="api-field__name">tool</code><span class="api-field__badge api-field__badge--required">required</span><div class="api-field__type"><div>string</div></div></div><div class="api-field__right"><div class="api-field__description">Tool is the name of the tool.</div></div></div>
<h4 class="anchor anchorTargetStickyNavbar_Vzrq" id="versionedapischema-1"><a id="github-com-envoyproxy-ai-gateway-api-v1beta1-versionedapischema">VersionedAPISchema</a><a href="#versionedapischema-1" class="hash-link" aria-label="Direct link to versionedapischema-1" title="Direct link to versionedapischema-1" translate="no">​</a></h4>
<p><strong>Appears in:</strong></p>
\<ul\>
<li class=""><a href="#github-com-envoyproxy-ai-gateway-api-v1beta1-aiservicebackendspec" class="">AIServiceBackendSpec</a></li>
</ul>
<p>VersionedAPISchema defines the API schema of either AIGatewayRoute (the input) or AIServiceBackend (the output).</p>
<p>This allows the ai-gateway to understand the input and perform the necessary transformation
depending on the API schema pair (input, output).</p>
<p>Note that this is vendor specific, and the stability of the API schema is not guaranteed by
the ai-gateway, but by the vendor via proper versioning.</p>
<h5 class="anchor anchorTargetStickyNavbar_Vzrq" id="fields-137">Fields<a href="#fields-137" class="hash-link" aria-label="Direct link to Fields" title="Direct link to Fields" translate="no">​</a></h5>
<div class="api-field"><div class="api-field__left"><code class="api-field__name">name</code><span class="api-field__badge api-field__badge--required">required</span><div class="api-field__type"><div><a href="#github-com-envoyproxy-ai-gateway-api-v1beta1-apischema">APISchema</a></div></div></div><div class="api-field__right"><div class="api-field__description">Name is the name of the API schema of the AIGatewayRoute or AIServiceBackend.</div></div></div>
<div class="api-field"><div class="api-field__left"><code class="api-field__name">version</code><span class="api-field__badge api-field__badge--optional">optional</span><div class="api-field__type"><div>string</div></div></div><div class="api-field__right"><div class="api-field__description">Version is the version of the API schema.<br>When the name is set to AzureOpenAI, this version maps to <code class="thin">API Version</code> in the<br>Azure OpenAI API documentation (https://learn.microsoft.com/en-us/azure/ai-services/openai/reference#rest-api-versioning).<br>This field is ignored for OpenAI, AWSBedrock, GCPVertexAI, and Anthropic.<br>For OpenAI and Anthropic, use prefix to configure custom request paths.<br>See https://aigateway.envoyproxy.io/docs/capabilities/llm-integrations/supported-providers for details.</div></div></div>
<div class="api-field"><div class="api-field__left"><code class="api-field__name">prefix</code><span class="api-field__badge api-field__badge--optional">optional</span><div class="api-field__type"><div>string</div></div></div><div class="api-field__right"><div class="api-field__description">Prefix is the prefix for the API.<br>When the name is set to <code class="thin">OpenAI</code>, <code class="thin">chat completions</code> API endpoint will be <code class="thin">$\{this_field\}/chat/completions</code>.<br>When the name is set to <code class="thin">Anthropic</code>, the <code class="thin">messages</code> API endpoint will be <code class="thin">$\{this_field\}/messages</code>.<br>It can be with or without a leading slash (<code class="thin">/</code>).<br>This field is ignored for AWSAnthropic and GCPAnthropic.<br>This is especially useful when routing to a backend that has an OpenAI or Anthropic compatible API but has a different<br>prefix. For example, Gemini OpenAI compatible API (https://ai.google.dev/gemini-api/docs/openai) uses<br><code class="thin">/v1beta/openai</code> prefix. Another example is that Cohere AI (https://docs.cohere.com/v2/docs/compatibility-api)<br>uses <code class="thin">/compatibility/v1</code> prefix. On the other hand, DeepSeek (https://api-docs.deepseek.com/) doesn't<br>use prefix, so you can leave this field unset.<br>See https://aigateway.envoyproxy.io/docs/capabilities/llm-integrations/supported-providers for details.</div></div></div></div></article><nav class="docusaurus-mt-lg pagination-nav" aria-label="Docs pages"><a class="pagination-nav__link pagination-nav__link--prev" href="/docs/terminology"><div class="pagination-nav__sublabel">Previous</div><div
